+        return {}
+
+
+def _with_instance(payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    """A copy of ``payload`` carrying DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID when set (never overrides the caller's)."""
+    instance_id = os.getenv("DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID")
+    if instance_id and "instance_id" not in payload:

                 detail = raw_text or f"ConPort request failed with status {response.status}"

diff --git a/services/working-memory-assistant/auth_identity.py b/services/working-memory-assistant/auth_identity.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..1a6ea562f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/services/working-memory-assistant/auth_identity.py
@@ -0,0 +1,388 @@
+"""Request identity for dope-memory HTTP writes (Wave 2A R2, W01).
+
+What this module does
+- ``AuthGate`` verifies a bearer token on a write: SHA-256 of the token string,
+  compared with ``hmac.compare_digest`` against a hash file, yielding a
+  principal such as ``runner:codex`` or the literal ``unverified``.
+- Three modes from one env setting, ``DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE`` (default ``log``):
+  ``off``      never verify; every write is recorded ``unverified``;
+  ``log``      verify; accept everything; record the verified principal or
+               ``unverified`` and count unverified calls per caller;
+  ``enforce``  as ``log`` but reject an unverified write with 401.
+- ``validate_write_ids`` fills an omitted ``workspace_id`` from the container's
+  binding (``DOPE_MEMORY_WORKSPACE_ID``) and an omitted ``instance_id`` from the
+  server's default instance, rejects a supplied ``workspace_id`` that differs
+  from the binding, and rejects every write when there is no binding
+  (Amendment 04).
+- ``WriteAuthMiddleware`` answers a real HTTP 401 for an unverified MCP
+  ``tools/call`` of a mutating tool in ``enforce`` mode.
+
+Hash file (written by the ``dopemux auth`` helper, read-only here)
+- ``$DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_DIR/memory-token-hashes.json``: a JSON object mapping the
+  lowercase hex SHA-256 of a token to its principal, for example
+  ``{"<64 hex>": "runner:codex"}``. Principals match
+  ``(runner|internal):<name>``. Malformed entries are skipped; an unreadable or
+  non-object file verifies nothing (fail closed). The file is re-read when its
+  mtime or size changes, so rotation needs no restart.
+
+Never logged: the Authorization header, a token, or a hash. Only principal
+names and caller labels appear in logs and in the health counters.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import hashlib
+import hmac
+import json
+import logging
+import os
+import re
+import threading
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Awaitable, Callable, Mapping, Optional
+
+logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+
+AUTH_MODES = ("off", "log", "enforce")
+DEFAULT_AUTH_MODE = "log"
+ENV_AUTH_MODE = "DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE"
+ENV_AUTH_DIR = "DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_DIR"
+HASH_FILE_NAME = "memory-token-hashes.json"
+UNVERIFIED_PRINCIPAL = "unverified"
+
+# Every mutating tool. memory_mark_issue writes nothing today but is mutating
+# by name (W00 Q6), so it is gated like the rest.
+WRITE_TOOLS = frozenset(
+    {
+        "memory_store",
+        "memory_correct",
+        "memory_link_resolution",
+        "memory_generate_reflection",
+        "memory_mark_issue",
+    }
+)
+
+MAX_TRACKED_CALLERS = 100
+MAX_TOKEN_CHARS = 512
+MAX_CALLER_LABEL_CHARS = 64
+MAX_BODY_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024
+
+_PRINCIPAL_RE = re.compile(r"^(runner|internal):[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]{0,63}$")
+_SHA256_HEX_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
+_NON_PRINTABLE_RE = re.compile(r"[^\x20-\x7e]")
+
+
+class Unauthorized(Exception):
+    """An unverified write was refused in ``enforce`` mode."""
+
+
+class WriteIdentityError(ValueError):
+    """A write names a workspace that is not this server's, or the server has none."""
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class Identity:
+    principal: str
+    verified: bool
+
+
+UNVERIFIED = Identity(principal=UNVERIFIED_PRINCIPAL, verified=False)
+
+
+def load_auth_mode(env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None) -> str:
+    """Return the auth mode from the environment; unknown values are an error."""
+    source = os.environ if env is None else env
+    raw = (source.get(ENV_AUTH_MODE) or "").strip().lower()
+    if not raw:
+        return DEFAULT_AUTH_MODE
+    if raw not in AUTH_MODES:
+        raise ValueError(
+            f"{ENV_AUTH_MODE}={raw!r} is not one of {', '.join(AUTH_MODES)}"
+        )
+    return raw
+
+
+def caller_label(user_agent: Optional[str], client_host: Optional[str]) -> str:
+    """A short, log-safe label for an unverified caller (never a credential)."""
+    label = _NON_PRINTABLE_RE.sub("", user_agent or "").strip()[:MAX_CALLER_LABEL_CHARS]
+    return label or (client_host or "").strip()[:MAX_CALLER_LABEL_CHARS] or "unknown"
+
+
+def validate_write_ids(
+    workspace_id: Optional[str],
+    instance_id: Optional[str],
+    bound_workspace_id: Optional[str],
+    default_instance_id: str = "A",
+) -> tuple[str, str]:
+    """Resolve a write's ids against this server's binding (Amendment 04).
+
+    Returns ``(workspace_id, instance_id)``. An omitted, empty or blank
+    ``workspace_id`` becomes ``bound_workspace_id``; an omitted, empty or blank
+    ``instance_id`` becomes ``default_instance_id``. A supplied value is never
+    replaced. Raises ``WriteIdentityError`` when the server has no binding
+    (every write is rejected) or when a supplied ``workspace_id`` differs from it.
+    """
+    if not bound_workspace_id:
+        raise WriteIdentityError(
+            "this dope-memory server has no workspace binding "
+            "(DOPE_MEMORY_WORKSPACE_ID is unset); writes are rejected"
+        )
+    if workspace_id is None or not str(workspace_id).strip():
+        workspace_id = bound_workspace_id
+    elif workspace_id != bound_workspace_id:
+        raise WriteIdentityError(
+            f"workspace_id {str(workspace_id)[:128]!r} does not match this "
+            f"server's bound workspace {bound_workspace_id!r}; write rejected"
+        )
+    if instance_id is None or not str(instance_id).strip():
+        instance_id = default_instance_id
+    return workspace_id, instance_id
+
+
+def _extract_bearer(authorization: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:
+    if not authorization:
+        return None
+    parts = authorization.strip().split(None, 1)
+    if len(parts) != 2 or parts[0].lower() != "bearer":
+        return None
+    token = parts[1].strip()
+    if not token or len(token) > MAX_TOKEN_CHARS:
+        return None
+    return token
+
+
+class AuthGate:
+    """Verifies bearer tokens and counts unverified callers."""
+
+    def __init__(self, mode: str, auth_dir: Optional[Path | str] = None):
+        if mode not in AUTH_MODES:
+            raise ValueError(f"invalid auth mode {mode!r}; use one of {', '.join(AUTH_MODES)}")
+        self.mode = mode
+        self._hash_path = Path(auth_dir) / HASH_FILE_NAME if auth_dir else None
+        self._lock = threading.Lock()
+        self._stamp: Optional[tuple[int, int]] = None
+        self._hashes: dict[str, str] = {}
+        self._status = "unconfigured" if self._hash_path is None else "missing"
+        self._unverified: dict[str, int] = {}
+
+    @classmethod
+    def from_env(cls, env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None) -> "AuthGate":
+        source = os.environ if env is None else env
+        return cls(load_auth_mode(source), (source.get(ENV_AUTH_DIR) or "").strip() or None)
+
+    # -- hash file ---------------------------------------------------------
+
+    def _refresh(self) -> tuple[dict[str, str], str]:
+        if self._hash_path is None:
+            return {}, "unconfigured"
+        with self._lock:
+            try:
+                stat = self._hash_path.stat()
+            except OSError:
+                self._stamp, self._hashes, self._status = None, {}, "missing"
+                return self._hashes, self._status
+            stamp = (stat.st_mtime_ns, stat.st_size)
+            if stamp != self._stamp:
+                self._stamp = stamp
+                self._hashes, self._status = self._read_hash_file()
+            return self._hashes, self._status
+
+    def _read_hash_file(self) -> tuple[dict[str, str], str]:
+        assert self._hash_path is not None
+        try:
+            data = json.loads(self._hash_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+        except (OSError, ValueError):
+            logger.error("dope-memory auth hash file is unreadable or not JSON")
+            return {}, "invalid"
+        if not isinstance(data, dict):
+            logger.error("dope-memory auth hash file is not a JSON object")
+            return {}, "invalid"
+        hashes: dict[str, str] = {}
+        skipped = 0
+        for digest, principal in data.items():
+            if (
+                isinstance(digest, str)
+                and isinstance(principal, str)
+                and _SHA256_HEX_RE.match(digest)
+                and _PRINCIPAL_RE.match(principal)
+            ):
+                hashes[digest] = principal
+            else:
+                skipped += 1
+        if skipped:
+            logger.warning("dope-memory auth hash file: skipped %d malformed entries", skipped)
+        return hashes, "ok"
+
+    # -- verification ----------------------------------------------------------
+
+    def authenticate(self, authorization: Optional[str]) -> Identity:
+        """Return the verified principal for a header value, else ``unverified``."""
+        token = _extract_bearer(authorization)
+        if token is None:
+            return UNVERIFIED
+        hashes, _ = self._refresh()
+        digest = hashlib.sha256(token.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
+        matched: Optional[str] = None
+        for stored, principal in hashes.items():
+            # No early exit: every stored hash is compared.
+            if hmac.compare_digest(digest, stored):
+                matched = principal
+        return Identity(matched, True) if matched else UNVERIFIED
+
+    def record_unverified(self, caller: str) -> None:
+        with self._lock:
+            if caller in self._unverified:
+                self._unverified[caller] += 1
+                return
+            if len(self._unverified) < MAX_TRACKED_CALLERS:
+                self._unverified[caller] = 1
+                logger.warning("unverified dope-memory write from caller=%s", caller)
+            else:
+                self._unverified["other"] = self._unverified.get("other", 0) + 1
+
+    def authorize_write(self, authorization: Optional[str], caller: str) -> Identity:
+        """Apply the mode to a write; return the identity to store with it."""
+        if self.mode == "off":
+            return UNVERIFIED
+        identity = self.authenticate(authorization)
+        if identity.verified:
+            return identity
+        self.record_unverified(caller)
+        if self.mode == "enforce":
+            raise Unauthorized(
+                "unauthorized (401): a valid bearer token is required for writes "
+                "while the auth mode is 'enforce'"
+            )
+        return identity
+
+    def health_fields(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Fields the health route reports (read by the generator gate and doctor)."""
+        _, status = self._refresh()
+        with self._lock:
+            counts = dict(self._unverified)
+        # identity_required_writes stays True: it means writes are bound to this
+        # server's workspace (a mismatched workspace is rejected), not that the
+        # caller must send the ids (omitted ids are filled from the binding).
+        return {
+            "auth_mode": self.mode,
+            "identity_required_writes": True,
+            "auth_hash_file": status,
+            "auth_unverified_calls": counts,
+        }
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- ASGI
+
+_Scope = dict
+_Message = dict
+_Receive = Callable[[], Awaitable[_Message]]
+_Send = Callable[[_Message], Awaitable[None]]
+
+
+def _scope_header(scope: _Scope, name: bytes) -> Optional[str]:
+    for key, value in scope.get("headers") or []:
+        if key.lower() == name:
+            return value.decode("latin-1")
+    return None
+
+
+def _count_write_calls(payload: Any) -> int:
+    items = payload if isinstance(payload, list) else [payload]
+    count = 0
+    for item in items:
+        if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("method") == "tools/call":
+            params = item.get("params")
+            if isinstance(params, dict) and params.get("name") in WRITE_TOOLS:
+                count += 1
+    return count
+
+
+async def _respond(send: _Send, status: int, payload: dict, extra: Optional[list] = None) -> None:
+    body = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
+    headers = [
+        (b"content-type", b"application/json"),
+        (b"content-length", str(len(body)).encode("ascii")),
+    ]
+    headers.extend(extra or [])
+    await send({"type": "http.response.start", "status": status, "headers": headers})
+    await send({"type": "http.response.body", "body": body})
+
+
+class WriteAuthMiddleware:
+    """HTTP 401 for an unverified mutating MCP ``tools/call`` in ``enforce`` mode.
+
+    Only ``POST /mcp`` is inspected. The body is buffered (up to
+    ``max_body_bytes``), parsed as JSON-RPC (single or batch), and replayed
+    unchanged to the wrapped app. Unparseable bodies and non-write traffic pass
+    through untouched; the tool itself re-checks authorization, so this layer
+    only adds the early HTTP status.
+    """
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        app: Any,
+        get_gate: Callable[[], AuthGate],
+        max_body_bytes: int = MAX_BODY_BYTES,
+    ):
+        self.app = app
+        self.get_gate = get_gate
+        self.max_body_bytes = max_body_bytes
+
+    async def __call__(self, scope: _Scope, receive: _Receive, send: _Send) -> None:
+        if (
+            scope.get("type") != "http"
+            or scope.get("method") != "POST"
+            or scope.get("path") != "/mcp"
+        ):
+            await self.app(scope, receive, send)
+            return
+
+        messages: list[_Message] = []
+        body = bytearray()
+        while True:
+            message = await receive()
+            messages.append(message)
+            if message.get("type") != "http.request":
+                break
+            body.extend(message.get("body", b""))
+            if len(body) > self.max_body_bytes:
+                await _respond(send, 413, {"error": "request body too large"})
+                return
+            if not message.get("more_body", False):
+                break
+
+        pending = list(messages)
+
+        async def replay() -> _Message:
+            if pending:
+                return pending.pop(0)
+            return await receive()
+
+        gate = self.get_gate()
+        if gate.mode == "enforce":
+            try:
+                writes = _count_write_calls(json.loads(bytes(body)))
+            except (ValueError, RecursionError):
+                writes = 0
+            if writes:
+                identity = gate.authenticate(_scope_header(scope, b"authorization"))
+                if not identity.verified:
+                    client = scope.get("client")
+                    caller = caller_label(
+                        _scope_header(scope, b"user-agent"), client[0] if client else None
+                    )
+                    for _ in range(writes):
+                        gate.record_unverified(caller)
+                    await _respond(
+                        send,
+                        401,
+                        {
+                            "error": "unauthorized",
+                            "detail": "a valid bearer token is required for writes",
+                        },
+                        [(b"www-authenticate", b"Bearer")],
+                    )
+                    return
+
+        await self.app(scope, replay, send)

+-- Wave 2A R2 W01: record who wrote each ledger row (design 5.8, B04).
+-- Version: v1.3.0
+-- Purpose: add a nullable `write_principal` (default 'unverified') and a
+--          `write_principal_self_asserted` marker to every table a writer
+--          touches. HTTP writers store the principal verified from their
+--          bearer token; local writers (eventbus_consumer, hooks) store a
+--          self-asserted internal principal; everything else stays
+--          'unverified'. Existing rows read as 'unverified' (no rewrite).
+--
+-- Applied automatically on first use by whichever process opens the ledger
+-- first (ChronicleStore.initialize_schema or capture_client). Older readers
+-- and writers that name their columns keep working: both columns are
+-- nullable-or-defaulted.
+--
+-- ROLLBACK (run these, one statement per line, then restart the writers on the
+-- previous code; rows keep all their other data; SQLite >= 3.35 for DROP COLUMN):
+-- ROLLBACK: ALTER TABLE raw_activity_events DROP COLUMN write_principal;
+-- ROLLBACK: ALTER TABLE raw_activity_events DROP COLUMN write_principal_self_asserted;

+-- ROLLBACK: ALTER TABLE work_log_entries DROP COLUMN write_principal_self_asserted;

+-- ROLLBACK: ALTER TABLE issue_links DROP COLUMN write_principal_self_asserted;

+-- ROLLBACK: ALTER TABLE reflection_cards DROP COLUMN write_principal_self_asserted;

+def _skip_existing_add_columns(conn: sqlite3.Connection, script: str) -> str:
+    """Drop ``ALTER TABLE .. ADD COLUMN`` statements that cannot or need not run.
+
+    SQLite has no ``ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS``. Without this a migration fails
+    on a ledger whose fresh schema.sql already created the column, or when two
+    processes (server and a hook) migrate the same ledger at once. A statement
+    whose table does not exist yet (a legacy ledger that predates the table) is
+    dropped too: schema.sql creates that table with the column already in it.
+    """
+
+    def _replace(match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
+        table, column = match.group(1), match.group(2)
+        if not _table_exists(conn, table):
+            return ""
+        existing = {row[1] for row in conn.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})")}
+        return "" if column in existing else match.group(0)
+
+    return _ADD_COLUMN_RE.sub(_replace, script)
+
+
 def _script_has_explicit_transaction(script: str) -> bool:
     return bool(_BEGIN_RE.search(script) and _COMMIT_RE.search(script))
 
@@ -114,12 +137,23 @@ def apply_chronicle_migrations(
             # Support bootstrap paths where schema.sql already seeded a newer version.
             continue
 
-        migration_sql = migration_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        migration_sql = _skip_existing_add_columns(
+            conn, migration_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        )
         try:
             _execute_script_transactional(conn, migration_sql)
             conn.commit()
         except Exception as exc:
             conn.rollback()
+            if conn.execute(
+                "SELECT 1 FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = ? LIMIT 1",
+                (version_str,),
+            ).fetchone():
+                # Another process applied it between our read and our write.
+                logger.info("ALREADY APPLIED %s (by another process)", migration_path.name)
+                applied_versions.add(version_str)
+                current_max = version_tuple
+                continue
             raise RuntimeError(
                 f"Failed applying chronicle migration {migration_path.name}: {exc}"
             ) from exc

+# HTTP 401 for an unverified mutating MCP tools/call in enforce mode (the tools
+# re-check; this adds the early status). The gate is looked up per request.
+app.add_middleware(WriteAuthMiddleware, get_gate=lambda: AUTH_GATE)
+
+
+def write_identity(request: Request) -> Identity:
+    """Dependency for mutating routes: verify the caller per the auth mode."""
+    client = request.client
+    caller = caller_label(request.headers.get("user-agent"), client.host if client else None)
+    try:
+        return AUTH_GATE.authorize_write(request.headers.get("authorization"), caller)
+    except Unauthorized as exc:
+        raise HTTPException(
+            status_code=401, detail=str(exc), headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"}
+        ) from None
+
+
+def require_write_ids(
+    workspace_id: Optional[str], instance_id: Optional[str]
+) -> tuple[str, str]:
+    """Resolve a write's ids: omitted ones come from this server's binding.
+
+    Rejects a workspace that is not this server's, and every write when the
+    server has no binding.
+    """
+    try:
+        return validate_write_ids(
+            workspace_id, instance_id, BOUND_WORKSPACE_ID, DEFAULT_INSTANCE_ID
+        )
+    except WriteIdentityError as exc:
+        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from None
+

+    def _authorize_write(
+        workspace_id: Optional[str], instance_id: Optional[str]
+    ) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
+        """Verify the caller and resolve the ids of a write.
+
+        Returns ``(principal, workspace_id, instance_id)`` with omitted ids
+        filled from the server's binding.
+        """
+        try:
+            request = get_http_request()
+            headers = request.headers
+            authorization = headers.get("authorization")
+            caller = caller_label(
+                headers.get("user-agent"), request.client.host if request.client else None
+            )
+        except RuntimeError:  # no HTTP request: in-process call, nothing to verify
+            authorization, caller = None, "in-process"
+        try:
+            identity = gate.authorize_write(authorization, caller)
+            workspace_id, instance_id = validate_write_ids(
+                workspace_id, instance_id, bound_workspace_id, read_default_instance_id
+            )
+        except (Unauthorized, WriteIdentityError) as exc:
+            raise ToolError(str(exc)) from None
+        return identity.principal, workspace_id, instance_id

+# Wave 2A R2 W05: ConPort writes carry this principal's token (design 5.3).
+_PRINCIPAL = "internal:adhd"
+
+
+def _bearer_headers() -> Dict[str, str]:
+    """Authorization header for this caller's principal, or {} when no token is available.
+
+    A missing token never fails a write: in log mode the server records it as
+    unverified. Only the principal name is logged, never a token.
+    """
+    try:
+        from dopemux.auth import AuthError, get_token
+    except ImportError:
+        logger.info("dopemux.auth is unavailable; sending %s writes without a token", _PRINCIPAL)
+        return {}
+    try:
+        return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {get_token(_PRINCIPAL)}"}
+    except AuthError:
+        logger.info("no token for %s; sending the write without Authorization", _PRINCIPAL)
+        return {}
+

@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@
+"""Runner/internal bearer tokens for the memory servers (Wave 2A R2, W03).
+
+Public API (import from here):
+
+- ``get_token(principal) -> str``: the token for a principal, read from the
+  macOS keychain (service ``dopemux-mcp``). Raises ``TokenNotFoundError`` (no
+  item), ``KeychainError`` (locked keychain, no login session, timeout, empty
+  output) or ``InvalidPrincipalError``. Never retries, never prints a token.
+  W04's bridge, W02's proxy fallback and W05's internal callers use this.
+- ``store_token`` / ``delete_token``: keychain writes (used by ``dopemux auth``).
+- ``generate_token()``, ``hash_token(token)``, ``validate_principal(p)``,
+  ``is_known_principal(p)``, ``KNOWN_PRINCIPALS``.
+- ``hashfile``: the shared ``memory-token-hashes.json`` and date-only sidecar.
+"""
+
+from . import hashfile
+from .errors import (
+    AuthError,
+    HashFileError,
+    InvalidPrincipalError,
+    KeychainError,
+    TokenNotFoundError,
+)
+from .keychain import SERVICE, delete_token, get_token, store_token
+from .tokens import (
+    KNOWN_PRINCIPALS,
+    generate_token,
+    hash_token,
+    is_known_principal,
+    validate_principal,
+)
+
+__all__ = [
+    "AuthError",
+    "HashFileError",
+    "InvalidPrincipalError",
+    "KNOWN_PRINCIPALS",
+    "KeychainError",
+    "SERVICE",
+    "TokenNotFoundError",
+    "delete_token",

+    "get_token",
+    "hash_token",
+    "hashfile",
+    "is_known_principal",
+    "store_token",
+    "validate_principal",
+]

@@ -0,0 +1,23 @@
+"""Typed errors for ``dopemux.auth``. No message here ever carries a token."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+
+class AuthError(Exception):
+    """Base class for every ``dopemux.auth`` failure."""
+
+
+class InvalidPrincipalError(AuthError, ValueError):
+    """A principal does not match ``(runner|internal):<name>``."""
+
+
+class KeychainError(AuthError):
+    """The macOS keychain could not be read or written (hard failure, no retry)."""
+
+
+class TokenNotFoundError(KeychainError):
+    """The keychain has no item for the principal."""
+
+
+class HashFileError(AuthError):
+    """The shared hash file exists but cannot be trusted or written."""
diff --git a/src/dopemux/auth/hashfile.py b/src/dopemux/auth/hashfile.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..5171f4ded
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/dopemux/auth/hashfile.py
@@ -0,0 +1,128 @@
+"""The shared token-hash file and the date-only metadata sidecar.
+
+``~/.config/dopemux/auth/memory-token-hashes.json`` is read by dope-memory
+(W01) and ConPort (W02): one JSON object ``{"<lowercase hex sha256 of the
+UTF-8 token>": "<principal>"}``. Directory mode 0700, file mode 0600, written
+atomically (temp file in the same directory, fsync, rename).
+
+``memory-token-meta.json`` beside it maps principal -> ``{"created", "rotated"}``
+(UTC timestamps). It holds no tokens and no hashes; ``dopemux auth list`` reads it.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import os
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+from .errors import HashFileError
+from .tokens import PRINCIPAL_RE
+
+__all__ = [
+    "HASH_FILE_NAME",
+    "META_FILE_NAME",
+    "HashFileError",
+    "auth_dir",
+    "hash_file_path",
+    "meta_file_path",
+    "load_hashes",
+    "write_hashes",
+    "load_meta",
+    "write_meta",
+]
+
+HASH_FILE_NAME = "memory-token-hashes.json"
+META_FILE_NAME = "memory-token-meta.json"
+
+
+def auth_dir() -> Path:
+    return Path.home() / ".config" / "dopemux" / "auth"
+
+
+def hash_file_path() -> Path:
+    return auth_dir() / HASH_FILE_NAME
+
+
+def meta_file_path() -> Path:
+    return auth_dir() / META_FILE_NAME
+
+
+def _write_json_atomic(path: Path, payload: Any) -> None:
+    directory = path.parent
+    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    os.chmod(directory, 0o700)
+    fd, tmp_name = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=directory, prefix=".tmp-", suffix=".json")
+    try:
+        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+            os.fchmod(fh.fileno(), 0o600)
+            json.dump(payload, fh, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
+            fh.write("\n")
+            fh.flush()
+            os.fsync(fh.fileno())
+        os.replace(tmp_name, path)
+    except BaseException:
+        try:
+            os.unlink(tmp_name)
+        except FileNotFoundError:
+            pass
+        raise
+    try:
+        dir_fd = os.open(directory, os.O_RDONLY)
+        try:
+            os.fsync(dir_fd)
+        finally:
+            os.close(dir_fd)
+    except OSError:
+        pass
+
+
+def load_hashes() -> dict[str, str]:
+    """Return the hash -> principal mapping; ``{}`` if the file is absent.
+
+    An unreadable, non-object or malformed file raises ``HashFileError`` so a
+    write never silently discards entries.
+    """
+    path = hash_file_path()
+    try:
+        raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    except FileNotFoundError:
+        return {}
+    except OSError as exc:
+        raise HashFileError(f"cannot read {path} ({type(exc).__name__})") from None
+    try:
+        data = json.loads(raw)
+    except ValueError:
+        raise HashFileError(f"{path} is not valid JSON; fix or remove it first") from None
+    if not isinstance(data, dict):
+        raise HashFileError(f"{path} is not a JSON object; fix or remove it first")
+    for digest, principal in data.items():
+        if not (
+            isinstance(digest, str)
+            and len(digest) == 64
+            and all(c in "0123456789abcdef" for c in digest)
+            and isinstance(principal, str)
+            and PRINCIPAL_RE.match(principal)
+        ):
+            raise HashFileError(f"{path} has a malformed entry; fix or remove it first")
+    return dict(data)
+
+
+def write_hashes(hashes: dict[str, str]) -> None:
+    _write_json_atomic(hash_file_path(), hashes)
+
+
+def load_meta() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
+    """Return principal -> metadata; unreadable metadata reads as empty (dates only)."""
+    try:
+        data = json.loads(meta_file_path().read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    except (OSError, ValueError):
+        return {}
+    if not isinstance(data, dict):
+        return {}
+    return {k: v for k, v in data.items() if isinstance(k, str) and isinstance(v, dict)}
+
+
+def write_meta(meta: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
+    _write_json_atomic(meta_file_path(), meta)
diff --git a/src/dopemux/auth/keychain.py b/src/dopemux/auth/keychain.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..fee4c8e9c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/dopemux/auth/keychain.py
@@ -0,0 +1,131 @@
+"""The only code that reads or writes the macOS keychain for runner tokens.
+
+Decision (proof/MACRO-DMX-RUNNER-MEMORY-2A-001-R2/review_bundle/KEYCHAIN_ACL_RESULT.md):
+- Items use the DEFAULT ACL: created by ``/usr/bin/security add-generic-password``
+  with no ``-T`` and no ``-A``.
+- Reads run ``/usr/bin/security find-generic-password -s dopemux-mcp -a <principal> -w``
+  in a child process with stdin closed and a timeout. Any nonzero exit, timeout
+  or empty output is a hard failure; the error names the likely cause. Nothing
+  is retried, so a keychain prompt is never waited on.
+- The token never goes on a command line (``ps`` would show it). Writes feed the
+  command to ``security -i`` on stdin.
+- Same-user processes can run the same read; that is a stated non-protection.
+
+Nothing in this module logs or formats a token into a message.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import hmac
+import logging
+import re
+import subprocess
+
+from .errors import KeychainError, TokenNotFoundError
+from .tokens import validate_principal
+
+logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+
+SERVICE = "dopemux-mcp"
+SECURITY_BIN = "/usr/bin/security"
+TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 10.0
+
+_ITEM_NOT_FOUND_EXIT = 44  # observed macOS security(1) exit for "item not found"
+_WRITABLE_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{15,511}$")
+
+_HINT = (
+    "The login keychain may be locked, or there is no login session "
+    "(for example over SSH or from a launchd job). Unlock it in a logged-in "
+    "terminal (`security unlock-keychain ~/Library/Keychains/login.keychain-db`) "
+    "and retry."
+)
+
+
+def _run(args: list[str], stdin_bytes: bytes | None = None) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
+    try:
+        if stdin_bytes is None:
+            return subprocess.run(
+                [SECURITY_BIN, *args],
+                stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
+                capture_output=True,
+                timeout=TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
+                check=False,
+            )
+        return subprocess.run(
+            [SECURITY_BIN, *args],
+            input=stdin_bytes,
+            capture_output=True,
+            timeout=TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
+            check=False,
+        )
+    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+        raise KeychainError(
+            f"keychain call timed out after {TIMEOUT_SECONDS:g}s (no retry). {_HINT}"
+        ) from None
+    except OSError as exc:
+        raise KeychainError(
+            f"cannot run {SECURITY_BIN} ({type(exc).__name__}); the keychain helper "
+            "needs macOS."
+        ) from None
+
+
+def get_token(principal: str) -> str:
+    """Return the token stored for ``principal``.
+
+    Raises ``InvalidPrincipalError`` for a malformed principal,
+    ``TokenNotFoundError`` when the keychain has no item, and ``KeychainError``
+    for every other failure (locked keychain, no login session, timeout, empty
+    output). Never retries.
+    """
+    validate_principal(principal)
+    proc = _run(["find-generic-password", "-s", SERVICE, "-a", principal, "-w"])
+    if proc.returncode == _ITEM_NOT_FOUND_EXIT:
+        raise TokenNotFoundError(
+            f"no {SERVICE} keychain item for {principal}; "
+            f"run `dopemux auth issue {principal} --apply`"
+        )
+    if proc.returncode != 0:
+        logger.warning("keychain read failed for %s (exit %s)", principal, proc.returncode)
+        raise KeychainError(
+            f"keychain read for {principal} failed (exit {proc.returncode}). {_HINT}"
+        )
+    token = proc.stdout.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
+    if not token:
+        raise KeychainError(f"keychain read for {principal} returned no value. {_HINT}")
+    return token
+
+
+def store_token(principal: str, token: str) -> None:
+    """Create or update the keychain item, then verify it by reading it back.
+
+    The token is sent on ``security -i`` stdin, never in argv. ``security -i``
+    can exit 0 after a failed command, so the read-back is part of success.
+    """
+    validate_principal(principal)
+    if not _WRITABLE_TOKEN_RE.match(token):
+        raise ValueError("token is not a base64url string produced by generate_token()")
+    line = f"add-generic-password -U -s {SERVICE} -a {principal} -w {token}\n"
+    proc = _run(["-i"], stdin_bytes=line.encode("utf-8"))
+    if proc.returncode != 0:
+        raise KeychainError(
+            f"keychain write for {principal} failed (exit {proc.returncode}). {_HINT}"
+        )
+    stored = get_token(principal)
+    if not hmac.compare_digest(stored.encode("utf-8"), token.encode("utf-8")):
+        raise KeychainError(
+            f"keychain write for {principal} did not take effect (read-back differs)"
+        )
+    logger.debug("stored keychain item for %s", principal)
+
+
+def delete_token(principal: str) -> bool:
+    """Delete the item. Return True if deleted, False if it was already absent."""
+    validate_principal(principal)
+    proc = _run(["delete-generic-password", "-s", SERVICE, "-a", principal])
+    if proc.returncode == 0:
+        return True
+    if proc.returncode == _ITEM_NOT_FOUND_EXIT:
+        return False
+    raise KeychainError(
+        f"keychain delete for {principal} failed (exit {proc.returncode}). {_HINT}"
+    )
diff --git a/src/dopemux/auth/tokens.py b/src/dopemux/auth/tokens.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..4cf3d42d1

+++ b/src/dopemux/auth/tokens.py
@@ -0,0 +1,61 @@
+"""Principals, token generation and hashing (pure functions, no I/O)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import base64
+import hashlib
+import re
+import secrets
+
+from .errors import InvalidPrincipalError
+
+# Same pattern the memory servers enforce when they load the hash file
+# (services/working-memory-assistant/auth_identity.py:69,
+# docker/mcp-servers-source/conport/auth_identity.py).
+PRINCIPAL_RE = re.compile(r"^(runner|internal):[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]{0,63}$")
+
+# Design 5.1 (Wave 2A). Other principals matching PRINCIPAL_RE are accepted.
+KNOWN_PRINCIPALS = (
+    "runner:claude",
+    "runner:codex",
+    "internal:hooks",
+    "internal:cli",
+    "internal:wma",
+    "internal:bridge-proxy",
+    "internal:dopecon",
+    "internal:adhd",
+    "internal:brain",
+)
+
+TOKEN_BYTES = 32
+
+
+def validate_principal(principal: str) -> str:
+    """Return ``principal`` unchanged or raise ``InvalidPrincipalError``."""
+    if not isinstance(principal, str) or not PRINCIPAL_RE.match(principal):
+        raise InvalidPrincipalError(
+            "principal must match (runner|internal):<name> with <name> in "
+            "[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]{0,63}"
+        )
+    return principal
+
+
+def is_known_principal(principal: str) -> bool:
+    return principal in KNOWN_PRINCIPALS
+
+
+def generate_token() -> str:
+    """32 random bytes as unpadded base64url.
+
+    A token that would begin with ``-`` is redrawn so it can never be read as an
+    option by ``security -i`` argument parsing.
+    """
+    while True:
+        token = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(secrets.token_bytes(TOKEN_BYTES)).decode("ascii").rstrip("=")
+        if not token.startswith("-"):
+            return token
+
+
+def hash_token(token: str) -> str:
+    """Lowercase hex SHA-256 of the UTF-8 token (the hash-file key format)."""
+    return hashlib.sha256(token.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

diff --git a/src/dopemux/commands/auth_commands.py b/src/dopemux/commands/auth_commands.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..202c5656f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/dopemux/commands/auth_commands.py

+"""``dopemux auth``: issue, rotate, revoke and list runner/internal memory tokens.
+
+Tokens live only in the macOS keychain (service ``dopemux-mcp``, account =
+principal). The memory servers verify SHA-256 hashes from
+``~/.config/dopemux/auth/memory-token-hashes.json``. Every command that writes
+prints a dry-run plan unless ``--apply`` is given. No command prints a token or
+a hash; ``list`` prints principals and dates only.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from typing import Any
+
+import click
+
+from ..auth import (
+    SERVICE,
+    AuthError,
+    InvalidPrincipalError,
+    delete_token,
+    generate_token,
+    hash_token,
+    hashfile,
+    is_known_principal,
+    store_token,
+    validate_principal,
+)
+
+
+def _principal_param(_ctx: click.Context, _param: click.Parameter, value: str) -> str:
+    try:
+        return validate_principal(value)
+    except InvalidPrincipalError as exc:
+        raise click.BadParameter(str(exc)) from None
+
+
+def _now() -> str:
+    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
+
+
+def _fail(exc: Exception) -> click.ClickException:
+    return click.ClickException(str(exc))
+
+
+def _load_hashes() -> dict[str, str]:
+    try:
+        return hashfile.load_hashes()
+    except AuthError as exc:
+        raise _fail(exc) from None
+
+
+def _show_plan(title: str, principal: str, steps: list[str]) -> None:
+    click.echo(f"DRY RUN: {title} {principal} (nothing changed; add --apply to execute)")
+    if not is_known_principal(principal):
+        click.echo(f"note: {principal} is not in the known 2A principal list")
+    for step in steps:
+        click.echo(f"  - {step}")
+
+
+_HASH_STEP = f"write {hashfile.HASH_FILE_NAME} atomically in ~/.config/dopemux/auth (dir 0700, file 0600)"
+
+
+@click.group()
+def auth() -> None:
+    """Manage runner and internal tokens for the memory servers (keychain-held)."""
+
+
+apply_option = click.option(
+    "--apply", "apply_", is_flag=True, help="Execute. Without it, print the plan only."
+)
+
+
+@auth.command()
+@click.argument("principal", callback=_principal_param)
+@apply_option
+def issue(principal: str, apply_: bool) -> None:
+    """Create a token for PRINCIPAL (for example runner:codex)."""
+    hashes = _load_hashes()
+    if principal in hashes.values():
+        raise click.ClickException(
+            f"{principal} already has a token; use `dopemux auth rotate {principal}`"
+        )
+    if not apply_:
+        _show_plan(
+            "issue",
+            principal,
+            [
+                f"generate 32 random bytes (base64url) and store them in the keychain: "
+                f"service {SERVICE}, account {principal}, default ACL",
+                _HASH_STEP + " with the token's SHA-256 -> principal",
+                "record the creation date in the sidecar (no token, no hash)",
+            ],
+        )
+        return
+    token = generate_token()
+    try:
+        store_token(principal, token)
+        hashes[hash_token(token)] = principal
+        hashfile.write_hashes(hashes)
+        meta = hashfile.load_meta()
+        meta[principal] = {"created": _now(), "rotated": None}
+        hashfile.write_meta(meta)
+    except (AuthError, OSError) as exc:
+        raise _fail(exc) from None
+    click.echo(f"issued {principal}: token stored in keychain, hash recorded")
+
+
+@auth.command()
+@click.argument("principal", callback=_principal_param)
+@apply_option
+def rotate(principal: str, apply_: bool) -> None:
+    """Replace the token for PRINCIPAL; the old hash stops verifying."""
+    hashes = _load_hashes()
+    if principal not in hashes.values():
+        raise click.ClickException(
+            f"{principal} has no token; use `dopemux auth issue {principal}`"
+        )
+    if not apply_:
+        _show_plan(
+            "rotate",
+            principal,
+            [
+                f"generate a new token and overwrite the keychain item (service {SERVICE}, "
+                f"account {principal})",
+                _HASH_STEP + "; the old hash for this principal is removed",
+                "record the rotation date in the sidecar (creation date kept)",
+            ],
+        )
+        return
+    token = generate_token()
+    try:
+        store_token(principal, token)
+        kept = {h: p for h, p in hashes.items() if p != principal}
+        kept[hash_token(token)] = principal
+        hashfile.write_hashes(kept)
+        meta = hashfile.load_meta()
+        entry: dict[str, Any] = dict(meta.get(principal) or {})
+        entry.setdefault("created", _now())
+        entry["rotated"] = _now()
+        meta[principal] = entry
+        hashfile.write_meta(meta)
+    except (AuthError, OSError) as exc:
+        raise _fail(exc) from None
+    click.echo(f"rotated {principal}: new token in keychain, old hash removed")
+
+
+@auth.command()
+@click.argument("principal", callback=_principal_param)
+@apply_option
+def revoke(principal: str, apply_: bool) -> None:
+    """Remove PRINCIPAL's hash, metadata and keychain item."""
+    hashes = _load_hashes()
+    if not apply_:
+        _show_plan(
+            "revoke",
+            principal,
+            [
+                _HASH_STEP + f"; every hash for {principal} is removed",
+                "drop the principal from the sidecar",
+                f"delete the keychain item (service {SERVICE}, account {principal})",
+            ],
+        )
+        return
+    try:
+        hashfile.write_hashes({h: p for h, p in hashes.items() if p != principal})
+        meta = hashfile.load_meta()
+        meta.pop(principal, None)
+        hashfile.write_meta(meta)
+        deleted = delete_token(principal)
+    except (AuthError, OSError) as exc:
+        raise _fail(exc) from None
+    suffix = "keychain item deleted" if deleted else "keychain item was already absent"
+    click.echo(f"revoked {principal}: hash removed, {suffix}")
+
+
+@auth.command(name="list")
+def list_principals() -> None:
+    """List issued principals and creation dates (never tokens or hashes)."""
+    principals = sorted(set(_load_hashes().values()))
+    if not principals:
+        click.echo("no principals issued")
+        return
+    meta = hashfile.load_meta()
+    click.echo(f"{'PRINCIPAL':<32} {'CREATED':<21} ROTATED")
+    for principal in principals:
+        entry = meta.get(principal) or {}
+        created = entry.get("created") or "unknown"
+        rotated = entry.get("rotated") or "-"
+        click.echo(f"{principal:<32} {created:<21} {rotated}")
diff --git a/src/dopemux/commands/decisions_commands.py b/src/dopemux/commands/decisions_commands.py
index dd68157d3..f4213af8c 100644
--- a/src/dopemux/commands/decisions_commands.py
+++ b/src/dopemux/commands/decisions_commands.py
@@ -6,6 +6,7 @@ were absent.  This module now implements them against the ConPort HTTP
 API (localhost:${CONPORT_HTTP_PORT:-3004} or CONPORT_URL).
 """
 
+import logging

+# Wave 2A R2 W05: decision writes carry this principal's token (design 5.3).
+_PRINCIPAL = "internal:cli"
+
+
+def _bearer_headers() -> dict[str, str]:
+    """Authorization header for this caller's principal, or {} when no token is available.
+
+    A missing token never fails a write: in log mode the server records it as
+    unverified. Only the principal name is logged, never a token.
+    """
+    try:
+        from dopemux.auth import AuthError, get_token
+    except ImportError:
+        logger.info("dopemux.auth is unavailable; sending %s writes without a token", _PRINCIPAL)
+        return {}
+    try:
+        return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {get_token(_PRINCIPAL)}"}
+    except AuthError:
+        logger.info("no token for %s; sending the write without Authorization", _PRINCIPAL)
+        return {}
+
+
+def _post_decision(workspace_id: str, summary: str, rationale: str) -> requests.Response:
+    """POST one decision. instance_id (optional for decisions) rides along when the envrc exports it."""
+    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
+        "workspace_id": workspace_id,
+        "summary": summary,
+        "rationale": rationale,
+        "alternatives": [],
+    }
+    if instance_id := os.environ.get("DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID"):
+        payload["instance_id"] = instance_id
+    headers = _bearer_headers()
+    return requests.post(
+        f"{_conport_url()}/api/decisions",
+        json=payload,
+        timeout=10,
+        **({"headers": headers} if headers else {}),
+    )
+
+

+# Wave 2A: the memory servers reach runners through the authenticating stdio
+# route (`dopemux.mcp.http_stdio_bridge --server NAME --principal P`), which
+# finds the checkout's ports in `.envrc.dopemux-mcp` and reads P's token from
+# the keychain. The bridge is the same in every worktree, so these entries are
+# rendered from this table, not from the catalog's per-worktree `url_template`
+# (which still feeds health probes, port allocation and compose alignment).
+# In Wave 2A only the Codex config uses it by default; Claude Code's bridge
+# entry (`_render_claude_memory_bridge_entry`) exists but is not wired in.
+#
+# The interpreter is the bare name `python`, the same choice the legacy
+# `--base-url` entries make (config/manager.py): a fixed string keeps the
+# generated files, the Codex parity gate and `ensure` drift detection
+# independent of which interpreter ran the generator.
+MEMORY_BRIDGE_SERVERS: Tuple[str, ...] = ("conport", "dope-memory")
+MEMORY_BRIDGE_PYTHON = "python"
+MEMORY_BRIDGE_MODULE = "dopemux.mcp.http_stdio_bridge"
+CLAUDE_MEMORY_PRINCIPAL = "runner:claude"
+CODEX_MEMORY_PRINCIPAL = "runner:codex"
+
+
+def _memory_bridge_command(server: str, principal: str) -> Tuple[str, List[str]]:
+    """`(command, args)` that start the stdio route for one memory server."""
+    return MEMORY_BRIDGE_PYTHON, ["-m", MEMORY_BRIDGE_MODULE, "--server", server, "--principal", principal]
+
+
+def _render_claude_memory_bridge_entry(name: str, spec: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    """Claude Code stdio entry that runs the authenticating bridge as `runner:claude`.
+
+    Not used by default: in Wave 2A Claude Code keeps its direct HTTP/SSE
+    entries from `_render_local_entry` (AMENDMENT_02); Wave 2B wires this up.
+    The entry carries no `env` because the bridge reads the checkout's envrc
+    itself.
+    """
+    if name not in MEMORY_BRIDGE_SERVERS:
+        raise click.ClickException(
+            f"`{name}` has no stdio bridge profile (expected one of {', '.join(MEMORY_BRIDGE_SERVERS)})."
+        )
+    command, args = _memory_bridge_command(name, CLAUDE_MEMORY_PRINCIPAL)
+    entry: Dict[str, Any] = {"type": "stdio", "command": command, "args": args}
+    if spec.get("description"):
+        entry["description"] = spec["description"]
+    return entry
+
+

     """
     Render a per-worktree catalog entry as a Claude Code .mcp.json mcpServers value.

+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Codex memory gate (Wave 2A design 5.7): replaces `--allow-sequenced`.
+#
+# Before `generate --apply` writes a write-capable Codex config for the memory
+# servers it checks (1) that dope-memory and ConPort report log or enforce mode
+# with identity-required writes (AMENDMENT_03), and (2) that a keychain token
+# exists for the target runner. Any failure leaves only the preview and names
+# the check.
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+ALLOW_SEQUENCED_REMOVED_MESSAGE = (
+    "--allow-sequenced was removed in Wave 2A. The in-repo .codex/config.toml write is now "
+    "gated by two checks that `dopemux mcp generate --agent-matrix --apply` runs itself: "
+    "(1) the dope-memory and ConPort /health endpoints report auth_mode 'log' or 'enforce' and "
+    "identity_required_writes true (ConPort also auth_principal_column 'ok'); "
+    f"(2) a keychain token exists for {CODEX_MEMORY_PRINCIPAL} "
+    f"(`dopemux auth issue {CODEX_MEMORY_PRINCIPAL} --apply`). "
+    "When a check fails only the preview is written and the check is named. "
+    "Re-run without --allow-sequenced."
+)
+
+# `off` and anything else fail. In `log` mode the servers still store the
+# verified principal for every request that carries a token.
+_GATE_ACCEPTED_AUTH_MODES = ("log", "enforce")
+
+_HEALTH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 3.0
+# Loopback health probes carry no credentials; an empty ProxyHandler keeps them
+# off any environment proxy.
+_HEALTH_OPENER = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}))
+
+# (server, envrc key holding the port that serves its REST /health, extra
+# fields that must equal a value). ConPort's REST port is CONPORT_HTTP_PORT,
+# not the MCP port the bridge uses.
+_MEMORY_HEALTH_TARGETS: Tuple[Tuple[str, str, Tuple[Tuple[str, str], ...]], ...] = (
+    ("dope-memory", "DOPE_MEMORY_PORT", ()),
+    ("conport", "CONPORT_HTTP_PORT", (("auth_principal_column", "ok"),)),
+)
+
+
+class _HealthProbeError(Exception):
+    """A server's /health could not be located or read (message is safe to print)."""
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class GateCheck:
+    name: str
+    ok: bool
+    detail: str
+    # Server name and its reported auth_mode for a passing health check; lets
+    # the apply output say when servers are still in log mode.
+    server: Optional[str] = None
+    auth_mode: Optional[str] = None
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class CodexMemoryGate:
+    """Outcome of every gate check; carries messages only, never a token."""
+
+    checks: Tuple[GateCheck, ...]
+
+    @property
+    def ok(self) -> bool:
+        return all(check.ok for check in self.checks)
+
+    @property
+    def failures(self) -> Tuple[str, ...]:
+        return tuple(check.detail for check in self.checks if not check.ok)
+
+    @property
+    def log_mode_servers(self) -> Tuple[str, ...]:
+        """Servers whose passing health check reported `log` (not yet `enforce`)."""
+        return tuple(
+            check.server
+            for check in self.checks
+            if check.ok and check.auth_mode == "log" and check.server
+        )
+
+
+def _fetch_health(port: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    """GET http://127.0.0.1:<port>/health and return the JSON object (200 only)."""
+    import http.client
+
+    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/health"
+    try:
+        with _HEALTH_OPENER.open(url, timeout=_HEALTH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS) as response:
+            if response.status != 200:
+                raise _HealthProbeError(f"{url} answered HTTP {response.status}")
+            raw = response.read(1 << 16)
+    except _HealthProbeError:
+        raise
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
+        raise _HealthProbeError(f"{url} answered HTTP {exc.code}") from None
+    except (OSError, ValueError, http.client.HTTPException) as exc:
+        raise _HealthProbeError(f"{url} is unreachable ({type(exc).__name__})") from None
+    try:
+        body = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
+    except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError):
+        raise _HealthProbeError(f"{url} did not return JSON") from None
+    if not isinstance(body, dict):
+        raise _HealthProbeError(f"{url} did not return a JSON object")
+    return body
+
+
+def _health_port(repo_path: Path, key: str) -> int:
+    """The `key` port from the checkout's envrc, reading no other value from it."""
+    from ..mcp import http_stdio_bridge as bridge
+    from ..mcp.envrc import safe_port_int
+
+    envrc = bridge._find_envrc(repo_path)
+    if envrc is None:
+        raise _HealthProbeError(
+            f"no {bridge.ENVRC_FILENAME} from {repo_path} up to the checkout root; "
+            "run `dopemux mcp init` in this checkout"
+        )
+    try:
+        values = bridge._read_selected_keys(envrc, frozenset({key}))
+    except bridge.BridgeStartupError as exc:
+        raise _HealthProbeError(str(exc)) from None
+    port = safe_port_int(values, key)
+    if port is None:
+        raise _HealthProbeError(
+            f"{envrc} has no valid {key}; run `dopemux mcp init` / `dopemux mcp start` in this checkout"
+        )
+    return port
+
+
+def _short(value: Any) -> str:
+    # Brackets would read as console markup when the message is logged.
+    text = repr(value).replace("[", "(").replace("]", ")")
+    return text if len(text) <= 40 else text[:37] + "..."
+
+
+def _check_memory_server_health(
+    repo_path: Path,
+    server: str,
+    port_key: str,
+    extra: Tuple[Tuple[str, str], ...],
+) -> GateCheck:
+    prefix = f"gate check 1 (server health) {server}"
+    name = f"memory-health:{server}"
+    try:
+        body = _fetch_health(_health_port(repo_path, port_key))
+    except _HealthProbeError as exc:
+        return GateCheck(name, False, f"{prefix}: {exc}")
+    problems: List[str] = []
+    mode = body.get("auth_mode")
+    if mode not in _GATE_ACCEPTED_AUTH_MODES:
+        problems.append(f"auth_mode is {_short(mode)}, expected 'log' or 'enforce'")
+    if body.get("identity_required_writes") is not True:
+        problems.append(
+            f"identity_required_writes is {_short(body.get('identity_required_writes'))}, expected true"
+        )
+    for key, expected in extra:
+        if body.get(key) != expected:
+            problems.append(f"{key} is {_short(body.get(key))}, expected {expected!r}")
+    if problems:
+        return GateCheck(name, False, f"{prefix}: " + "; ".join(problems))
+    return GateCheck(name, True, f"{prefix}: {mode}, identity-required writes", server=server, auth_mode=mode)
+
+
+def _check_runner_token(principal: str) -> GateCheck:
+    name = f"runner-token:{principal}"
+    try:
+        get_token(principal)  # existence check only: the value is discarded unread
+    except AuthError as exc:
+        return GateCheck(
+            name,
+            False,
+            f"gate check 2 (runner token) {principal}: no usable keychain token "
+            f"({type(exc).__name__}: {exc}); issue one with `dopemux auth issue {principal} --apply`",
+        )
+    return GateCheck(name, True, f"gate check 2 (runner token) {principal}: present")
+
+
+def evaluate_codex_memory_gate(
+    repo_path: Path,
+    principal: str = CODEX_MEMORY_PRINCIPAL,
+) -> CodexMemoryGate:
+    """Run every gate check for the checkout at `repo_path`; never raises for a failed check."""
+    checks = [
+        _check_memory_server_health(repo_path, server, port_key, extra)
+        for server, port_key, extra in _MEMORY_HEALTH_TARGETS
+    ]
+    checks.append(_check_runner_token(principal))
+    return CodexMemoryGate(tuple(checks))
+
+

 DEFAULT_CONPORT_PORT = 3004
 
+# Wave 2A R2 W05: writes carry this principal's token (design 5.3). custom_data
+# rows have no instance column, so the only id a write needs is workspace_id.
+_PRINCIPAL = "internal:cli"
+
+
+def _bearer_headers() -> dict:
+    """Authorization header for this caller's principal, or {} when no token is available.
+
+    A missing token never fails a write: in log mode the server records it as
+    unverified. Only the principal name is logged, never a token.
+    """
+    try:
+        from dopemux.auth import AuthError, get_token
+    except ImportError:
+        logger.info("dopemux.auth is unavailable; sending %s writes without a token", _PRINCIPAL)
+        return {}
+    try:
+        return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {get_token(_PRINCIPAL)}"}
+    except AuthError:
+        logger.info("no token for %s; sending the write without Authorization", _PRINCIPAL)
+        return {}
+

+DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODES = ("off", "log", "enforce")
+DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MOUNT = "/run/dopemux-auth"
+
+
+def resolve_dope_memory_auth_mode(auth_mode: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
+    """The dope-memory auth mode for a sidecar: argument, else env, else ``log``."""
+    raw = (auth_mode or os.environ.get("DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE") or "log").strip().lower()
+    if raw not in DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODES:
+        raise ValueError(
+            f"invalid dope-memory auth mode {raw!r}; use one of "
+            f"{', '.join(DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODES)}"
+        )
+    return raw
+
+
+def default_dope_memory_auth_dir() -> Path:
+    """Host directory holding the token-hash file (never inside a repo)."""
+    return Path.home() / ".config" / "dopemux" / "auth"
+
+
+def resolve_conport_auth_mode(auth_mode: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
+    """The ConPort auth mode for a sidecar: argument, else env, else ``log``.
+
+    ConPort and dope-memory are switched to ``enforce`` separately, so this
+    reads ``CONPORT_AUTH_MODE`` and never ``DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE``.
+    """
+    raw = (auth_mode or os.environ.get("CONPORT_AUTH_MODE") or "log").strip().lower()
+    if raw not in DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODES:
+        raise ValueError(
+            f"invalid conport auth mode {raw!r}; use one of "
+            f"{', '.join(DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODES)}"
+        )
+    return raw
+

-"""Bridge stdio JSON-RPC traffic to an MCP HTTP endpoint."""
+"""Bridge stdio JSON-RPC traffic to an MCP HTTP endpoint.
+
+Two modes:
+
+* ``--base-url URL`` (original): forward each line to ``URL/mcp`` as plain
+  JSON-RPC, unauthenticated.
+* ``--server dope-memory|conport --principal runner:<name>`` (Wave 2A): find the
+  server's port in the nearest ``.envrc.dopemux-mcp``, read the principal's
+  bearer token through ``dopemux.auth.get_token``, speak streamable HTTP
+  (session id echoed, SSE or JSON responses) and fill ``workspace_id`` /
+  ``instance_id`` where the tool schema declares them and the caller omitted
+  them. Every failure to find a port or a token ends in a stderr message and a
+  JSON-RPC error; no request is ever sent without ``Authorization``.
+"""

+ID_KEYS = ("workspace_id", "instance_id")
+MAX_TOOL_PAGES = 10

+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Wave 2A: discovery, identity and streamable HTTP
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+class BridgeStartupError(RuntimeError):
+    """Discovery or token lookup failed; nothing was sent."""
+
+
+class BridgeAuthError(RuntimeError):
+    """The server answered 401 or 403."""
+
+    def __init__(self, status: int) -> None:
+        super().__init__(f"HTTP {status} from MCP endpoint")
+        self.status = status
+
+
+class _SessionExpired(RuntimeError):
+    """HTTP 404 on a request that carried a session id."""
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class _Profile:
+    port_key: str
+    workspace_key: str
+    instance_key: str
+
+    @property
+    def keys(self) -> frozenset:
+        return frozenset({self.port_key, self.workspace_key, self.instance_key})
+
+
+# dope-memory and ConPort bind to different workspace/instance keys (W01, W02
+# returns); each server is sent only the pair it checks.
+PROFILES: Dict[str, _Profile] = {
+    "dope-memory": _Profile("DOPE_MEMORY_PORT", "DOPE_MEMORY_WORKSPACE_ID", "DOPE_MEMORY_INSTANCE_ID"),
+    "conport": _Profile("CONPORT_MCP_PORT", "DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID", "DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID"),
+}
+
+_ENVRC_KEY_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:export\s+)?([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\s*=")
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class Discovery:
+    """What the bridge keeps from the envrc: one port and up to two ids."""
+
+    server: str
+    envrc_path: Path
+    port: int
+    ids: Mapping[str, str]
+
+    @property
+    def base_url(self) -> str:
+        return f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.port}"
+
+
+def _find_envrc(start: Path) -> Optional[Path]:
+    """Nearest envrc at or above ``start``, never leaving the checkout.
+
+    A directory holding ``.git`` (a worktree has a ``.git`` file) is the
+    checkout root: if it has no envrc, the answer is None. This keeps a
+    worktree under ``<main>/.worktrees/`` from silently binding to the main
+    checkout's containers.
+    """
+    current = start.resolve()
+    while True:
+        candidate = current / ENVRC_FILENAME
+        if candidate.is_file():
+            return candidate
+        if (current / ".git").exists() or current.parent == current:
+            return None
+        current = current.parent
+
+
+def _read_selected_keys(path: Path, keys: frozenset) -> Dict[str, str]:
+    """Parse only the lines whose key is in ``keys``.
+
+    The file also exports a database password. Other lines are dropped before
+    the parser sees them, so no other value is held, logged or exported.
+    """
+    kept: List[str] = []
+    try:
+        with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
+            for raw in handle:
+                match = _ENVRC_KEY_RE.match(raw)
+                if match and match.group(1) in keys and not is_secret_like_key(match.group(1)):
+                    kept.append(raw)
+    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
+        raise BridgeStartupError(f"cannot read {path}: {type(exc).__name__}") from None
+    values, _errors = parse_envrc_text("".join(kept))
+    return {key: value for key, value in values.items() if key in keys}
+
+
+def discover_profile(server: str, start_dir: Path) -> Discovery:
+    """Resolve ``server``'s port and ids from the checkout's envrc, or fail closed."""
+    profile = PROFILES[server]
+    envrc = _find_envrc(Path(start_dir))
+    if envrc is None:
+        raise BridgeStartupError(
+            f"no {ENVRC_FILENAME} found from {start_dir} up to the checkout root; "
+            "run `dopemux mcp init` in this checkout (another checkout's file is never used)"
+        )
+    values = _read_selected_keys(envrc, profile.keys)
+    port = safe_port_int(values, profile.port_key)
+    if port is None:
+        raise BridgeStartupError(
+            f"{envrc} has no valid {profile.port_key}; "
+            "run `dopemux mcp init` / `dopemux mcp start` in this checkout"
+        )
+    ids = {
+        name: values[key]
+        for name, key in (("workspace_id", profile.workspace_key), ("instance_id", profile.instance_key))
+        if values.get(key)
+    }
+    return Discovery(server=server, envrc_path=envrc, port=port, ids=ids)
+
+
+class _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):
+    """Never follow a redirect: the bearer token must not leave the host we resolved."""
+
+    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):  # type: ignore[override]
+        return None
+
+
+# An empty ProxyHandler keeps the token away from any environment proxy.
+_OPENER = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}), _NoRedirect())
+
+
+def _parse_body(body: str, content_type: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    """Messages in a response body: SSE-framed or plain JSON."""
+    text = body.strip()
+    if not text:
+        return []
+    if "text/event-stream" in content_type:
+        payloads = []
+        for event in text.replace("\r\n", "\n").split("\n\n"):
+            data_lines = [
+                line[5:][1:] if line[5:].startswith(" ") else line[5:]
+                for line in event.split("\n")
+                if line.startswith("data:")
+            ]
+            if data_lines:
+                payloads.append("\n".join(data_lines))
+    else:
+        payloads = [text]
+    messages: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
+    for payload in payloads:
+        try:
+            parsed = json.loads(payload)
+        except JSONDecodeError as exc:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid JSON response from MCP endpoint: {payload[:200]}") from exc
+        items = parsed if isinstance(parsed, list) else [parsed]
+        for item in items:
+            if not isinstance(item, dict):
+                raise RuntimeError("Invalid MCP response payload: expected JSON object")
+            messages.append(item)
+    return messages
+
+
+class _StreamableClient:
+    """One streamable-HTTP session; the token lives only in the Authorization header."""
+
+    def __init__(self, endpoint: str, token: str, ids: Mapping[str, str], timeout: float) -> None:
+        self.endpoint = endpoint
+        self.session_id: Optional[str] = None
+        self._token = token
+        self._ids = dict(ids)
+        self._timeout = timeout
+        self._init_message: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
+        self._schemas: Dict[str, Tuple[str, ...]] = {}
+        self._listed = False
+        self._counter = 0
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return f"_StreamableClient(endpoint={self.endpoint!r}, session={self.session_id is not None})"
+
+    def _scrub(self, text: str) -> str:
+        return text.replace(self._token, "[REDACTED]") if self._token else text
+
+    def _exchange(self, message: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+        """POST one message; return the messages in the reply (empty for a 202)."""
+        is_initialize = message.get("method") == "initialize"
+        if is_initialize:
+            self.session_id = None
+        headers = {
+            "Accept": STREAMABLE_ACCEPT,
+            "Content-Type": "application/json",
+            "Authorization": f"Bearer {self._token}",
+        }
+        if self.session_id:
+            headers["Mcp-Session-Id"] = self.session_id
+        request = urllib.request.Request(
+            self.endpoint,
+            data=json.dumps(message).encode("utf-8"),
+            method="POST",
+            headers=headers,
+        )
+        try:
+            with _OPENER.open(request, timeout=self._timeout) as response:
+                body = response.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
+                content_type = response.headers.get("Content-Type", "")
+                new_session = response.headers.get("Mcp-Session-Id")
+        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
+            detail = ""
+            if exc.fp is not None:
+                detail = exc.fp.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
+            if exc.code in (401, 403):
+                raise BridgeAuthError(exc.code) from None
+            if exc.code == 404 and self.session_id:
+                raise _SessionExpired("MCP session expired (HTTP 404)") from None
+            text = f"HTTP {exc.code} from MCP endpoint"
+            if detail:
+                text = f"{text}: {self._scrub(detail)[:400]}"
+            raise RuntimeError(text) from None
+        except urllib.error.URLError as exc:
+            if isinstance(exc.reason, socket.timeout):
+                raise RuntimeError(f"Timeout contacting MCP endpoint after {self._timeout}s") from None
+            raise RuntimeError(f"Connection error to MCP endpoint: {exc.reason}") from None
+        except socket.timeout:
+            raise RuntimeError(f"Timeout contacting MCP endpoint after {self._timeout}s") from None
+
+        if is_initialize and new_session:
+            self.session_id = new_session
+        return _parse_body(self._scrub(body), content_type)
+
+    def _exchange_with_reinit(self, message: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+        """``_exchange``, re-initializing once if the server dropped the session."""
+        try:
+            return self._exchange(message)
+        except _SessionExpired:
+            if self._init_message is None:
+                raise RuntimeError("MCP session expired and the client never initialized it") from None
+        _stderr("MCP session expired; re-initializing once")
+        self.session_id = None
+        self._exchange(self._init_message)
+        self._exchange({"jsonrpc": JSONRPC_VERSION, "method": "notifications/initialized"})
+        return self._exchange(message)
+
+    # -- identity ----------------------------------------------------------
+
+    def _learn_schemas(self, messages: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
+        for message in messages:
+            result = message.get("result")
+            tools = result.get("tools") if isinstance(result, dict) else None
+            if not isinstance(tools, list):
+                continue
+            for tool in tools:
+                if not isinstance(tool, dict) or not isinstance(tool.get("name"), str):
+                    continue
+                schema = tool.get("inputSchema")
+                props = schema.get("properties") if isinstance(schema, dict) else None
+                declared = props if isinstance(props, dict) else {}
+                self._schemas[tool["name"]] = tuple(key for key in ID_KEYS if key in declared)
+
+    def _fetch_tools(self) -> None:
+        """Ask the server for its tool schemas once (the client never listed them)."""
+        self._listed = True
+        cursor: Optional[str] = None
+        for _ in range(MAX_TOOL_PAGES):
+            self._counter += 1
+            request: Dict[str, Any] = {
+                "jsonrpc": JSONRPC_VERSION,
+                "id": f"dopemux-bridge-tools-list-{self._counter}",
+                "method": "tools/list",
+            }
+            if cursor:
+                request["params"] = {"cursor": cursor}
+            try:
+                replies = self._exchange_with_reinit(request)
+            except BridgeAuthError:
+                raise
+            except Exception:
+                logger.debug("tool schema lookup failed", exc_info=True)
+                return
+            self._learn_schemas(replies)
+            cursor = next(
+                (
+                    r["result"]["nextCursor"]
+                    for r in replies
+                    if isinstance(r.get("result"), dict) and r["result"].get("nextCursor")
+                ),
+                None,
+            )
+            if not cursor:
+                return
+
+    def _hide_filled_required(self, messages: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+        """Copy of a ``tools/list`` reply with the ids the bridge fills no longer required.
+
+        Otherwise the model, told the ids are required, invents values the
+        server then rejects. Properties and every other field stay as the
+        server sent them; an id the bridge holds no value for stays required.
+        Only the copy printed for the client changes: ``_learn_schemas`` has
+        already read the original, so filling still knows the tool declares the id.
+        """
+        fillable = set(self._ids)
+        if not fillable:
+            return messages
+        shown: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
+        for message in messages:
+            result = message.get("result")
+            tools = result.get("tools") if isinstance(result, dict) else None
+            if not isinstance(tools, list):
+                shown.append(message)
+                continue
+            new_tools = []
+            for tool in tools:
+                schema = tool.get("inputSchema") if isinstance(tool, dict) else None
+                required = schema.get("required") if isinstance(schema, dict) else None
+                if not isinstance(required, list) or not fillable.intersection(required):
+                    new_tools.append(tool)
+                    continue
+                kept = [item for item in required if item not in fillable]
+                new_schema = {}
+                for key, value in schema.items():
+                    if key != "required":
+                        new_schema[key] = value
+                    elif kept:
+                        new_schema[key] = kept
+                new_tools.append({**tool, "inputSchema": new_schema})
+            shown.append({**message, "result": {**result, "tools": new_tools}})
+        return shown
+
+    def _fill_ids(self, message: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        """Add the ids a tool declares and the caller omitted; never replace a caller value."""
+        if message.get("method") != "tools/call" or not self._ids:
+            return message
+        params = message.get("params")
+        if not isinstance(params, dict) or not isinstance(params.get("name"), str):
+            return message
+        arguments = params.get("arguments")
+        if arguments is None:
+            arguments = {}
+        if not isinstance(arguments, dict):
+            return message
+        name = params["name"]
+        if name not in self._schemas and not self._listed and self.session_id:
+            self._fetch_tools()
+        additions = {
+            key: self._ids[key]
+            for key in self._schemas.get(name, ())
+            if key in self._ids and key not in arguments
+        }
+        if not additions:
+            return message
+        return {**message, "params": {**params, "arguments": {**arguments, **additions}}}
+
+    # -- entry point -------------------------------------------------------
+
+    def send(self, message: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+        """Forward one client message; return what must be printed on stdout."""
+        outgoing = self._fill_ids(message)
+        replies = self._exchange_with_reinit(outgoing)
+        method = message.get("method")
+        if method == "initialize":
+            self._init_message = message
+        elif method == "tools/list":
+            self._learn_schemas(replies)
+            return self._hide_filled_required(replies)
+        return replies
+
+
+def _parse_line(line: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    """Decode one stdin line; write the JSON-RPC error and return None if it is bad."""
+    try:
+        request_data = json.loads(line)
+    except JSONDecodeError:
+        _write_stdout(_jsonrpc_error(None, PARSE_ERROR_CODE, "Parse error"))
+        return None
+    if not isinstance(request_data, dict):
+        _write_stdout(_jsonrpc_error(None, INVALID_REQUEST_CODE, "Invalid Request"))
+        return None
+    return request_data
+
+
+def _fail_startup(message: str) -> int:
+    """Fail closed: stderr message, a JSON-RPC error for the first request, nonzero exit."""
+    _stderr(message)
+    while True:
+        line = sys.stdin.readline()
+        if line == "":
+            return EXIT_STARTUP
+        line = line.strip()
+        if not line:
+            continue
+        request_data = _parse_line(line)
+        if request_data is not None and "id" in request_data:
+            _write_stdout(_jsonrpc_error(request_data["id"], SERVER_ERROR_CODE, message))
+            return EXIT_STARTUP
+
+
+def _run_streamable(server: str, principal: str, timeout: float) -> int:
+    try:
+        discovery = discover_profile(server, Path.cwd())
+        token = get_token(principal)
+        if not token:
+            raise BridgeStartupError(f"the token for principal {principal} is empty")
+    except BridgeStartupError as exc:
+        return _fail_startup(str(exc))
+    except AuthError as exc:
+        return _fail_startup(f"cannot get the token for principal {principal}: {exc}")
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return _fail_startup(f"cannot read the working directory: {type(exc).__name__}")
+
+    client = _StreamableClient(f"{discovery.base_url}/mcp", token, discovery.ids, timeout)
+    del token
+
+    while True:
+        line = sys.stdin.readline()
+        if line == "":
+            return 0
+        line = line.strip()
+        if not line:
+            continue
+        request_data = _parse_line(line)
+        if request_data is None:
+            continue
+
+        is_request = "id" in request_data
+        request_id = request_data.get("id")
+        try:
+            replies = client.send(request_data)
+        except BridgeAuthError as exc:
+            message = (
+                f"HTTP {exc.status} from {server} for principal {principal}: the token was rejected; "
+                f"check that it is issued and that the server is configured to accept it. Stopping."
+            )
+            _stderr(message)
+            if is_request:
+                _write_stdout(_jsonrpc_error(request_id, SERVER_ERROR_CODE, message))
+            return EXIT_AUTH
+        except Exception as exc:
+            logger.debug("Bridge forward error", exc_info=True)
+            if is_request:
+                _write_stdout(
+                    _jsonrpc_error(
+                        request_id,
+                        SERVER_ERROR_CODE,
+                        "Bridge transport error",
+                        {"detail": str(exc)},
+                    )
+                )
+            else:
+                _stderr(f"notification not delivered: {exc}")
+            continue
+
+        for reply in replies:
+            _write_stdout(reply)
+        if is_request and not replies:
+            _write_stdout(
+                _jsonrpc_error(
+                    request_id,
+                    SERVER_ERROR_CODE,
+                    "Bridge transport error",
+                    {"detail": "Empty response body from MCP endpoint"},
+                )
+            )
+
+

 class CaptureError(RuntimeError):

+def _resolve_write_principal(principal: Optional[str]) -> str:
+    """Return the self-asserted principal to record, failing closed on bad input."""
+    if principal is None:
+        return PRINCIPAL_CLI
+    if not isinstance(principal, str) or not _INTERNAL_PRINCIPAL_RE.match(principal):
+        raise CaptureError(
+            "Invalid write principal: local writers may only self-assert "
+            "'internal:<name>' matching ^internal:[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]{0,63}$"
+        )
+    return principal

 logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 
+# Wave 2A R2 W05: writes carry this principal's token (design 5.3).
+_PRINCIPAL = "internal:cli"
+
+
+def _bearer_headers() -> Dict[str, str]:
+    """Authorization header for this caller's principal, or {} when no token is available.
+
+    A missing token never fails a write: in log mode the server records it as
+    unverified. Only the principal name is logged, never a token.
+    """
+    try:
+        from dopemux.auth import AuthError, get_token
+    except ImportError:
+        logger.info("dopemux.auth is unavailable; sending %s writes without a token", _PRINCIPAL)
+        return {}
+    try:
+        return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {get_token(_PRINCIPAL)}"}
+    except AuthError:
+        logger.info("no token for %s; sending the write without Authorization", _PRINCIPAL)
+        return {}
+
