 class DopeMemoryAdapter:
     """Adapter for communicating with the dope-memory HTTP API."""
 
-    def __init__(self, base_url: Optional[str] = None):
+    def __init__(self, base_url: Optional[str] = None, instance_id: Optional[str] = None):
         self.base_url = (base_url or os.getenv("DOPE_MEMORY_URL", "http://localhost:3020")).rstrip("/")
+        # dope-memory requires instance_id on writes; the envrc exports it as DOPE_MEMORY_INSTANCE_ID.
+        # workspace_id stays the caller's argument and must equal the server's DOPE_MEMORY_WORKSPACE_ID.
+        self.instance_id = instance_id or os.getenv("DOPE_MEMORY_INSTANCE_ID") or None
+
+    async def _write(self, path: str, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> httpx.Response:
+        """POST a write with this caller's token and instance id (omitted when unknown)."""
+        if self.instance_id:
+            payload = {**payload, "instance_id": self.instance_id}
+        headers = await asyncio.to_thread(_bearer_headers)
+        kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {"headers": headers} if headers else {}
+        return await self._request("POST", path, json=payload, **kwargs)
 
     async def _request(self, method: str, path: str, **kwargs) -> httpx.Response:

+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def _no_real_keychain(monkeypatch):
+    """Keep every test off the real macOS keychain.
+
+    `dopemux.auth.get_token` shells out to `security`; on a Mac with issued
+    runner tokens an unguarded test would read the real keychain. Pointing
+    SECURITY_BIN at a missing path makes every `get_token` raise KeychainError
+    (the Linux-container case) unless a test installs its own stub, as the
+    dopemux.auth tests do. Does nothing if `dopemux.auth` cannot be imported.
+    """
+    try:
+        keychain = importlib.import_module("dopemux.auth.keychain")
+    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - import shape varies across envs
+        return
+    monkeypatch.setattr(keychain, "SECURITY_BIN", "/nonexistent/security")
+

+# PreToolUse guard: it also tests the case-folded reading of every path (ADR-226 residual
+# F-001-A), and the lower-case spelling of a capitalized name matches the deny pattern.
+# Known and accepted (fail closed); a later guard change resolves it.
+_MEMORY_SPINE_REGEX_ONLY = (
+    "services/working-memory-assistant/Dockerfile.dope-memory",
+    "docker/mcp-servers-source/conport/Dockerfile",
+)
+
+
+def test_memory_spine_carved_out_paths_are_editable():
+    assert len(_MEMORY_SPINE_CARVED_OUT) == 22
+    for rel in _MEMORY_SPINE_CARVED_OUT:
+        if rel in _MEMORY_SPINE_REGEX_ONLY:
+            continue
+        for tool in ("Edit", "Write", "NotebookEdit"):
+            inp = {"file_path": str(_ROOT / rel)}
+            assert surface_guard_block(tool, inp, _ROOT) is None, (tool, rel)
+
+
+def test_memory_spine_carved_out_paths_are_all_exempt_at_the_regex_layer():
+    for rel in _MEMORY_SPINE_CARVED_OUT:
+        assert not _matches_forbidden_pattern(rel), rel
+
+
+def test_memory_spine_dockerfiles_are_still_denied_by_the_guard_case_fold_reading():
+    for rel in _MEMORY_SPINE_REGEX_ONLY:
+        for tool in ("Edit", "Write", "NotebookEdit"):
+            result = surface_guard_block(tool, {"file_path": str(_ROOT / rel)}, _ROOT)
+            assert result is not None, (tool, rel)
+            assert RED_LANE_ID in result, (tool, rel)
+
+
+def test_memory_spine_other_paths_remain_blocked():
+    for rel in _MEMORY_SPINE_STILL_BLOCKED:
+        result = surface_guard_block("Edit", {"file_path": str(_ROOT / rel)}, _ROOT)
+        assert result is not None, rel
+        assert RED_LANE_ID in result, rel
+
+
+def test_memory_spine_newline_variants_are_not_falsely_exempt():
+    for rel in _MEMORY_SPINE_CARVED_OUT:
+        for suffix in ("\n", "\nextra.py"):
+            assert _matches_forbidden_pattern(rel + suffix), repr(rel + suffix)
+            result = surface_guard_block("Edit", {"file_path": str(_ROOT / rel) + suffix}, _ROOT)
+            assert result is not None, repr(rel + suffix)
+
+
+def test_memory_spine_trailing_slash_is_not_exempt_at_regex_level():
+    for rel in _MEMORY_SPINE_CARVED_OUT:
+        assert _matches_forbidden_pattern(rel + "/"), rel
+
+
+def test_task_orchestrator_remains_fully_blocked_after_memory_spine_carveout():
+    for rel in (
+        "services/task-orchestrator/auth_identity.py",
+        "services/task-orchestrator/mcp_http.py",
+        "services/task-orchestrator/app/main.py",
+    ):
+        result = surface_guard_block("Edit", {"file_path": str(_ROOT / rel)}, _ROOT)
+        assert result is not None, rel
+        assert RED_LANE_ID in result, rel
+
+

 # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

+The dope-memory service modules are top-level imports (``chronicle``,
+``mcp_http``, ``auth_identity`` ...), so the service directory must be on

+"""Pure helpers shared by the W01 tests (no service imports)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import functools
+import hashlib
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import secrets
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+from types import ModuleType
+
+REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+SERVICE_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "services" / "working-memory-assistant"
+HASH_FILE_NAME = "memory-token-hashes.json"
+AUTH_IDENTITY_IMPORTERS = ("dope_memory_main", "mcp_http")
+
+
+@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=1)
+def load_dope_memory_auth_identity() -> ModuleType:
+    """Load dope-memory's ``auth_identity`` by path, once.
+
+    ConPort ships a top-level ``auth_identity`` too, and older tests leave ConPort's
+    directory ahead of this one on ``sys.path``, so a bare import can find the wrong
+    file.
+    """
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
+        "auth_identity", SERVICE_DIR / "auth_identity.py"
+    )
+    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    saved = sys.modules.get("auth_identity")
+    # dataclasses look their module up while it executes
+    sys.modules["auth_identity"] = module
+    try:
+        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
+    finally:
+        if saved is not None:
+            sys.modules["auth_identity"] = saved
+        else:
+            del sys.modules["auth_identity"]
+    return module
+
+
+def pin_dope_memory_modules(monkeypatch) -> None:
+    """For one test, resolve ``auth_identity`` and its importers to dope-memory."""
+    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "auth_identity", load_dope_memory_auth_identity())
+    for name in AUTH_IDENTITY_IMPORTERS:
+        monkeypatch.delitem(sys.modules, name, raising=False)
+
+
+def fake_token(label: str = "codex") -> str:
+    """An obviously fake bearer token, generated per call."""
+    return f"FAKE-TEST-TOKEN-{label}-{secrets.token_hex(12)}"
+
+
+def write_hash_file(auth_dir: Path, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> Path:
+    """Write ``{sha256(token): principal}`` the way the W03 helper will."""
+    auth_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    path = auth_dir / HASH_FILE_NAME
+    payload = {
+        hashlib.sha256(token.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(): principal
+        for token, principal in mapping.items()
+    }
+    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload), encoding="utf-8")
+    return path

+"""auth_identity: modes, bearer verification, principal, counters, id checks."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import hashlib
+import hmac
+import json
+import logging
+
+import pytest
+
+from .helpers import (
+    HASH_FILE_NAME,
+    fake_token,
+    load_dope_memory_auth_identity,
+    write_hash_file,
+)
+
+auth_identity = load_dope_memory_auth_identity()
+
+
+def _gate(mode, tmp_path, tokens=None):
+    auth_dir = tmp_path / "auth"
+    if tokens is not None:
+        write_hash_file(auth_dir, tokens)
+    return auth_identity.AuthGate(mode=mode, auth_dir=auth_dir)
+
+
+# --- mode ------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def test_should_default_to_log_mode_when_env_unset():
+    assert auth_identity.load_auth_mode({}) == "log"
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("raw,expected", [("off", "off"), ("LOG", "log"), (" Enforce ", "enforce")])
+def test_should_parse_mode_case_insensitively(raw, expected):
+    assert auth_identity.load_auth_mode({"DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE": raw}) == expected
+
+
+def test_should_reject_unknown_mode_instead_of_failing_open():
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE"):
+        auth_identity.load_auth_mode({"DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE": "permissive"})
+
+
+def test_should_reject_unknown_mode_on_gate_construction(tmp_path):
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
+        auth_identity.AuthGate(mode="nope", auth_dir=tmp_path)
+
+
+# --- bearer verification ---------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def test_should_return_principal_for_valid_bearer_token(tmp_path):
+    token = fake_token()
+    gate = _gate("log", tmp_path, {token: "runner:codex"})
+    identity = gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {token}")
+    assert identity.verified is True
+    assert identity.principal == "runner:codex"
+
+
+def test_should_accept_lowercase_bearer_scheme(tmp_path):
+    token = fake_token()
+    gate = _gate("log", tmp_path, {token: "runner:claude"})
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"bearer {token}").principal == "runner:claude"
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "header",
+    [None, "", "Bearer", "Bearer ", "Basic abc", "Token abc", "Bearer " + "x" * 5000],
+)
+def test_should_treat_missing_or_malformed_header_as_unverified(tmp_path, header):
+    gate = _gate("log", tmp_path, {fake_token(): "runner:codex"})
+    identity = gate.authenticate(header)
+    assert identity.verified is False
+    assert identity.principal == "unverified"
+
+
+def test_should_treat_wrong_token_as_unverified(tmp_path):
+    gate = _gate("log", tmp_path, {fake_token(): "runner:codex"})
+    identity = gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {fake_token('other')}")
+    assert identity.verified is False
+    assert identity.principal == "unverified"
+
+
+def test_should_compare_hashes_with_hmac_compare_digest(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    token = fake_token()
+    gate = _gate("log", tmp_path, {token: "runner:codex"})
+    calls = []
+    real = hmac.compare_digest
+
+    def spy(a, b):
+        calls.append((a, b))
+        return real(a, b)
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(hmac, "compare_digest", spy)
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {token}").verified is True
+    assert calls, "hmac.compare_digest was never used"
+    digest = hashlib.sha256(token.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
+    assert any(digest in pair for pair in calls)
+    # The raw token is never one of the compared values.
+    assert all(token not in pair for pair in calls)
+
+
+def test_should_never_store_plaintext_tokens_in_hash_file(tmp_path):
+    token = fake_token()
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path / "auth", {token: "runner:codex"})
+    text = (tmp_path / "auth" / HASH_FILE_NAME).read_text()
+    assert token not in text
+

+def test_should_report_missing_hash_file_and_fail_closed(tmp_path):

+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {fake_token()}").verified is False
+    assert gate.health_fields()["auth_hash_file"] == "missing"

+def test_should_report_unconfigured_when_no_directory_given():

+    assert gate.authenticate("Bearer abc").verified is False
+    assert gate.health_fields()["auth_hash_file"] == "unconfigured"

+def test_should_fail_closed_on_malformed_hash_file(tmp_path):

+    gate = auth_identity.AuthGate(mode="enforce", auth_dir=auth_dir)
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {fake_token()}").verified is False
+    assert gate.health_fields()["auth_hash_file"] == "invalid"

+def test_should_skip_entries_with_bad_hash_or_principal(tmp_path):
+    token = fake_token()
+    good = hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).hexdigest()
+    auth_dir = tmp_path / "auth"
+    auth_dir.mkdir()
+    (auth_dir / HASH_FILE_NAME).write_text(
+        json.dumps(
+            {
+                good: "runner:codex",
+                "not-a-hash": "runner:evil",
+                hashlib.sha256(b"x").hexdigest(): "root",
+                hashlib.sha256(b"y").hexdigest(): 7,
+            }
+        ),
+        encoding="utf-8",
+    )
+    gate = auth_identity.AuthGate(mode="log", auth_dir=auth_dir)
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {token}").principal == "runner:codex"
+    assert gate.authenticate("Bearer x").verified is False
+    assert gate.authenticate("Bearer y").verified is False
+
+
+def test_should_pick_up_rotated_hash_file_without_restart(tmp_path):
+    old, new = fake_token("old"), fake_token("new")
+    gate = _gate("log", tmp_path, {old: "runner:codex"})
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {old}").verified is True
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path / "auth", {new: "runner:codex", fake_token("pad"): "internal:cli"})
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {old}").verified is False
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {new}").verified is True
+
+
+def test_should_read_hash_file_from_env_directory(tmp_path):
+    token = fake_token()
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path / "auth", {token: "runner:codex"})
+    gate = auth_identity.AuthGate.from_env(
+        {"DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE": "enforce", "DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_DIR": str(tmp_path / "auth")}
+    )
+    assert gate.mode == "enforce"
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {token}").principal == "runner:codex"

+# --- authorize_write -------------------------------------------------------

+@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["off", "log", "enforce"])
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", ["valid", "invalid", "missing"])
+def test_should_apply_each_mode_to_valid_invalid_and_missing_tokens(tmp_path, mode, kind):
+    token = fake_token()
+    gate = _gate(mode, tmp_path, {token: "runner:codex"})
+    header = {
+        "valid": f"Bearer {token}",
+        "invalid": f"Bearer {fake_token('bad')}",
+        "missing": None,
+    }[kind]
+
+    if mode == "enforce" and kind != "valid":
+        with pytest.raises(auth_identity.Unauthorized):
+            gate.authorize_write(header, "curl/8")
+        return
+
+    identity = gate.authorize_write(header, "curl/8")
+    if mode == "off":
+        assert identity.principal == "unverified"  # off never verifies
+    elif kind == "valid":
+        assert identity.principal == "runner:codex" and identity.verified
+    else:
+        assert identity.principal == "unverified" and not identity.verified
+
+
+def test_should_count_unverified_calls_per_caller_in_log_and_enforce(tmp_path):
+    log_gate = _gate("log", tmp_path, {fake_token(): "runner:codex"})
+    log_gate.authorize_write(None, "curl/8")
+    log_gate.authorize_write("Bearer nope", "curl/8")
+    log_gate.authorize_write(None, "python-httpx")
+    assert log_gate.health_fields()["auth_unverified_calls"] == {"curl/8": 2, "python-httpx": 1}
+
+    enf_gate = _gate("enforce", tmp_path / "e", {fake_token(): "runner:codex"})
+    with pytest.raises(auth_identity.Unauthorized):
+        enf_gate.authorize_write(None, "curl/8")
+    assert enf_gate.health_fields()["auth_unverified_calls"] == {"curl/8": 1}
+
+
+def test_should_not_count_verified_or_off_mode_calls(tmp_path):
+    token = fake_token()
+    gate = _gate("log", tmp_path, {token: "runner:codex"})
+    gate.authorize_write(f"Bearer {token}", "curl/8")
+    assert gate.health_fields()["auth_unverified_calls"] == {}
+    off = _gate("off", tmp_path / "o", {token: "runner:codex"})
+    off.authorize_write(None, "curl/8")
+    assert off.health_fields()["auth_unverified_calls"] == {}
+
+
+def test_should_bound_the_number_of_tracked_callers(tmp_path):

+    counts = gate.health_fields()["auth_unverified_calls"]
+    assert len(counts) <= auth_identity.MAX_TRACKED_CALLERS + 1
+    assert counts["other"] == 20

+
+def test_should_not_log_the_authorization_header_or_token(tmp_path, caplog):
+    token = fake_token("leak")

+            gate.authorize_write(f"Bearer {token}", "curl/8")
+    assert token not in caplog.text

+    assert token not in str(excinfo.value)

+
+def test_should_describe_health_fields(tmp_path):

+    assert fields["auth_mode"] == "enforce"
+    assert fields["identity_required_writes"] is True

+
+def test_should_sanitize_caller_labels():

+"""Ledger principal column: migration up/down, store API, older readers,
+capture_client compatibility, eventbus_consumer self-asserted principal."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import re
+import sqlite3
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from chronicle import sqlite_migrations  # noqa: E402
+
+from chronicle.store import ChronicleStore  # noqa: E402
+
+from .helpers import SERVICE_DIR  # noqa: E402
+
+MIGRATION = SERVICE_DIR / "chronicle" / "migrations" / "v1_3_0_add_write_principal.sql"
+SCHEMA = SERVICE_DIR / "chronicle" / "schema.sql"
+TABLES = ("raw_activity_events", "work_log_entries", "issue_links", "reflection_cards")
+COLUMNS = ("write_principal", "write_principal_self_asserted")
+NOW = "2026-10-31T00:00:00+00:00"
+
+
+def _columns(conn, table):
+    return {row[1] for row in conn.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})")}
+
+
+def _make_pre_migration_ledger(path: Path) -> None:
+    """Build a ledger as it looked at v1.2.1 (no principal columns)."""
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path))
+    conn.executescript(SCHEMA.read_text())
+    for table in TABLES:
+        for column in COLUMNS:
+            conn.execute(f"ALTER TABLE {table} DROP COLUMN {column}")
+    conn.execute("DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 'v1.3.0'")
+    conn.commit()
+    conn.close()
+
+
+def _old_style_insert(conn, entry_id="old-1"):
+    """INSERT the way pre-migration writers (capture_client.py) do: no principal."""
+    conn.execute(
+        """
+        INSERT INTO raw_activity_events (
+            id, workspace_id, instance_id, session_id, ts_utc, event_type, source,
+            payload_json, redaction_level, ttl_days, created_at_utc
+        ) VALUES (?, 'ws', 'i', NULL, ?, 'shell.command', 'cli', '{}', 'strict', 7, ?)
+        """,
+        (entry_id, NOW, NOW),
+    )
+    conn.commit()
+
+
+def _apply(conn):
+    return sqlite_migrations.apply_chronicle_migrations(
+        conn, schema_path=SCHEMA, migrations_dir=MIGRATION.parent
+    )
+
+
+# --- migration -----------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def test_should_ship_migration_file_with_documented_rollback():
+    text = MIGRATION.read_text()
+    assert re.search(r"(?im)^--\s*ROLLBACK:", text), "rollback statement missing from header"
+    assert "v1.3.0" in text
+    for table in TABLES:
+        assert f"ALTER TABLE {table} ADD COLUMN write_principal" in text
+
+
+def test_should_add_nullable_defaulted_columns_to_an_older_ledger(tmp_path):
+    ledger = tmp_path / "old.sqlite"
+    _make_pre_migration_ledger(ledger)
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(ledger))
+    _old_style_insert(conn)
+    assert "write_principal" not in _columns(conn, "raw_activity_events")
+
+    applied = _apply(conn)
+
+    assert "v1_3_0_add_write_principal.sql" in applied
+    for table in TABLES:
+        assert set(COLUMNS) <= _columns(conn, table)
+    row = conn.execute(
+        "SELECT write_principal, write_principal_self_asserted FROM raw_activity_events"
+    ).fetchone()
+    assert tuple(row) == ("unverified", 0)  # pre-existing rows read as unverified
+    info = {r[1]: r for r in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(raw_activity_events)")}
+    assert info["write_principal"][4] == "'unverified'"  # default
+    assert info["write_principal"][3] == 0  # nullable (notnull flag)
+    conn.close()
+
+
+def test_should_roll_back_with_the_documented_statements_and_reapply(tmp_path):
+    ledger = tmp_path / "old.sqlite"
+    _make_pre_migration_ledger(ledger)
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(ledger))
+    _old_style_insert(conn)
+    _apply(conn)
+    conn.execute(
+        "UPDATE raw_activity_events SET write_principal = 'runner:codex' WHERE id = 'old-1'"
+    )
+    conn.commit()
+
+    statements = [
+        m.group(1).strip()
+        for m in re.finditer(r"(?im)^--\s*ROLLBACK:\s*(.+)$", MIGRATION.read_text())
+    ]
+    assert statements
+    for statement in statements:
+        conn.execute(statement)
+    conn.commit()
+
+    for table in TABLES:
+        assert not (set(COLUMNS) & _columns(conn, table))
+    # rows keep their data
+    assert conn.execute("SELECT id FROM raw_activity_events").fetchone()[0] == "old-1"
+    assert conn.execute("SELECT version FROM schema_migrations WHERE version='v1.3.0'").fetchone() is None
+
+    _apply(conn)  # re-apply after rollback works
+    for table in TABLES:
+        assert set(COLUMNS) <= _columns(conn, table)
+    conn.close()
+
+
+def test_should_boot_blank_ledger_with_columns_and_record_version(tmp_path):
+    store = ChronicleStore(tmp_path / "fresh.sqlite")
+    store.initialize_schema()
+    conn = store.connect()
+    for table in TABLES:
+        assert set(COLUMNS) <= _columns(conn, table)
+    versions = {r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT version FROM schema_migrations")}
+    assert "v1.3.0" in versions
+    store.initialize_schema()  # second init is a no-op, not a duplicate-column error
+    store.close()
+
+
+def test_should_not_fail_when_columns_already_exist_but_version_unrecorded(tmp_path):
+    """First-use race / bootstrap path: another process added the columns."""
+    ledger = tmp_path / "raced.sqlite"
+    _make_pre_migration_ledger(ledger)
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(ledger))
+    conn.execute("ALTER TABLE raw_activity_events ADD COLUMN write_principal TEXT DEFAULT 'unverified'")
+    conn.commit()
+    _apply(conn)
+    for table in TABLES:
+        assert set(COLUMNS) <= _columns(conn, table)
+    conn.close()
+

+def test_should_skip_tables_a_legacy_ledger_does_not_have_yet(tmp_path):
+    """A v1.0.0-era ledger has no raw_activity_events/issue_links table."""
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(tmp_path / "legacy.sqlite"))
+    conn.executescript(
+        """
+        CREATE TABLE work_log_entries (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+          instance_id TEXT NOT NULL, summary TEXT NOT NULL, created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL);
+        CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (version TEXT PRIMARY KEY, applied_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL);
+        INSERT INTO schema_migrations VALUES ('v1.2.1', datetime('now'));
+        """
+    )
+    _apply(conn)
+    assert set(COLUMNS) <= _columns(conn, "work_log_entries")
+    assert not _columns(conn, "raw_activity_events")
+    conn.close()

+def test_should_keep_older_readers_and_writers_working_on_a_migrated_ledger(tmp_path):
+    ledger = tmp_path / "old.sqlite"
+    _make_pre_migration_ledger(ledger)
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(ledger))
+    _old_style_insert(conn, "before")
+    _apply(conn)
+    _old_style_insert(conn, "after")  # old INSERT without the new columns
+    # old reader: explicit column list, as pre-migration code uses
+    rows = conn.execute(
+        "SELECT id, workspace_id, instance_id, event_type, payload_json "
+        "FROM raw_activity_events ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+    assert [r[0] for r in rows] == ["after", "before"]
+    principals = [r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT write_principal FROM raw_activity_events")]
+    assert principals == ["unverified", "unverified"]
+    conn.close()

+def test_should_let_capture_client_write_to_a_migrated_ledger(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    from dopemux.memory.capture_client import emit_capture_event
+
+    ledger = tmp_path / "chronicle.sqlite"
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_CAPTURE_LEDGER_PATH", str(ledger))
+    repo_root = SERVICE_DIR.parents[1]
+    # An older ledger that the capture client migrates on first use.
+    _make_pre_migration_ledger(ledger)
+    result = emit_capture_event(
+        {"event_type": "shell.command", "payload": {"command": "echo hi"}},
+        mode="cli",
+        repo_root=repo_root,
+    )
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(result.ledger_path))
+    try:
+        assert set(COLUMNS) <= _columns(conn, "raw_activity_events")
+        row = conn.execute(
+            "SELECT write_principal, write_principal_self_asserted"
+            " FROM raw_activity_events WHERE id = ?",
+            (result.event_id,),
+        ).fetchone()
+    finally:
+        conn.close()
+    # W06: the capture client records its self-asserted principal.
+    assert tuple(row) == ("internal:cli", 1)

+def _insert_entry(store, **kwargs):
+    return store.insert_work_log_entry(
+        workspace_id="ws",
+        instance_id="i",
+        category="implementation",
+        entry_type="decision",
+        summary="s",
+        source_event_id=f"manual-{kwargs.pop('n', 1)}",
+        source_event_type="manual.memory_store",
+        source_adapter="mcp-server",
+        source_event_ts_utc=NOW,
+        promotion_rule="manual",
+        **kwargs,
+    )
+
+
+def _row(store, table):
+    return tuple(
+        store.connect().execute(
+            f"SELECT write_principal, write_principal_self_asserted FROM {table}"
+        ).fetchone()
+    )
+

+def test_should_default_to_unverified_when_no_principal_is_given(store):

+def test_should_store_self_asserted_marker(store):

+    )

+def test_should_store_principal_on_issue_links_and_reflections_and_corrections(store):
+    a = _insert_entry(store, n=1)
+    b = _insert_entry(store, n=2)
+    store.insert_issue_link("ws", "i", a, b, write_principal="runner:codex")
+    assert _row(store, "issue_links") == ("runner:codex", 0)

+    store.insert_corrected_work_log_entry(
+        "ws", "i", b, "update", "fixed", write_principal="runner:codex"

+            "suggested_next": [],
+        },
+        write_principal="runner:codex",
+    )
+    assert _row(store, "reflection_cards") == ("runner:codex", 0)

+            "data": {"decision_id": "d", "title": "T", "choice": "C", "rationale": "R"},
+        }
+    )
+    assert entry is not None
+    store.insert_promoted_entry(
+        "ws", "i", entry, write_principal="internal:wma", principal_self_asserted=True
+    )
+    assert _row(store, "work_log_entries") == ("internal:wma", 1)

+async def test_should_record_internal_wma_principal_with_self_asserted_marker(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    pytest.importorskip("redis")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_CAPTURE_LEDGER_PATH", str(tmp_path / "bus.sqlite"))
+    from eventbus_consumer import EventBusConsumer
+
+    consumer = EventBusConsumer(redis_url="redis://localhost:6379")
+    msg = {
+        b"id": b"event-1",
+        b"ts": b"2026-02-02T10:00:00Z",
+        b"workspace_id": b"ws",
+        b"instance_id": b"i",
+        b"type": b"decision.logged",
+        b"source": b"dopequery",
+        b"data": json.dumps(
+            {"decision_id": "d-1", "title": "Use SQLite", "choice": "SQLite", "rationale": "simple"}
+        ).encode(),
+    }
+    await consumer._process_message(b"m-1", msg)
+
+    conn = consumer._get_store("ws").connect()
+    raw = conn.execute(
+        "SELECT write_principal, write_principal_self_asserted FROM raw_activity_events"
+    ).fetchall()
+    promoted = conn.execute(
+        "SELECT write_principal, write_principal_self_asserted FROM work_log_entries"
+    ).fetchall()
+    assert raw and promoted
+    assert {tuple(r) for r in raw + promoted} == {("internal:wma", 1)}

+"""MCP surface: omitted ids, binding, auth (in-process and over HTTP)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import sqlite3
+
+import httpx
+import pytest
+
+from .helpers import fake_token, load_dope_memory_auth_identity, write_hash_file
+
+pytest.importorskip("fastmcp")
+auth_identity = load_dope_memory_auth_identity()
+
+from fastmcp import Client  # noqa: E402
+from fastmcp.client.transports import StreamableHttpTransport  # noqa: E402
+from fastmcp.exceptions import ToolError  # noqa: E402
+from starlette.applications import Starlette  # noqa: E402
+from starlette.routing import Mount  # noqa: E402
+
+import mcp_http  # noqa: E402
+
+BOUND = "ws-bound"
+IDS = {"workspace_id": BOUND, "instance_id": "inst-1"}
+
+WRITE_ARGS = {
+    "memory_store": {"category": "implementation", "entry_type": "decision", "summary": "s"},
+    "memory_correct": {"entry_id": "missing", "correction_type": "summary"},
+    "memory_link_resolution": {"issue_entry_id": "a", "resolution_entry_id": "b"},
+    "memory_generate_reflection": {},
+    "memory_mark_issue": {"issue_entry_id": "a", "description": "d"},
+}
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def backend(ledger_path):
+    from dope_memory_main import DopeMemoryMCPServer
+
+    return DopeMemoryMCPServer(workspace_id=BOUND, instance_id="A")
+
+
+def _server(backend, mode="log", tokens=None, tmp_path=None, bound=BOUND):
+    auth_dir = tmp_path / "auth" if tmp_path else None
+    if tokens is not None:
+        write_hash_file(auth_dir, tokens)
+    gate = auth_identity.AuthGate(mode=mode, auth_dir=auth_dir)
+    server = mcp_http.build_mcp_server(
+        lambda: backend,
+        bound_workspace_id=bound,
+        read_default_instance_id="A",
+        gate=gate,
+    )
+    return server, gate
+
+
+def _principals(ledger_path):
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(ledger_path))
+    try:
+        return [r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT write_principal FROM work_log_entries")]
+    finally:
+        conn.close()
+
+
+# --- in-process (no HTTP request) -------------------------------------------
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize("missing", ["workspace_id", "instance_id"])
+async def test_should_fill_a_tool_call_missing_an_id_from_the_binding(backend, ledger_path, missing):
+    # Amendment 04 (W12): an omitted id is filled from the server's binding.
+    server, _ = _server(backend)
+    args = {**WRITE_ARGS["memory_store"], **{k: v for k, v in IDS.items() if k != missing}}
+    async with Client(server) as client:
+        stored = await client.call_tool("memory_store", args)
+    assert stored.data["created"] is True
+    assert _principals(ledger_path) == ["unverified"]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("tool", sorted(WRITE_ARGS))
+async def test_should_reject_a_binding_mismatch(backend, tool):
+    server, _ = _server(backend)
+    async with Client(server) as client:
+        with pytest.raises(ToolError, match="bound"):
+            await client.call_tool(
+                tool, {**WRITE_ARGS[tool], "workspace_id": "other", "instance_id": "i"}
+            )

+async def test_should_reject_writes_when_server_unbound(backend):

+async def test_should_advertise_ids_as_optional_on_every_tool(backend):
+    server, _ = _server(backend)
+    async with Client(server) as client:

+    for name in WRITE_ARGS:
+        required = set(tools[name].inputSchema.get("required", []))
+        assert not ({"workspace_id", "instance_id"} & required), name
+    for name in ("memory_search", "memory_recap", "memory_trajectory", "memory_reflections"):
+        required = set(tools[name].inputSchema.get("required", []))
+        assert not ({"workspace_id", "instance_id"} & required), name

+async def test_should_keep_reads_working_without_ids(backend):

+        assert (await client.call_tool("memory_search", {"query": "x"})).data["items"] == []
+        await client.call_tool("memory_recap", {})
+        await client.call_tool("memory_trajectory", {})

+async def test_should_round_trip_write_with_unverified_principal_in_process(backend, ledger_path):
+    server, gate = _server(backend, mode="log")
+    async with Client(server) as client:
+        stored = await client.call_tool("memory_store", {**WRITE_ARGS["memory_store"], **IDS})
+        assert stored.data["created"] is True
+        found = await client.call_tool("memory_search", {"query": "s", **IDS})
+        assert [i["summary"] for i in found.data["items"]] == ["s"]
+    assert _principals(ledger_path) == ["unverified"]

+async def test_should_refuse_unauthenticated_in_process_write_in_enforce(backend, ledger_path):

+            await client.call_tool("memory_store", {**WRITE_ARGS["memory_store"], **IDS})
+    assert not ledger_path.exists() or _principals(ledger_path) == []

+async def test_should_unwrap_dict_shaped_backend_results():
+    class DictBackend:
+        def memory_trajectory(self, workspace_id, instance_id):
+            return {"success": True, "current_stream": "idle", "boost_factor": 1.0}
+
+        def memory_recap(self, **kwargs):
+            return {"success": False, "error": "dict backend failure"}
+
+    server = mcp_http.build_mcp_server(
+        lambda: DictBackend(),
+        bound_workspace_id="x",
+        read_default_instance_id="A",
+        gate=auth_identity.AuthGate(mode="log", auth_dir=None),
+    )
+    async with Client(server) as client:
+        ok = await client.call_tool("memory_trajectory", {})
+        assert ok.data["current_stream"] == "idle"
+        with pytest.raises(ToolError, match="dict backend failure"):
+            await client.call_tool("memory_recap", {})

+# --- over HTTP ---------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _http_app(server, gate):
+    mcp_app = mcp_http.build_mcp_http_app(server)
+    app = Starlette(routes=[Mount("", mcp_app)], lifespan=mcp_app.lifespan)
+    app.add_middleware(auth_identity.WriteAuthMiddleware, get_gate=lambda: gate)
+    return app
+
+
+def _client(app, token=None, user_agent="w01-test"):
+    headers = {"User-Agent": user_agent}
+    if token:
+        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {token}"
+
+    def factory(headers=None, timeout=None, auth=None, **kwargs):
+        return httpx.AsyncClient(
+            transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app),
+            headers=headers,
+            timeout=timeout,
+            auth=auth,
+            follow_redirects=True,
+        )
+
+    return Client(
+        StreamableHttpTransport("http://test/mcp", headers=headers, httpx_client_factory=factory)
+    )
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "mode,kind,principal",
+    [
+        ("log", "valid", "runner:codex"),
+        ("log", "invalid", "unverified"),
+        ("log", "missing", "unverified"),
+        ("enforce", "valid", "runner:codex"),
+    ],
+)
+async def test_should_store_verified_principal_over_http(
+    backend, ledger_path, tmp_path, mode, kind, principal
+):
+    token = fake_token()
+    server, gate = _server(backend, mode, {token: "runner:codex"}, tmp_path)
+    app = _http_app(server, gate)
+    sent = {"valid": token, "invalid": fake_token("bad"), "missing": None}[kind]
+    async with app.router.lifespan_context(app):
+        async with _client(app, sent) as client:
+            stored = await client.call_tool("memory_store", {**WRITE_ARGS["memory_store"], **IDS})
+            assert stored.data["created"] is True
+    assert _principals(ledger_path) == [principal]
+
+
+async def test_should_answer_401_over_http_for_unverified_write_in_enforce(
+    backend, ledger_path, tmp_path
+):
+    token = fake_token()
+    server, gate = _server(backend, "enforce", {token: "runner:codex"}, tmp_path)
+    app = _http_app(server, gate)
+    body = {
+        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
+        "id": 1,
+        "method": "tools/call",
+        "params": {"name": "memory_store", "arguments": {**WRITE_ARGS["memory_store"], **IDS}},
+    }
+    async with app.router.lifespan_context(app):
+        async with httpx.AsyncClient(
+            transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app), base_url="http://test"
+        ) as raw:
+            headers = {"Accept": "application/json, text/event-stream", "User-Agent": "raw/1"}
+            resp = await raw.post("/mcp", json=body, headers=headers)
+            assert resp.status_code == 401
+            assert resp.headers["www-authenticate"].lower().startswith("bearer")
+            bad = await raw.post(
+                "/mcp", json=body, headers={**headers, "Authorization": f"Bearer {fake_token('bad')}"}
+            )
+            assert bad.status_code == 401
+            # A batch containing one write is rejected as a whole.
+            batch = await raw.post("/mcp", json=[{**body, "method": "tools/list"}, body], headers=headers)
+            assert batch.status_code == 401
+            # Non-write JSON-RPC traffic is not gated by the middleware.
+            listing = await raw.post(
+                "/mcp", json={"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 2, "method": "tools/list"}, headers=headers
+            )
+            assert listing.status_code != 401
+    assert gate.health_fields()["auth_unverified_calls"]["raw/1"] == 3
+    assert not ledger_path.exists() or _principals(ledger_path) == []
+
+
+async def test_should_allow_reads_over_http_without_token_in_enforce(backend, tmp_path):
+    server, gate = _server(backend, "enforce", {fake_token(): "runner:codex"}, tmp_path)
+    app = _http_app(server, gate)
+    async with app.router.lifespan_context(app):
+        async with _client(app) as client:
+            found = await client.call_tool("memory_search", {"query": "none"})
+            assert found.data["items"] == []

+# --- middleware mechanics ------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+async def _run_asgi(app, method, path, body: bytes, headers=None):
+    sent: list[dict] = []
+    received = [{"type": "http.request", "body": body, "more_body": False}]
+
+    async def receive():
+        if received:
+            return received.pop(0)
+        return {"type": "http.disconnect"}
+
+    async def send(message):
+        sent.append(message)
+
+    scope = {
+        "type": "http",
+        "method": method,
+        "path": path,
+        "headers": [(k.lower().encode(), v.encode()) for k, v in (headers or {}).items()],
+        "client": ("10.1.2.3", 5555),
+    }
+    await app(scope, receive, send)
+    status = next(m["status"] for m in sent if m["type"] == "http.response.start")
+    return status, sent
+
+
+def _echo_app():
+    seen = {}
+
+    async def app(scope, receive, send):
+        chunks = []
+        while True:
+            message = await receive()
+            chunks.append(message.get("body", b""))
+            if not message.get("more_body"):
+                break
+        seen["body"] = b"".join(chunks)
+        await send({"type": "http.response.start", "status": 200, "headers": []})
+        await send({"type": "http.response.body", "body": b"ok"})
+
+    return app, seen
+
+
+async def test_should_replay_request_body_to_inner_app_when_allowed(tmp_path):
+    inner, seen = _echo_app()
+    gate = auth_identity.AuthGate(mode="log", auth_dir=None)
+    app = auth_identity.WriteAuthMiddleware(inner, get_gate=lambda: gate)
+    body = json.dumps(
+        {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": "tools/call", "params": {"name": "memory_store"}}
+    ).encode()
+    status, _ = await _run_asgi(app, "POST", "/mcp", body)
+    assert status == 200
+    assert seen["body"] == body
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("body", [b"not json", b"123", b'"str"', b"[]", b"{}"])
+async def test_should_pass_through_unparseable_or_non_call_bodies(body):
+    inner, seen = _echo_app()
+    gate = auth_identity.AuthGate(mode="enforce", auth_dir=None)
+    app = auth_identity.WriteAuthMiddleware(inner, get_gate=lambda: gate)
+    status, _ = await _run_asgi(app, "POST", "/mcp", body)
+    assert status == 200 and seen["body"] == body
+
+
+async def test_should_not_gate_other_paths_or_methods():
+    inner, _ = _echo_app()
+    gate = auth_identity.AuthGate(mode="enforce", auth_dir=None)
+    app = auth_identity.WriteAuthMiddleware(inner, get_gate=lambda: gate)
+    body = json.dumps({"method": "tools/call", "params": {"name": "memory_store"}}).encode()
+    assert (await _run_asgi(app, "GET", "/mcp", b""))[0] == 200
+    assert (await _run_asgi(app, "POST", "/health", body))[0] == 200
+
+
+async def test_should_reject_oversized_mcp_bodies_with_413():
+    inner, _ = _echo_app()
+    gate = auth_identity.AuthGate(mode="enforce", auth_dir=None)
+    app = auth_identity.WriteAuthMiddleware(inner, get_gate=lambda: gate, max_body_bytes=64)
+    status, _ = await _run_asgi(app, "POST", "/mcp", b"x" * 65)
+    assert status == 413

+"""REST write routes: omitted ids, workspace binding, auth modes, principal, health."""

+from __future__ import annotations

+import logging

+# Minimal valid bodies (without the identity fields) for every mutating route.
+WRITE_ROUTES = {
+    "memory_store": {"category": "implementation", "entry_type": "decision", "summary": "s"},
+    "memory_correct": {"entry_id": "missing", "correction_type": "summary"},
+    "memory_link_resolution": {"issue_entry_id": "a", "resolution_entry_id": "b"},
+    "memory_generate_reflection": {},
+    "memory_mark_issue": {"issue_entry_id": "a", "description": "d"},

+@pytest.fixture()
+def make_client(monkeypatch, ledger_path, tmp_path):
+    import dope_memory_main as dm
+
+    clients = []
+
+    def factory(mode="log", tokens=None, bound=BOUND):
+        auth_dir = tmp_path / "auth"
+        if tokens is not None:
+            write_hash_file(auth_dir, tokens)
+        gate = auth_identity.AuthGate(mode=mode, auth_dir=auth_dir)
+        monkeypatch.setattr(dm, "AUTH_GATE", gate)
+        monkeypatch.setattr(dm, "BOUND_WORKSPACE_ID", bound)
+        monkeypatch.setattr(
+            dm, "mcp_server", dm.DopeMemoryMCPServer(workspace_id=bound or "x", instance_id="A")
+        )
+        client = httpx.AsyncClient(
+            transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=dm.app), base_url="http://test"
+        )
+        clients.append(client)
+        return client, gate
+
+    yield factory
+
+
+def _principals(ledger_path) -> list[str]:
+    if not ledger_path.exists():
+        return []
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(ledger_path))
+    try:
+        return [r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT write_principal FROM work_log_entries")]
+    finally:
+        conn.close()
+
+
+def _body(route, **ids):
+    return {**WRITE_ROUTES[route], **ids}
+
+
+IDS = {"workspace_id": BOUND, "instance_id": "inst-1"}
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("route", sorted(WRITE_ROUTES))
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("missing", ["workspace_id", "instance_id"])
+async def test_should_not_reject_write_without_an_id_schema_wise(make_client, route, missing):
+    # Amendment 04 (W12): an omitted id is filled from the binding, so the request
+    # model no longer answers 422 for it. (Fill behaviour: tests/unit/runner_memory_2a_r2_w12.)
+    client, _ = make_client()
+    ids = {k: v for k, v in IDS.items() if k != missing}
+    resp = await client.post(f"/tools/{route}", json=_body(route, **ids))
+    assert resp.status_code != 422
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize("omitted", [("workspace_id",), ("instance_id",), ("workspace_id", "instance_id")])
+async def test_should_store_a_write_with_omitted_ids_under_the_binding(make_client, ledger_path, omitted):
+    client, _ = make_client()
+    ids = {k: v for k, v in IDS.items() if k not in omitted}
+    resp = await client.post("/tools/memory_store", json=_body("memory_store", **ids))
+    assert resp.status_code == 200, resp.text
+    assert resp.json()["created"] is True
+    assert _principals(ledger_path) == ["unverified"]

+    assert "workspace_id" not in resp.text or "required" not in resp.text

+    token = fake_token()

+        "missing": {},
+    }[kind]
+    resp = await client.post("/tools/memory_store", json=_body("memory_store", **IDS), headers=headers)
+    assert resp.status_code == status, resp.text
+    if status == 401:
+        assert resp.headers["www-authenticate"].lower().startswith("bearer")
+        assert _principals(ledger_path) == []
+    else:
+        assert _principals(ledger_path) == [principal]

+"""docker_runtime: the generated compose override carries the dope-memory auth
+mode and a read-only auth-directory mount (dope-memory only)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from dopemux.mcp import docker_runtime as dr
+
+
+def _override(**extra):
+    labels = dr.build_labels(
+        project_id="dnh",
+        workspace_id="/w",
+        project_root="/w",
+        worktree_root="/w",
+        project_hash="h",
+        worktree_hash="8d6d",
+        instance_id="dnh-8d6d-x",
+        service="x",
+        scope="worktree",
+        transport="http",
+    )
+    return dr.generate_compose_override(
+        services=["conport", "dope-memory"],
+        identity={
+            "DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID": "8d6d",
+            "DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID": "/w",
+            "DOPE_MEMORY_WORKSPACE_ID": "w",
+            "DOPE_MEMORY_INSTANCE_ID": "8d6d",
+        },
+        ports={"CONPORT_HTTP_PORT": 3040, "CONPORT_MCP_PORT": 3041,
+               "CONPORT_INFO_PORT": 4040, "DOPE_MEMORY_PORT": 3060},
+        container_names={"conport": "c-conport", "dope-memory": "c-mem"},
+        labels_by_service={"conport": labels, "dope-memory": labels},
+        memory_data_path=Path("/tmp/mem-data"),
+        **extra,
+    )
+
+
+def _service_block(text: str, name: str) -> str:
+    start = text.index(f"  {name}:\n")
+    rest = text[start + 1 :]
+    nxt = [i for i in (rest.find("\n  conport:\n"), rest.find("\n  dope-memory:\n"), rest.find("\nnetworks:")) if i > 0]
+    return text[start : start + 1 + (min(nxt) if nxt else len(rest))]
+
+
+def test_should_render_auth_mode_and_readonly_directory_mount(tmp_path):
+    auth_dir = tmp_path / "auth"
+    auth_dir.mkdir()
+    block = _service_block(_override(auth_mode="enforce", auth_dir=auth_dir), "dope-memory")
+    assert 'DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE: "enforce"' in block
+    assert 'DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_DIR: "/run/dopemux-auth"' in block
+    assert f'- "{auth_dir.resolve()}:/run/dopemux-auth:ro"' in block
+    assert ":/data" in block  # the data mount is preserved
+
+
+def test_should_default_to_log_mode(tmp_path):
+    auth_dir = tmp_path / "auth"
+    auth_dir.mkdir()
+    block = _service_block(_override(auth_dir=auth_dir), "dope-memory")
+    assert 'DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE: "log"' in block
+
+
+def test_should_read_mode_from_environment_when_not_passed(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE", "enforce")
+    block = _service_block(_override(auth_dir=tmp_path), "dope-memory")
+    assert 'DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE: "enforce"' in block
+
+
+def test_should_not_render_mount_for_a_missing_directory(tmp_path):
+    block = _service_block(_override(auth_mode="log", auth_dir=tmp_path / "nope"), "dope-memory")
+    assert "dopemux-auth" not in block
+    assert 'DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE: "log"' in block  # mode still travels
+
+
+def test_should_keep_dope_memory_auth_settings_out_of_the_conport_service(tmp_path):
+    # W02 (B06) gives ConPort its own CONPORT_AUTH_* env and mount; the
+    # dope-memory settings must stay in the dope-memory block only.
+    auth_dir = tmp_path / "auth"
+    auth_dir.mkdir()
+    text = _override(auth_mode="enforce", auth_dir=auth_dir)
+    conport = _service_block(text, "conport")
+    memory = _service_block(text, "dope-memory")
+    assert "DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH" not in conport
+    assert conport.count("dopemux-auth:ro") == 1
+    assert memory.count("dopemux-auth:ro") == 1
+
+
+def test_should_reject_an_unknown_mode():
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="auth mode"):
+        _override(auth_mode="permissive")
+
+
+def test_should_keep_dropping_token_and_password_keys_from_generated_env():
+    text = dr.generate_mcp_env(
+        {"DOPE_MEMORY_PORT": 3060, "SOME_TOKEN": "x", "DB_PASSWORD": "y", "DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE": "log"}
+    )
+    assert "SOME_TOKEN" not in text and "DB_PASSWORD" not in text
+    assert "DOPE_MEMORY_PORT=3060" in text

+"""Shared fixtures for the Wave 2A R2 W02 (ConPort identity) tests.
+
+The ConPort image copies its modules flat into ``/app`` (``server``,
+``enhanced_server``, ``auth_identity`` ...), so they are top-level imports.  The
+loader below imports them from the ConPort source directory under a temporary
+``sys.path`` entry and then restores any same-named modules that other tests
+(for example dope-memory's own ``auth_identity``) already placed in
+``sys.modules``.  The returned module objects keep working after the restore.
+
+``enhanced_server`` falls back to ``pip install`` when ``asyncpg`` is missing,
+so the loader installs a stub ``asyncpg`` for the import when the real one is
+absent (the tests never open a database connection).
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import contextlib
+import importlib
+import sys
+import types
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from typing import Any, Callable, Optional
+
+import pytest
+
+from .helpers import BOUND_WORKSPACE, CONPORT_DIR, INSTANCE, write_hash_file
+
+_FLAT_MODULES = (
+    "auth_identity",
+    "instance_detector",
+    "enhanced_server",
+    "server",
+    "conport_mcp_stdio",
+    "integration_bridge_client",
+    "unified_queries",
+)
+
+
+@contextlib.contextmanager
+def conport_import_scope():
+    saved = {name: sys.modules.pop(name, None) for name in _FLAT_MODULES}
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(CONPORT_DIR))
+    try:
+        yield
+    finally:
+        try:
+            sys.path.remove(str(CONPORT_DIR))
+        except ValueError:
+            pass
+        for name in _FLAT_MODULES:
+            sys.modules.pop(name, None)
+            if saved[name] is not None:
+                sys.modules[name] = saved[name]
+
+
+def load_conport_module(name: str, monkeypatch: Optional[pytest.MonkeyPatch] = None):
+    """Import ``name`` from the ConPort source directory (fresh every call)."""
+    if name in ("enhanced_server", "server", "conport_mcp_stdio"):
+        pytest.importorskip("aiohttp")
+    if name == "enhanced_server":
+        pytest.importorskip("redis.asyncio")
+        try:
+            import asyncpg  # noqa: F401
+        except ImportError:
+            if monkeypatch is None:
+                raise
+            monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "asyncpg", types.ModuleType("asyncpg"))
+    if name in ("server", "conport_mcp_stdio"):
+        pytest.importorskip("mcp.server.fastmcp")
+    with conport_import_scope():
+        return importlib.import_module(name)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def auth_identity():
+    return load_conport_module("auth_identity")
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def instance_detector():
+    return load_conport_module("instance_detector")
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# A fake asyncpg pool: records every statement, returns canned rows.
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 31, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
+PRINCIPAL_TABLES = ("workspace_contexts", "decisions", "progress_entries", "custom_data")
+
+
+def _norm(sql: str) -> str:
+    return " ".join(sql.split())
+
+
+class FakeConn:
+    def __init__(self, db: "FakeDB"):
+        self.db = db
+
+    async def execute(self, sql: str, *args: Any) -> str:
+        self.db.calls.append(("execute", _norm(sql), args))
+        return "OK"
+
+    async def fetch(self, sql: str, *args: Any):
+        norm = _norm(sql)
+        self.db.calls.append(("fetch", norm, args))
+        if "information_schema.columns" in norm:
+            return [{"table_name": table} for table in self.db.principal_tables]
+        if "FROM progress_entries" in norm:
+            return [dict(self.db.progress_row)]
+        return []
+
+    async def fetchrow(self, sql: str, *args: Any):
+        norm = _norm(sql)
+        self.db.calls.append(("fetchrow", norm, args))
+        if "FROM decisions WHERE id" in norm:
+            return dict(self.db.decision_row)
+        if "FROM progress_entries WHERE id" in norm:
+            return dict(self.db.progress_row)
+        if "FROM workspace_contexts" in norm:
+            return dict(self.db.context_row)
+        if "RETURNING value" in norm:
+            return {"value": '{"_fingerprint_v1": "x"}'}
+        return None
+
+    async def fetchval(self, sql: str, *args: Any):
+        self.db.calls.append(("fetchval", _norm(sql), args))
+        return 1
+
+    @contextlib.asynccontextmanager
+    async def transaction(self):
+        yield
+
+
+class FakeDB:
+    def __init__(self, principal_tables=PRINCIPAL_TABLES, progress_workspace: str = BOUND_WORKSPACE):
+        self.calls: list[tuple[str, str, tuple]] = []
+        self.principal_tables = tuple(principal_tables)
+        self.decision_row = {
+            "id": "11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
+            "workspace_id": BOUND_WORKSPACE,
+            "summary": "s",
+            "rationale": "r",
+            "alternatives": "[]",
+            "tags": [],
+            "created_at": NOW,
+            "updated_at": NOW,
+        }
+        self.progress_row = {
+            "id": "22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222",
+            "workspace_id": progress_workspace,
+            "description": "d",
+            "status": "IN_PROGRESS",
+            "percentage": 0,
+            "linked_decision_id": None,
+            "priority": "medium",
+            "estimated_hours": None,
+            "actual_hours": None,
+            "instance_id": None,
+            "created_at": NOW,
+            "updated_at": NOW,
+            "completed_at": None,
+        }
+        self.context_row = {
+            "workspace_id": BOUND_WORKSPACE,
+            "instance_id": INSTANCE,
+            "active_context": "c",
+            "last_activity": "a",
+            "session_time": "1m",
+            "focus_state": "f",
+            "session_milestone": None,
+            "updated_at": NOW,
+        }
+
+    # -- pool interface ----------------------------------------------------
+    @contextlib.asynccontextmanager
+    async def acquire(self):
+        yield FakeConn(self)
+
+    async def fetch(self, sql: str, *args: Any):
+        return await FakeConn(self).fetch(sql, *args)
+
+    # -- inspection helpers --------------------------------------------------
+    def writes(self) -> list[tuple[str, tuple]]:
+        out = []
+        for kind, sql, args in self.calls:
+            if kind == "execute" or (kind == "fetchrow" and sql.startswith("INSERT")):
+                out.append((sql, args))
+        return out
+
+    def write_sql(self, fragment: str) -> list[tuple[str, tuple]]:
+        return [(sql, args) for sql, args in self.writes() if fragment in sql]
+
+
+class FakeRedis:
+    async def get(self, key):
+        return None
+
+    async def setex(self, *args, **kwargs):
+        return True
+
+    async def delete(self, *args, **kwargs):
+        return 1
+
+    async def ping(self):
+        return True
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def enhanced_server_module(monkeypatch):
+    return load_conport_module("enhanced_server", monkeypatch)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def make_rest_server(monkeypatch, tmp_path, enhanced_server_module):
+    """Factory: an ``EnhancedConPortServer`` wired to fakes (no network, no DB)."""
+
+    def factory(
+        mode: str = "log",
+        tokens: Optional[dict[str, str]] = None,
+        *,
+        bound_workspace: Optional[str] = BOUND_WORKSPACE,
+        principal_tables=PRINCIPAL_TABLES,
+        write_hash_dir: bool = True,
+        progress_workspace: str = BOUND_WORKSPACE,
+    ):
+        monkeypatch.setenv("CONPORT_AUTH_MODE", mode)
+        if write_hash_dir:
+            auth_dir = tmp_path / "auth"
+            write_hash_file(auth_dir, tokens or {})
+            monkeypatch.setenv("CONPORT_AUTH_DIR", str(auth_dir))
+        else:
+            monkeypatch.delenv("CONPORT_AUTH_DIR", raising=False)
+        if bound_workspace is None:
+            monkeypatch.delenv("DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID", raising=False)
+        else:
+            monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID", bound_workspace)
+        monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_AUTO_FORK_PROGRESS", "0")
+        server = enhanced_server_module.EnhancedConPortServer()
+        server.db_pool = FakeDB(principal_tables, progress_workspace)
+        server.redis = FakeRedis()
+        server.dopecon_bridge = None
+        return server
+
+    return factory
+
+
+def rest_client(server):
+    """An aiohttp ``TestClient`` for the server's app (use as ``async with``)."""
+    from aiohttp.test_utils import TestClient, TestServer
+
+    return TestClient(TestServer(server.app))

+"""Pure helpers for the Wave 2A R2 W02 (ConPort identity) tests."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import hashlib
+import json
+import secrets
+from pathlib import Path
+
+REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+CONPORT_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "docker" / "mcp-servers-source" / "conport"
+MIGRATIONS_DIR = CONPORT_DIR / "migrations"
+WMA_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "services" / "working-memory-assistant"
+HASH_FILE_NAME = "memory-token-hashes.json"
+
+BOUND_WORKSPACE = "/test/bound-workspace"
+OTHER_WORKSPACE = "/test/some-other-workspace"
+INSTANCE = "inst-a"
+
+SSE_TOOL_NAMES = frozenset(
+    {
+        "get_progress",
+        "update_progress",
+        "get_decisions",
+        "log_decision",
+        "get_recent_activity",
+        "get_active_work",
+        "workspace_summary",
+        "fork_instance",
+        "promote",
+        "promote_all",
+        "get_context",
+        "update_context",
+        "log_progress",
+        "get_custom_data",
+        "save_custom_data",
+        "delete_custom_data",
+        "search_content",
+    }
+)
+
+
+def fake_token(label: str = "codex") -> str:
+    """An obviously fake bearer token, generated per call."""
+    return f"FAKE-TEST-TOKEN-{label}-{secrets.token_hex(12)}"
+
+
+def sha256_hex(token: str) -> str:
+    return hashlib.sha256(token.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
+
+
+def write_hash_file(auth_dir: Path, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> Path:
+    """Write ``{sha256(token): principal}`` the way the W03 helper will."""
+    auth_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    path = auth_dir / HASH_FILE_NAME
+    payload = {sha256_hex(token): principal for token, principal in mapping.items()}
+    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload), encoding="utf-8")
+    return path

+def test_should_use_conport_specific_env_names_and_default_to_log(auth_identity):
+    assert auth_identity.ENV_AUTH_MODE == "CONPORT_AUTH_MODE"
+    assert auth_identity.ENV_AUTH_DIR == "CONPORT_AUTH_DIR"
+    assert auth_identity.load_auth_mode({}) == "log"
+    assert auth_identity.load_auth_mode({"CONPORT_AUTH_MODE": " Enforce "}) == "enforce"
+    # the dope-memory switch does not move ConPort
+    assert auth_identity.load_auth_mode({"DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE": "enforce"}) == "log"

+def test_should_reject_an_unknown_mode(auth_identity):
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="CONPORT_AUTH_MODE"):
+        auth_identity.load_auth_mode({"CONPORT_AUTH_MODE": "permissive"})
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="auth mode"):
+        auth_identity.AuthGate("permissive")

+# -- the shared hash-file contract (byte-compatible with dope-memory's) ---------
+
+
+def test_should_share_the_dope_memory_hash_file_contract(auth_identity):
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("wma_auth_identity_parity", WMA_DIR / "auth_identity.py")
+    wma = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    sys.modules[spec.name] = wma
+    try:
+        spec.loader.exec_module(wma)
+    finally:
+        sys.modules.pop(spec.name, None)
+    assert auth_identity.HASH_FILE_NAME == wma.HASH_FILE_NAME == HASH_FILE_NAME
+    assert auth_identity._PRINCIPAL_RE.pattern == wma._PRINCIPAL_RE.pattern
+    assert auth_identity._SHA256_HEX_RE.pattern == wma._SHA256_HEX_RE.pattern
+    assert auth_identity.UNVERIFIED_PRINCIPAL == wma.UNVERIFIED_PRINCIPAL
+    assert auth_identity.MAX_TOKEN_CHARS == wma.MAX_TOKEN_CHARS
+
+
+def test_should_verify_what_the_dope_memory_gate_verifies(auth_identity, tmp_path):
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("wma_auth_identity_parity2", WMA_DIR / "auth_identity.py")
+    wma = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    sys.modules[spec.name] = wma
+    try:
+        spec.loader.exec_module(wma)
+    finally:
+        sys.modules.pop(spec.name, None)
+    codex, hooks = fake_token("codex"), fake_token("hooks")
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path, {codex: "runner:codex", hooks: "internal:hooks"})
+    cp = auth_identity.AuthGate("log", tmp_path)
+    dm = wma.AuthGate("log", tmp_path)
+    for token in (codex, hooks, fake_token("nobody")):
+        header = f"Bearer {token}"
+        assert cp.authenticate(header).principal == dm.authenticate(header).principal
+

+def test_should_verify_a_token_against_the_hash_file(auth_identity, tmp_path):
+    token = fake_token()
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path, {token: "runner:codex"})
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "log", tmp_path)
+    identity = gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {token}")
+    assert identity.verified and identity.principal == "runner:codex"
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"bearer {token}").principal == "runner:codex"

+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "header",

+def test_should_treat_missing_or_malformed_headers_as_unverified(auth_identity, tmp_path, header):
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path, {fake_token(): "runner:codex"})
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "log", tmp_path)
+    assert gate.authenticate(header).principal == "unverified"
+    assert not gate.authenticate(header).verified

+def test_should_skip_malformed_entries_and_keep_good_ones(auth_identity, tmp_path):
+    good = fake_token()
+    payload = {
+        sha256_hex(good): "runner:codex",
+        "not-a-digest": "runner:x",
+        sha256_hex("a"): "root",
+        sha256_hex("b"): "Runner:Upper",
+        sha256_hex("c"): 7,
+    }
+    (tmp_path / HASH_FILE_NAME).write_text(json.dumps(payload), encoding="utf-8")
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "log", tmp_path)
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {good}").principal == "runner:codex"
+    assert gate.authenticate("Bearer a").principal == "unverified"
+    assert gate.health_fields()["auth_hash_file"] == "ok"
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("content", ["{not json", "[]", '"text"', ""])
+def test_should_verify_nothing_when_the_file_is_unreadable_or_not_an_object(
+    auth_identity, tmp_path, content
+):
+    (tmp_path / HASH_FILE_NAME).write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "enforce", tmp_path)
+    assert gate.authenticate("Bearer anything").principal == "unverified"
+    assert gate.health_fields()["auth_hash_file"] == "invalid"
+    with pytest.raises(auth_identity.Unauthorized):
+        gate.authorize_write("Bearer anything", "c")

+
+def test_should_report_missing_and_unconfigured_hash_files(auth_identity, tmp_path):
+    assert _gate(auth_identity, "log", tmp_path / "nope").health_fields()["auth_hash_file"] == "missing"
+    assert _gate(auth_identity, "log", None).health_fields()["auth_hash_file"] == "unconfigured"
+    with pytest.raises(auth_identity.Unauthorized):
+        _gate(auth_identity, "enforce", None).authorize_write("Bearer x", "c")

+
+def test_should_reread_the_file_when_it_changes(auth_identity, tmp_path):
+    old, new = fake_token("old"), fake_token("new")
+    path = write_hash_file(tmp_path, {old: "runner:codex"})
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "log", tmp_path)
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {old}").verified
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path, {new: "runner:claude", fake_token("pad"): "internal:hooks"})
+    os.utime(path, ns=(1, 1))  # force a different mtime even on coarse clocks
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {old}").principal == "unverified"
+    assert gate.authenticate(f"Bearer {new}").principal == "runner:claude"

+def test_should_bound_the_number_of_tracked_callers(auth_identity, tmp_path):

+    counts = gate.health_fields()["auth_unverified_calls"]

+def test_should_never_log_a_token_a_hash_or_the_header(auth_identity, tmp_path, caplog):
+    token = fake_token()
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path, {token: "runner:codex"})
+    (tmp_path / "junk").write_text("x")
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "enforce", tmp_path)
+    with caplog.at_level(logging.DEBUG):
+        gate.authorize_write(f"Bearer {token}", "ok-caller")
+        with pytest.raises(auth_identity.Unauthorized):
+            gate.authorize_write("Bearer " + token + "-wrong", "bad-caller")
+    text = caplog.text
+    assert token not in text and sha256_hex(token) not in text and "Bearer" not in text
+    assert "bad-caller" in text

+def test_should_make_a_log_safe_caller_label(auth_identity):

+    assert auth_identity.caller_label(None, "1.2.3.4") == "1.2.3.4"
+    assert auth_identity.caller_label("", None) == "unknown"
+    assert len(auth_identity.caller_label("x" * 500, None)) == auth_identity.MAX_CALLER_LABEL_CHARS

+def test_should_fill_an_omitted_workspace_id_from_the_binding(auth_identity):
+    # Amendment 04 (W12): an omitted or blank workspace_id is the server's binding.
+    for ws in (None, "", "   "):
+        assert auth_identity.validate_write_ids(ws, "i", BOUND_WORKSPACE) == (BOUND_WORKSPACE, "i")
+        assert auth_identity.validate_write_ids(
+            ws, "i", BOUND_WORKSPACE, require_instance=False) == (BOUND_WORKSPACE, "i")
+
+
+def test_should_fill_an_omitted_instance_id_only_from_a_default_the_caller_offers(auth_identity):
+    for inst in (None, "", "  "):
+        # no default offered (fork_instance): still required where the row carries one
+        with pytest.raises(auth_identity.WriteIdentityError, match="instance_id is required"):
+            auth_identity.validate_write_ids(BOUND_WORKSPACE, inst, BOUND_WORKSPACE)
+        auth_identity.validate_write_ids(BOUND_WORKSPACE, inst, BOUND_WORKSPACE, require_instance=False)
+        assert auth_identity.validate_write_ids(
+            BOUND_WORKSPACE, inst, BOUND_WORKSPACE, default_instance_id="srv") == (BOUND_WORKSPACE, "srv")
+    auth_identity.validate_write_ids(BOUND_WORKSPACE, "shared", BOUND_WORKSPACE)
+
+
+def test_should_reject_a_workspace_that_is_not_the_bound_one(auth_identity):
+    with pytest.raises(auth_identity.WriteIdentityError, match="does not match"):
+        auth_identity.validate_write_ids(OTHER_WORKSPACE, "i", BOUND_WORKSPACE)
+
+
+def test_should_fail_closed_without_a_workspace_binding(auth_identity):
+    for bound in (None, ""):
+        with pytest.raises(auth_identity.WriteIdentityError, match="no workspace binding"):
+            auth_identity.validate_write_ids(BOUND_WORKSPACE, "i", bound)

+def test_should_map_rejections_to_http_statuses(auth_identity):
+    assert auth_identity.WriteIdentityError("x").status == 400
+    assert auth_identity.PrincipalStorageUnavailable("x").status == 503
+    assert issubclass(auth_identity.WriteIdentityError, auth_identity.WriteRejected)
+    assert issubclass(auth_identity.PrincipalStorageUnavailable, auth_identity.WriteRejected)

+# -- forwarding headers for the in-image proxies ----------------------------------

+
+def test_should_forward_the_callers_bearer_token(auth_identity, tmp_path):
+    token = fake_token()
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path, {token: "runner:codex"})
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "enforce", tmp_path)
+    headers = auth_identity.build_rest_write_headers(gate, f"Bearer {token}", "c")
+    assert headers == {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}

+def test_should_use_the_proxy_principal_only_in_log_mode_without_a_caller_token(auth_identity, tmp_path):
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path, {})
+    proxy = fake_token("proxy")
+    log_gate = _gate(auth_identity, "log", tmp_path)
+    headers = auth_identity.build_rest_write_headers(log_gate, None, "curl/8", fallback_token=proxy)
+    assert headers["Authorization"] == f"Bearer {proxy}"
+    assert headers[auth_identity.UNVERIFIED_CALLER_HEADER] == "curl/8"
+    # a caller who sent a (bad) token is forwarded as-is, never replaced
+    bad = auth_identity.build_rest_write_headers(log_gate, "Bearer nope", "curl/8", fallback_token=proxy)
+    assert bad["Authorization"] == "Bearer nope"
+    # no fallback token available (W03 helper not landed): nothing is invented
+    none = auth_identity.build_rest_write_headers(log_gate, None, "curl/8")
+    assert "Authorization" not in none and none[auth_identity.UNVERIFIED_CALLER_HEADER] == "curl/8"
+
+
+def test_should_refuse_to_forward_for_an_unverified_caller_in_enforce_mode(auth_identity, tmp_path):
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path, {})
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "enforce", tmp_path)
+    with pytest.raises(auth_identity.Unauthorized):
+        auth_identity.build_rest_write_headers(gate, None, "c", fallback_token=fake_token())
+
+
+def test_should_forward_nothing_special_in_off_mode(auth_identity, tmp_path):
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "off", tmp_path)
+    assert auth_identity.build_rest_write_headers(gate, None, "c", fallback_token=fake_token()) == {}
+

+# -- the ASGI gate for the SSE and streamable surfaces ------------------------------
+
+
+class _Recorder:
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.calls = 0
+
+    async def __call__(self, scope, receive, send):
+        self.calls += 1
+        if scope["type"] != "http":
+            return
+        body = b""
+        while True:
+            message = await receive()
+            body += message.get("body", b"")
+            if not message.get("more_body"):
+                break
+        await send({"type": "http.response.start", "status": 200, "headers": []})
+        await send({"type": "http.response.body", "body": b"seen:" + body})
+
+
+def _asgi(auth_identity, gate, downstream):
+    return auth_identity.WriteAuthMiddleware(
+        downstream,
+        lambda: gate,
+        write_paths=frozenset({"/mcp", "/messages/"}),
+        write_tools=frozenset({"log_progress", "update_context"}),
+    )
+
+
+async def _post(app, path, payload, headers=None):
+    import httpx
+
+    transport = httpx.ASGITransport(app=app)
+    async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=transport, base_url="http://localhost:3005") as client:
+        return await client.post(path, content=json.dumps(payload), headers=headers or {})
+
+
+CALL_WRITE = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": "tools/call", "params": {"name": "log_progress", "arguments": {}}}
+CALL_READ = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 2, "method": "tools/call", "params": {"name": "get_context", "arguments": {}}}
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", ["/mcp", "/messages/"])
+async def test_should_answer_401_for_an_unverified_write_in_enforce_mode(auth_identity, tmp_path, path):
+    pytest.importorskip("httpx")
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path, {fake_token(): "runner:codex"})
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "enforce", tmp_path)
+    down = _Recorder()
+    response = await _post(_asgi(auth_identity, gate, down), path, CALL_WRITE)
+    assert response.status_code == 401 and down.calls == 0
+    assert response.headers["www-authenticate"] == "Bearer"
+    assert gate.health_fields()["auth_unverified_calls"]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", ["/mcp", "/messages/"])
+async def test_should_pass_verified_writes_reads_and_other_paths(auth_identity, tmp_path, path):
+    pytest.importorskip("httpx")
+    token = fake_token()
+    write_hash_file(tmp_path, {token: "runner:codex"})
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "enforce", tmp_path)
+    app = _asgi(auth_identity, gate, _Recorder())
+    ok = await _post(app, path, CALL_WRITE, {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"})
+    assert ok.status_code == 200 and ok.content.startswith(b"seen:")
+    read = await _post(app, path, CALL_READ)
+    assert read.status_code == 200
+    batch = await _post(app, path, [CALL_READ, CALL_WRITE])
+    assert batch.status_code == 401
+    other = await _post(app, "/elsewhere", CALL_WRITE)
+    assert other.status_code == 200
+    junk = await _post(app, path, {"not": "json-rpc"})
+    assert junk.status_code == 200
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["off", "log"])
+async def test_should_not_block_in_off_or_log_mode(auth_identity, tmp_path, mode):
+    pytest.importorskip("httpx")
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, mode, tmp_path)
+    response = await _post(_asgi(auth_identity, gate, _Recorder()), "/mcp", CALL_WRITE)
+    assert response.status_code == 200
+
+
+async def test_should_reject_oversized_bodies(auth_identity, tmp_path):
+    pytest.importorskip("httpx")
+    gate = _gate(auth_identity, "log", tmp_path)
+    app = auth_identity.WriteAuthMiddleware(
+        _Recorder(), lambda: gate, write_paths=frozenset({"/mcp"}), write_tools=frozenset({"log_progress"}),
+        max_body_bytes=64,
+    )
+    response = await _post(app, "/mcp", {"pad": "x" * 200})
+    assert response.status_code == 413

+"""``instance_detector``: the ``shared`` instance value and the workspace binding."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import pytest
+
+from .helpers import BOUND_WORKSPACE
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", [None, "", "   ", "shared", "SHARED", " Shared "])
+def test_should_treat_shared_and_empty_as_the_null_instance(instance_detector, value):
+    assert instance_detector.normalize_instance_id(value) is None
+    assert instance_detector.SimpleInstanceDetector.normalize_instance_id(value) is None
+
+
+def test_should_keep_a_real_instance_id_as_given(instance_detector):
+    assert instance_detector.normalize_instance_id("feature-auth") == "feature-auth"
+    assert instance_detector.normalize_instance_id(" feature-auth ") == "feature-auth"
+    assert instance_detector.SHARED_INSTANCE_ID == "shared"
+
+
+def test_should_read_the_binding_from_env_without_a_cwd_fallback(instance_detector, monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.delenv("DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID", raising=False)
+    assert instance_detector.get_bound_workspace_id() is None
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID", "")
+    assert instance_detector.SimpleInstanceDetector.get_bound_workspace_id() is None
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID", BOUND_WORKSPACE)
+    assert instance_detector.get_bound_workspace_id() == BOUND_WORKSPACE
+
+
+def test_should_keep_the_read_side_behaviour_unchanged(instance_detector, monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID", "")
+    assert instance_detector.get_instance_id() is None
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID", "feature-auth")
+    assert instance_detector.get_instance_id() == "feature-auth"
+    monkeypatch.delenv("DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID", raising=False)
+    assert instance_detector.get_workspace_id()  # still falls back to the cwd for reads
