+"""The SSE and the new streamable-HTTP surface of ``server.py``.
+
+Both are served by one uvicorn listener (as in the image) in front of a fake
+REST port, and driven with the real MCP client.  Covered: the streamable
+handshake, 17 tools on both surfaces, auth in each mode, token forwarding to
+REST and the ids on the write tools.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncio
+import contextlib
+import json
+import logging
+from urllib.parse import quote
+
+import pytest
+
+pytest.importorskip("mcp.server.fastmcp")
+pytest.importorskip("uvicorn")
+pytest.importorskip("httpx")
+
+import httpx  # noqa: E402
+from aiohttp import web  # noqa: E402
+from aiohttp.test_utils import TestServer  # noqa: E402
+
+from .conftest import load_conport_module  # noqa: E402
+from .helpers import BOUND_WORKSPACE, INSTANCE, SSE_TOOL_NAMES, fake_token, write_hash_file  # noqa: E402
+
+WS = BOUND_WORKSPACE
+PID = "22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222"
+ACCEPT = "application/json, text/event-stream"
+
+WRITE_CALLS = {
+    "update_progress": (
+        {"progress_id": PID, "workspace_id": WS, "instance_id": INSTANCE, "updates": {"status": "COMPLETED"}},
+        "PUT", f"/api/progress/{PID}",
+    ),
+    "log_decision": (
+        {"workspace_id": WS, "topic": "t", "decision": "d", "rationale": "r"}, "POST", "/api/decisions",
+    ),
+    "fork_instance": (
+        {"workspace_id": WS, "target_instance": INSTANCE}, "POST", "/api/instance/fork",
+    ),
+    "promote": (
+        {"progress_id": PID, "workspace_id": WS, "instance_id": INSTANCE}, "POST", "/api/progress/promote",
+    ),
+    "promote_all": ({"workspace_id": WS, "instance_id": INSTANCE}, "POST", "/api/progress/promote_all"),
+    "update_context": (
+        {"workspace_id": WS, "instance_id": INSTANCE, "context_data": {"active_context": "x"}},
+        "POST", f"/api/context/{quote(WS, safe='')}",
+    ),
+    "log_progress": (
+        {"workspace_id": WS, "instance_id": INSTANCE, "description": "d"}, "POST", "/api/progress",
+    ),
+    "save_custom_data": (
+        {"workspace_id": WS, "category": "c", "key": "k", "value": {"a": 1}}, "POST", "/api/custom_data",
+    ),
+    "delete_custom_data": (
+        {"workspace_id": WS, "category": "c", "key": "k"}, "DELETE", "/api/custom_data",
+    ),
+}
+
+
+@contextlib.asynccontextmanager
+async def fake_rest():
+    calls: list[dict] = []
+
+    async def handler(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
+        body = await request.read()
+        calls.append(
+            {
+                "method": request.method,
+                "path": request.raw_path.split("?", 1)[0],
+                "headers": {k.lower(): v for k, v in request.headers.items()},
+                "json": json.loads(body) if body else None,
+            }
+        )
+        return web.json_response({"ok": True})
+
+    app = web.Application()
+    app.router.add_route("*", "/{tail:.*}", handler)
+    server = TestServer(app)
+    await server.start_server()
+    try:
+        yield f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.port}", calls
+    finally:
+        await server.close()
+
+
+@contextlib.asynccontextmanager
+async def serve(app):
+    import uvicorn
+
+    config = uvicorn.Config(app, host="127.0.0.1", port=0, log_level="warning", lifespan="on")
+    server = uvicorn.Server(config)
+    task = asyncio.create_task(server.serve())
+    for _ in range(500):
+        if server.started:
+            break
+        await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
+    else:  # pragma: no cover
+        raise RuntimeError("uvicorn did not start")
+    port = server.servers[0].sockets[0].getsockname()[1]
+    try:
+        yield f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
+    finally:
+        server.should_exit = True
+        await task
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def load_server(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
+    def factory(mode="log", tokens=None, proxy_token=None, rest_url=None):
+        monkeypatch.setenv("CONPORT_AUTH_MODE", mode)
+        write_hash_file(tmp_path / "auth", tokens or {})
+        monkeypatch.setenv("CONPORT_AUTH_DIR", str(tmp_path / "auth"))
+        module = load_conport_module("server", monkeypatch)
+        module.PROXY_TOKEN_PROVIDER = lambda: proxy_token
+        if rest_url:
+            module.CONPORT_URL = rest_url
+        return module
+
+    return factory
+
+
+@contextlib.asynccontextmanager
+async def mcp_session(transport: str, base: str, headers=None):
+    from mcp import ClientSession
+
+    headers = headers or {}
+    if transport == "sse":
+        from mcp.client.sse import sse_client
+
+        async with sse_client(f"{base}/sse", headers=headers) as (read, write):
+            async with ClientSession(read, write) as session:
+                await session.initialize()
+                yield session
+    else:
+        from mcp.client import streamable_http as sh
+
+        client = httpx.AsyncClient(headers=headers, timeout=30)
+        async with client:
+            async with sh.streamable_http_client(f"{base}/mcp", http_client=client) as (read, write, _):
+                async with ClientSession(read, write) as session:
+                    await session.initialize()
+                    yield session
+
+
+def _sse_data(text: str) -> dict:
+    for line in text.splitlines():
+        if line.startswith("data:"):
+            return json.loads(line[5:].strip())
+    return json.loads(text)
+
+
+def _rpc(method, params=None, rid=1):
+    return {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": rid, "method": method, **({"params": params} if params else {})}
+
+
+INIT = _rpc(
+    "initialize",
+    {"protocolVersion": "2025-03-26", "capabilities": {}, "clientInfo": {"name": "w02-test", "version": "0"}},
+)
+
+
+# -- transport ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+async def test_should_complete_the_streamable_handshake_with_a_session_id(load_server):
+    module = load_server("log")
+    async with serve(module.build_http_app()) as base:
+        async with httpx.AsyncClient(base_url=base, timeout=30) as client:
+            init = await client.post("/mcp", json=INIT, headers={"Accept": ACCEPT})
+            assert init.status_code == 200
+            session = init.headers["mcp-session-id"]
+            assert session
+            assert _sse_data(init.text)["result"]["serverInfo"]["name"] == "conport"
+            headers = {"Accept": ACCEPT, "Mcp-Session-Id": session}
+            done = await client.post("/mcp", json={"jsonrpc": "2.0", "method": "notifications/initialized"},
+                                     headers=headers)
+            assert done.status_code == 202
+            listing = await client.post("/mcp", json=_rpc("tools/list", rid=2), headers=headers)
+            names = {tool["name"] for tool in _sse_data(listing.text)["result"]["tools"]}
+            assert names == SSE_TOOL_NAMES and len(names) == 17
+            no_session = await client.post("/mcp", json=_rpc("tools/list", rid=3), headers={"Accept": ACCEPT})
+            assert no_session.status_code == 400
+
+
+async def test_should_keep_serving_sse_beside_the_streamable_path(load_server):
+    module = load_server("log")
+    paths = {getattr(route, "path", None) for route in module.build_http_app().app.routes}
+    assert {"/mcp", "/sse", "/messages"} <= paths  # Starlette's Mount drops the trailing slash
+    assert module.mcp.settings.streamable_http_path == "/mcp"
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize("transport", ["sse", "streamable"])
+async def test_should_expose_the_same_17_tools_on_both_surfaces(load_server, transport):
+    module = load_server("log")
+    async with serve(module.build_http_app()) as base:
+        async with mcp_session(transport, base) as session:
+            tools = (await session.list_tools()).tools
+    assert {tool.name for tool in tools} == SSE_TOOL_NAMES
+    assert len(tools) == 17
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize("transport", ["sse", "streamable"])
+async def test_should_mark_the_ids_optional_on_the_write_tools(load_server, transport):
+    # Amendment 04 (W12): the ids are optional; the server fills them from its binding.
+    module = load_server("log")
+    async with serve(module.build_http_app()) as base:
+        async with mcp_session(transport, base) as session:
+            tools = {tool.name: tool.inputSchema for tool in (await session.list_tools()).tools}
+    for name in ("update_progress", "promote", "promote_all", "update_context", "log_progress"):
+        assert not ({"workspace_id", "instance_id"} & set(tools[name].get("required", []))), name
+    assert "target_instance" in tools["fork_instance"]["required"]
+    assert "workspace_id" not in tools["fork_instance"]["required"]
+    for name in ("log_decision", "save_custom_data", "delete_custom_data"):
+        assert "workspace_id" not in tools[name].get("required", []), name
+        assert "ctx" not in tools[name]["properties"]  # the request context is not a tool argument
+    assert "instance_id" not in tools["save_custom_data"]["properties"]  # custom_data has no instance column
+

+# -- token forwarding ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

+@pytest.mark.parametrize("transport", ["sse", "streamable"])
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("tool", list(WRITE_CALLS))
+async def test_should_forward_the_callers_token_and_ids_to_rest(load_server, transport, tool):
+    token = fake_token()
+    arguments, method, path = WRITE_CALLS[tool]
+    async with fake_rest() as (rest_url, calls):
+        module = load_server("enforce", {token: "runner:codex"}, rest_url=rest_url)
+        async with serve(module.build_http_app()) as base:
+            async with mcp_session(transport, base, {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}) as session:
+                result = await session.call_tool(tool, arguments)
+                assert not result.isError, result
+    (call,) = calls
+    assert (call["method"], call["path"]) == (method, path)
+    assert call["headers"]["authorization"] == f"Bearer {token}"
+    assert "x-dopemux-unverified-caller" not in call["headers"]
+    if call["json"] is not None and tool != "update_context":
+        assert call["json"]["workspace_id"] == WS or tool == "update_progress"
+    if tool in ("log_progress", "promote_all"):
+        assert call["json"]["instance_id"] == INSTANCE
+    if tool == "update_progress":
+        assert call["json"] == {"status": "COMPLETED", "workspace_id": WS, "instance_id": INSTANCE}
+    if tool == "update_context":
+        assert call["json"] == {"active_context": "x", "instance_id": INSTANCE, "workspace_id": WS}
+    if tool == "delete_custom_data":
+        assert call["json"] is None
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize("transport", ["sse", "streamable"])
+async def test_should_not_send_credentials_with_reads(load_server, transport):

+                    assert response.status_code == 401, (path, headers)
+                    assert response.headers["www-authenticate"] == "Bearer"

+                    headers={"Accept": ACCEPT},
+                )
+                assert read.status_code != 401
+    assert calls == []

+"""Migration 008 (write principal): the SQL text, schema.sql and the gate.
+
+No database is touched.  The migration is operator-run only (packet C10).
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import importlib.util
+import re
+import sys
+
+import pytest
+
+from .helpers import CONPORT_DIR, MIGRATIONS_DIR
+
+TABLES = ("workspace_contexts", "decisions", "progress_entries", "custom_data")
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
+def gate():
+    path = MIGRATIONS_DIR / "conport_migration_gate.py"
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("conport_gate_w02", path)
+    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    sys.modules[spec.name] = module
+    try:
+        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
+    finally:
+        sys.modules.pop(spec.name, None)
+    return module
+
+
+def _strip_comments(sql: str) -> str:
+    return "\n".join(line.split("--", 1)[0] for line in sql.splitlines())
+
+
+def test_should_add_a_nullable_defaulted_principal_column_to_each_written_table():
+    sql = _strip_comments((MIGRATIONS_DIR / "008_write_principal.sql").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    for table in TABLES:
+        assert re.search(
+            rf"ALTER TABLE {table}\s+ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS write_principal VARCHAR\(255\)\s+DEFAULT 'unverified'",
+            sql,
+        ), table
+    assert "NOT NULL" not in sql
+    assert "DROP" not in sql.upper().replace("DROP-", "")
+
+
+def test_should_be_idempotent_and_transactional():
+    sql = _strip_comments((MIGRATIONS_DIR / "008_write_principal.sql").read_text(encoding="utf-8")).upper()
+    assert "BEGIN;" in sql and "COMMIT;" in sql
+    assert sql.count("ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS") == len(TABLES)
+    assert "UPDATE " not in sql  # no existing row is rewritten
+
+
+def test_should_verify_the_columns_inside_the_migration():
+    sql = (MIGRATIONS_DIR / "008_write_principal.sql").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    assert "RAISE EXCEPTION" in sql and "write_principal" in sql
+
+
+def test_should_roll_back_by_dropping_exactly_those_columns():
+    sql = _strip_comments((MIGRATIONS_DIR / "008_rollback.sql").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    for table in TABLES:
+        assert re.search(rf"ALTER TABLE {table}\s+DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS write_principal", sql), table
+    assert sql.upper().count("DROP COLUMN") == len(TABLES)
+    assert "BEGIN;" in sql.upper() and "COMMIT;" in sql.upper()
+    # a rollback may not touch the migration ledger of other versions or any other column
+    assert "conport_schema_migrations" not in sql or "version = 8" in sql
+
+
+def test_should_put_the_principal_in_the_canonical_schema():
+    schema = (CONPORT_DIR / "schema.sql").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    for table in TABLES:
+        block = re.search(rf"CREATE TABLE {table} \((.*?)\n\);", schema, re.S)
+        assert block, table
+        assert "write_principal VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT 'unverified'" in block.group(1), table
+    # no other table gains one
+    assert schema.count("write_principal") == len(TABLES)
+
+
+def test_should_name_the_migration_files_so_the_gate_discovers_only_the_up_file(gate):
+    found = {m.filename for m in gate.discover_migrations(MIGRATIONS_DIR)}
+    assert "008_write_principal.sql" in found
+    assert "008_rollback.sql" not in found  # rollback files are never applied
+
+
+def test_should_require_the_new_migration_and_columns(gate):
+    assert "008_write_principal.sql" in gate.REQUIRED_MIGRATIONS
+    for table in TABLES:
+        assert (table, "write_principal") in gate.REQUIRED_COLUMNS
+    # the five existing names are untouched
+    assert {
+        "001_enhanced_decision_model.sql",
+        "002_decision_patterns_table.sql",
+        "003_multi_tenancy_foundation.sql",
+        "004_unified_query_indexes.sql",
+        "007_worktree_support_simple.sql",
+    } <= gate.REQUIRED_MIGRATIONS
+
+
+def test_should_let_the_adopt_path_recognise_a_psql_applied_008(gate):
+    """psql-then-adopt: ``apply`` must see 008 as already applied, not re-run it."""
+
+    class Conn:
+        def __init__(self, present):
+            self.present = present
+
+    calls = []
+
+    def column_exists(conn, schema, table, column):
+        calls.append((table, column))
+        return (table, column) in conn.present
+
+    gate.column_exists, original = column_exists, gate.column_exists
+    try:
+        present = Conn({(t, "write_principal") for t in TABLES})
+        assert gate.migration_schema_errors(present, "public", 8) == []
+        missing = Conn(set())
+        errors = gate.migration_schema_errors(missing, "public", 8)
+        assert len(errors) == len(TABLES) and all("write_principal" in e for e in errors)
+    finally:
+        gate.column_exists = original
+    assert all(column == "write_principal" for _, column in calls)
+
+
+def test_should_keep_the_existing_adoption_checks(gate):
+    # version 7 keeps its own checks; unknown versions still fail closed
+    seen = []
+    original = (gate.column_exists, gate.index_exists)
+    gate.column_exists = lambda conn, schema, table, column: seen.append((table, column)) or True
+    gate.index_exists = lambda conn, schema, index: True
+    try:
+        assert gate.migration_schema_errors(object(), "public", 7) == []
+    finally:
+        gate.column_exists, gate.index_exists = original
+    assert ("decisions", "created_by_instance") in seen
+    assert gate.migration_schema_errors(object(), "public", 99) == [
+        "missing schema adoption checks for migration version 099"
+    ]

+"""ConPort REST port (enhanced_server.py): ids per table, ``shared``, the binding
+check, the three auth modes, the stored principal and the /mcp write tools.
+
+The server runs against a fake pool and fake Redis; nothing touches a network
+except the aiohttp test client talking to the in-process app.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import logging
+from urllib.parse import quote
+
+import pytest
+
+from .conftest import rest_client
+from .helpers import BOUND_WORKSPACE, INSTANCE, OTHER_WORKSPACE, fake_token
+
+PROGRESS_ID = "22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222"
+WS = BOUND_WORKSPACE
+
+
+def _ids(**extra):
+    return {"workspace_id": WS, "instance_id": INSTANCE, **extra}
+
+
+# name, method, path, body, table, requires_instance
+CASES = {
+    "context": ("POST", f"/api/context/{quote(WS, safe='')}", {"active_context": "x", "instance_id": INSTANCE},
+                "workspace_contexts", True),
+    "decision": ("POST", "/api/decisions", {"workspace_id": WS, "summary": "s", "rationale": "r"},
+                 "decisions", False),
+    "progress": ("POST", "/api/progress", _ids(description="do it"), "progress_entries", True),
+    "progress_update": ("PUT", f"/api/progress/{PROGRESS_ID}", _ids(status="COMPLETED"),
+                        "progress_entries", True),
+    "custom_data": ("POST", "/api/custom_data",
+                    {"workspace_id": WS, "category": "c", "key": "k", "value": {"a": 1}},
+                    "custom_data", False),
+    "claim": ("POST", "/api/custom_data/claim",
+              {"workspace_id": WS, "category": "c", "key": "k", "value": {"_fingerprint_v1": "x"}},
+              "custom_data", False),
+    "delete": ("DELETE", f"/api/custom_data?workspace_id={WS}&category=c&key=k", None,
+               "custom_data", False),
+    "fork": ("POST", "/api/instance/fork", {"workspace_id": WS, "target_instance": INSTANCE},
+             "progress_entries", True),
+    "promote": ("POST", "/api/progress/promote", _ids(progress_id=PROGRESS_ID),
+                "progress_entries", True),
+    "promote_all": ("POST", "/api/progress/promote_all", _ids(), "progress_entries", True),
+}
+IDS = list(CASES)
+
+
+async def _call(client, name, *, body=None, headers=None, path=None):
+    method, default_path, default_body, _, _ = CASES[name]
+    kwargs = {"headers": headers or {}}
+    payload = default_body if body is None else body
+    if payload is not None and method != "DELETE":
+        kwargs["json"] = payload
+    return await client.request(method, path or default_path, **kwargs)
+
+
+def _drop(body, key):
+    return {k: v for k, v in body.items() if k != key}
+
+
+# -- ids per table -------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", IDS)
+async def test_should_accept_a_write_with_bound_ids(make_rest_server, name):
+    server = make_rest_server("off")
+    async with rest_client(server) as client:
+        response = await _call(client, name)
+        assert response.status == 200, await response.text()
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", [n for n in IDS if n not in ("context", "delete")])
+async def test_should_fill_an_omitted_workspace_id_on_body_writes(make_rest_server, name):
+    # Amendment 04 (W12): an omitted workspace_id is the server's binding.
+    server = make_rest_server("off")
+    body = _drop(CASES[name][2], "workspace_id")
+    async with rest_client(server) as client:
+        response = await _call(client, name, body=body)
+        assert response.status == 200, await response.text()
+    assert server.db_pool.writes() != []
+
+
+async def test_should_fill_an_omitted_workspace_id_on_delete_and_reject_the_wrong_one(make_rest_server):
+    server = make_rest_server("off")
+    async with rest_client(server) as client:
+        omitted = await client.delete("/api/custom_data?category=c&key=k")
+        assert omitted.status == 200, await omitted.text()
+        assert any(args[:1] == (WS,) for _, args in server.db_pool.writes())
+        before = len(server.db_pool.writes())
+        wrong = await client.delete(f"/api/custom_data?workspace_id={OTHER_WORKSPACE}&category=c&key=k")
+        assert wrong.status == 400
+    assert len(server.db_pool.writes()) == before
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", IDS)
+async def test_should_reject_a_workspace_that_is_not_the_servers_binding(make_rest_server, name):
+    server = make_rest_server("off")
+    method, path, body, _, _ = CASES[name]
+    if name == "context":
+        path = f"/api/context/{quote(OTHER_WORKSPACE, safe='')}"
+    elif name == "delete":
+        path = f"/api/custom_data?workspace_id={OTHER_WORKSPACE}&category=c&key=k"
+    else:
+        body = {**body, "workspace_id": OTHER_WORKSPACE}
+    async with rest_client(server) as client:
+        response = await _call(client, name, body=body, path=path)
+        assert response.status == 400
+        assert "does not match" in (await response.text())
+    assert server.db_pool.writes() == []
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", [n for n in IDS if CASES[n][4] and n not in ("fork", "promote_all")])
+async def test_should_fall_back_to_shared_for_an_omitted_instance_id(make_rest_server, name, monkeypatch):
+    # Amendment 04 (W12): an omitted instance_id is the server's DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID, else shared (NULL).
+    monkeypatch.delenv("DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID", raising=False)
+    server = make_rest_server("off")
+    body = _drop(CASES[name][2], "instance_id")
+    async with rest_client(server) as client:
+        response = await _call(client, name, body=body)
+        assert response.status == 200, await response.text()
+    assert server.db_pool.writes() != []
+

+    server = make_rest_server("off")

+        assert response.status == 400
+        assert "real instance" in await response.text()

+async def test_should_count_callers_forwarded_by_the_verified_bridge_proxy(make_rest_server):
+    from .helpers import fake_token as ft
+
+    proxy, other = ft("proxy"), ft("codex")
+    server = make_rest_server("log", {proxy: "internal:bridge-proxy", other: "runner:codex"})
+    async with rest_client(server) as client:
+        await _call(client, "progress", headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {proxy}",
+                                                 "X-Dopemux-Unverified-Caller": "some-agent/1"})
+        # the header from anyone else is not trusted as a verified signal
+        await _call(client, "progress", headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {other}",
+                                                 "X-Dopemux-Unverified-Caller": "spoof/1"})
+        health = await (await client.get("/health")).json()
+    assert health["auth_unverified_calls"] == {"some-agent/1": 1}
+    stored = [args for sql, args in server.db_pool.writes() if "write_principal" in sql]
+    assert any("internal:bridge-proxy" in args for args in stored)

+# -- /mcp write tools -------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+MCP_WRITES = {
+    "conport_update_context": _ids(active_context="x"),
+    "conport_log_decision": {"workspace_id": WS, "summary": "s", "rationale": "r"},
+    "conport_log_progress": _ids(description="d"),
+    "conport_update_progress": _ids(progress_id=PROGRESS_ID, status="COMPLETED"),
+    "conport_save_custom_data": {"workspace_id": WS, "category": "c", "key": "k", "value": {"a": 1}},
+    "conport_delete_custom_data": {"workspace_id": WS, "category": "c", "key": "k"},
+    "conport_fork_instance": {"workspace_id": WS, "target_instance": INSTANCE},
+    "conport_promote": _ids(progress_id=PROGRESS_ID),
+    "conport_promote_all": _ids(),
+}
+
+
+def _rpc(name, arguments, rid=1):
+    return {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": rid, "method": "tools/call", "params": {"name": name, "arguments": arguments}}
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("tool", list(MCP_WRITES))
+async def test_should_gate_every_mcp_write_tool_in_enforce_mode(make_rest_server, tool):
+    token = fake_token()
+    server = make_rest_server("enforce", {token: "runner:codex"})
+    async with rest_client(server) as client:
+        server.port = client.port  # the custom_data tools call back into the REST port
+        denied = await client.post("/mcp", json=_rpc(tool, MCP_WRITES[tool]))
+        assert denied.status == 401
+        assert server.db_pool.writes() == []
+        ok = await client.post("/mcp", json=_rpc(tool, MCP_WRITES[tool]),
+                               headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"})
+        assert ok.status == 200
+        assert "error" not in await ok.json(), tool
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("tool", list(MCP_WRITES))
+async def test_should_bind_every_mcp_write_tool_to_the_servers_workspace(make_rest_server, tool, monkeypatch):
+    # Amendment 04 (W12): an omitted workspace_id is filled; a different one is rejected.
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID", INSTANCE)
+    server = make_rest_server("off")
+    omitted = {k: v for k, v in MCP_WRITES[tool].items() if k != "workspace_id"}
+    wrong = {**omitted, "workspace_id": OTHER_WORKSPACE}
+    async with rest_client(server) as client:
+        server.port = client.port
+        filled = await (await client.post("/mcp", json=_rpc(tool, omitted))).json()
+        assert "error" not in filled, (tool, filled)
+        written = len(server.db_pool.writes())
+        rejected = await (await client.post("/mcp", json=_rpc(tool, wrong))).json()
+    assert "does not match" in rejected["error"]["message"], tool
+    assert len(server.db_pool.writes()) == written
+
+
+async def test_should_store_the_principal_for_mcp_custom_data_through_the_loopback(make_rest_server):

+            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
+        )
+        assert response.status == 200 and "error" not in await response.json()
+    stored = [args for sql, args in server.db_pool.writes() if "INSERT INTO custom_data" in sql]
+    assert stored and "runner:codex" in stored[0]

+async def test_should_reject_writes_in_enforce_mode_without_the_column(make_rest_server):
+    token = fake_token()
+    server = make_rest_server("enforce", {token: "runner:codex"}, principal_tables=())
+    async with rest_client(server) as client:
+        response = await _call(client, "progress", headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"})
+        assert response.status == 503
+        assert "migration 008" in await response.text()
+    assert server.db_pool.writes() == []
+
+
+async def test_should_report_a_partly_migrated_database(make_rest_server):
+    server = make_rest_server("log", principal_tables=("decisions",))
+    async with rest_client(server) as client:
+        health = await (await client.get("/health")).json()
+    assert health["auth_principal_column"] == "partial"
+
+
+# -- secrets ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+async def test_should_not_log_tokens_or_authorization_headers(make_rest_server, caplog):
+    token = fake_token()
+    server = make_rest_server("enforce", {token: "runner:codex"})

+    assert token not in caplog.text and "Authorization" not in caplog.text and "Bearer" not in caplog.text

+"""docker_runtime: the ConPort sidecar gets its own auth mode and the read-only
+hash-directory mount; W01's dope-memory lines are preserved."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from dopemux.mcp import docker_runtime as dr
+
+
+def _override(**extra):
+    labels = dr.build_labels(
+        project_id="dnh",
+        workspace_id="/w",
+        project_root="/w",
+        worktree_root="/w",
+        project_hash="h",
+        worktree_hash="8d6d",
+        instance_id="dnh-8d6d-x",
+        service="x",
+        scope="worktree",
+        transport="http",
+    )
+    return dr.generate_compose_override(
+        services=["conport", "dope-memory"],
+        identity={
+            "DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID": "8d6d",
+            "DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID": "/w",
+            "DOPE_MEMORY_WORKSPACE_ID": "w",
+            "DOPE_MEMORY_INSTANCE_ID": "8d6d",
+        },
+        ports={"CONPORT_HTTP_PORT": 3040, "CONPORT_MCP_PORT": 3041,
+               "CONPORT_INFO_PORT": 4040, "DOPE_MEMORY_PORT": 3060},
+        container_names={"conport": "c-conport", "dope-memory": "c-mem"},
+        labels_by_service={"conport": labels, "dope-memory": labels},
+        memory_data_path=Path("/tmp/mem-data"),
+        **extra,
+    )
+
+
+def _block(text: str, name: str) -> str:
+    start = text.index(f"  {name}:\n")
+    rest = text[start + 1 :]
+    nxt = [i for i in (rest.find("\n  conport:\n"), rest.find("\n  dope-memory:\n"), rest.find("\nnetworks:")) if i > 0]
+    return text[start : start + 1 + (min(nxt) if nxt else len(rest))]
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def auth_dir(tmp_path):
+    path = tmp_path / "auth"
+    path.mkdir()
+    return path
+
+
+def test_should_render_the_conport_mode_and_a_readonly_directory_mount(auth_dir):
+    block = _block(_override(conport_auth_mode="enforce", auth_dir=auth_dir), "conport")
+    assert 'CONPORT_AUTH_MODE: "enforce"' in block
+    assert 'CONPORT_AUTH_DIR: "/run/dopemux-auth"' in block
+    assert f'- "{auth_dir.resolve()}:/run/dopemux-auth:ro"' in block
+    assert "volumes:" in block
+    # the pre-existing identity env is kept
+    assert 'DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID: "/w"' in block and "ports: !override" in block
+
+
+def test_should_switch_each_server_separately(auth_dir):
+    text = _override(auth_mode="enforce", conport_auth_mode="log", auth_dir=auth_dir)
+    assert 'CONPORT_AUTH_MODE: "log"' in _block(text, "conport")
+    assert 'DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE: "enforce"' in _block(text, "dope-memory")
+    assert "DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE" not in _block(text, "conport")
+    assert "CONPORT_AUTH_MODE" not in _block(text, "dope-memory")
+
+
+def test_should_default_to_log_and_read_the_env_when_not_passed(auth_dir, monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.delenv("CONPORT_AUTH_MODE", raising=False)
+    assert 'CONPORT_AUTH_MODE: "log"' in _block(_override(auth_dir=auth_dir), "conport")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("CONPORT_AUTH_MODE", "enforce")
+    assert 'CONPORT_AUTH_MODE: "enforce"' in _block(_override(auth_dir=auth_dir), "conport")
+
+
+def test_should_not_mount_a_missing_directory_but_still_send_the_mode(tmp_path):
+    block = _block(_override(conport_auth_mode="log", auth_dir=tmp_path / "nope"), "conport")
+    assert "dopemux-auth" not in block and "volumes:" not in block
+    assert 'CONPORT_AUTH_MODE: "log"' in block
+
+
+def test_should_reject_an_unknown_conport_mode():
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="conport auth mode"):
+        _override(conport_auth_mode="permissive")
+
+
+def test_should_preserve_the_dope_memory_changes(auth_dir):
+    text = _override(auth_mode="enforce", auth_dir=auth_dir)
+    memory = _block(text, "dope-memory")
+    assert 'DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_MODE: "enforce"' in memory
+    assert 'DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_DIR: "/run/dopemux-auth"' in memory
+    assert f'- "{auth_dir.resolve()}:/run/dopemux-auth:ro"' in memory
+    assert ":/data" in memory
+    assert text.count("dopemux-auth:ro") == 2  # one mount per server, nothing else
+
+
+def test_should_never_put_a_token_or_hash_in_the_override(auth_dir):
+    (auth_dir / "memory-token-hashes.json").write_text('{"' + "a" * 64 + '": "runner:codex"}')
+    text = _override(conport_auth_mode="enforce", auth_dir=auth_dir)
+    assert "a" * 64 not in text and "runner:codex" not in text
+    assert "bearer" not in text.lower() and "memory-token-hashes" not in text  # only the directory travels
+
+
+def test_should_keep_generated_env_dropping_secret_keys():
+    text = dr.generate_mcp_env({"CONPORT_HTTP_PORT": 3040, "CONPORT_AUTH_TOKEN": "x", "DB_PASSWORD": "y"})
+    assert "CONPORT_AUTH_TOKEN" not in text and "DB_PASSWORD" not in text
+    assert "CONPORT_HTTP_PORT=3040" in text

+"""``conport_mcp_stdio.py``: the in-image stdio proxy forwards the caller's token.
+
+A stdio process has no HTTP headers, so the caller's token comes from the
+process environment (``CONPORT_STDIO_TOKEN``) and the ``internal:bridge-proxy``
+fallback from the injectable provider (production default: none, until the W03
+helper exists).
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import pytest
+
+pytest.importorskip("mcp.server.fastmcp")
+
+from .test_mcp_surfaces import WRITE_CALLS, fake_rest  # noqa: E402
+from .conftest import load_conport_module  # noqa: E402
+from .helpers import SSE_TOOL_NAMES, fake_token, write_hash_file  # noqa: E402
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def load_stdio(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
+    def factory(mode="log", tokens=None, proxy_token=None, rest_url=None, caller_token=None):
+        monkeypatch.setenv("CONPORT_AUTH_MODE", mode)
+        write_hash_file(tmp_path / "auth", tokens or {})
+        monkeypatch.setenv("CONPORT_AUTH_DIR", str(tmp_path / "auth"))
+        if caller_token:
+            monkeypatch.setenv("CONPORT_STDIO_TOKEN", caller_token)
+        else:
+            monkeypatch.delenv("CONPORT_STDIO_TOKEN", raising=False)
+        module = load_conport_module("conport_mcp_stdio", monkeypatch)
+        module.PROXY_TOKEN_PROVIDER = lambda: proxy_token
+        if rest_url:
+            module.CONPORT_URL = rest_url
+        return module
+
+    return factory
+
+
+async def test_should_keep_the_17_tool_names(load_stdio):
+    module = load_stdio()
+    assert {tool.name for tool in await module.mcp.list_tools()} == SSE_TOOL_NAMES
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("tool", list(WRITE_CALLS))
+async def test_should_forward_the_callers_token_from_the_environment(load_stdio, tool):
+    token = fake_token()
+    arguments, method, path = WRITE_CALLS[tool]
+    async with fake_rest() as (rest_url, calls):
+        module = load_stdio("enforce", {token: "runner:codex"}, rest_url=rest_url, caller_token=token)
+        await module.mcp.call_tool(tool, arguments)
+    (call,) = calls
+    assert (call["method"], call["path"]) == (method, path)
+    assert call["headers"]["authorization"] == f"Bearer {token}"
+
+
+async def test_should_refuse_an_unverified_write_in_enforce_mode(load_stdio):
+    async with fake_rest() as (rest_url, calls):
+        module = load_stdio("enforce", {fake_token(): "runner:codex"}, rest_url=rest_url)
+        with pytest.raises(Exception, match="401|unauthorized"):
+            await module.mcp.call_tool("log_progress", WRITE_CALLS["log_progress"][0])
+    assert calls == []
+
+
+async def test_should_use_the_proxy_principal_only_in_log_mode_with_no_caller_token(load_stdio):
+    proxy = fake_token("proxy")
+    async with fake_rest() as (rest_url, calls):
+        module = load_stdio("log", {proxy: "internal:bridge-proxy"}, proxy_token=proxy, rest_url=rest_url)
+        await module.mcp.call_tool("log_progress", WRITE_CALLS["log_progress"][0])
+    (call,) = calls
+    assert call["headers"]["authorization"] == f"Bearer {proxy}"
+    assert call["headers"]["x-dopemux-unverified-caller"] == "stdio"
+
+
+async def test_should_send_no_credentials_when_there_is_no_token_at_all(load_stdio):
+    async with fake_rest() as (rest_url, calls):
+        module = load_stdio("log", {}, rest_url=rest_url)
+        await module.mcp.call_tool("log_progress", WRITE_CALLS["log_progress"][0])
+    (call,) = calls
+    assert "authorization" not in call["headers"]
+    assert call["headers"]["x-dopemux-unverified-caller"] == "stdio"
+
+
+async def test_should_not_send_credentials_with_reads(load_stdio):
+    token = fake_token()
+    async with fake_rest() as (rest_url, calls):
+        module = load_stdio("enforce", {token: "runner:codex"}, rest_url=rest_url, caller_token=token)
+        await module.mcp.call_tool("get_context", {"workspace_id": "/w"})
+    assert "authorization" not in calls[0]["headers"]

+"""Fixtures for the W03 auth tests: temp HOME and a stubbed ``security`` binary.
+
+Nothing here touches the real keychain or the real ~/.config. The stub keeps its
+fake keychain and its argv log OUTSIDE the temp HOME so that "no token in any
+file under HOME" is a meaningful assertion.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import os
+import stat
+import sys
+import textwrap
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+STUB_SOURCE = textwrap.dedent(
+    '''\
+    #!{python}
+    import json, os, shlex, sys, time
+
+    STORE = os.environ["FAKE_SECURITY_STORE"]
+    LOG = os.environ["FAKE_SECURITY_LOG"]
+    MODE = os.environ.get("FAKE_SECURITY_MODE", "ok")
+
+
+    def load():
+        try:
+            with open(STORE, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+                return json.load(fh)
+        except FileNotFoundError:
+            return {{}}
+
+
+    def save(data):
+        with open(STORE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+            json.dump(data, fh)
+
+
+    def opt(args, flag):
+        return args[args.index(flag) + 1]
+
+
+    def run(args):
+        verb = args[0]
+        data = load()
+        key = opt(args, "-s") + "|" + opt(args, "-a")
+        if verb == "find-generic-password":
+            if MODE == "locked":
+                sys.exit(36)
+            if MODE == "hang":
+                time.sleep(30)
+            if MODE == "empty":
+                print("")
+                return
+            if key not in data:
+                sys.exit(44)
+            print(data[key])
+        elif verb == "add-generic-password":
+            if MODE == "write_fail":
+                sys.exit(1)
+            data[key] = opt(args, "-w")
+            save(data)
+        elif verb == "delete-generic-password":
+            if key not in data:
+                sys.exit(44)
+            del data[key]
+            save(data)
+        else:
+            sys.exit(2)
+
+
+    argv = sys.argv[1:]
+    with open(LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+        fh.write(json.dumps({{"argv": argv}}) + "\\n")
+    if argv == ["-i"]:
+        for line in sys.stdin.read().splitlines():
+            line = line.strip()
+            if line:
+                run(shlex.split(line))
+    else:
+        if argv and argv[0] == "find-generic-password":
+            stdin_text = sys.stdin.read()
+            with open(LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+                fh.write(json.dumps({{"stdin_len": len(stdin_text)}}) + "\\n")
+        run(argv)
+    '''
+)
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Env:
+    home: Path
+    store: Path
+    log: Path
+    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
+
+    @property
+    def auth_dir(self) -> Path:
+        return self.home / ".config" / "dopemux" / "auth"
+
+    @property
+    def hash_file(self) -> Path:
+        return self.auth_dir / "memory-token-hashes.json"
+
+    def keychain(self) -> dict:
+        if not self.store.exists():
+            return {}
+        return json.loads(self.store.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+
+    def keychain_token(self, principal: str) -> str:
+        return self.keychain()[f"dopemux-mcp|{principal}"]
+
+    def set_keychain_token(self, principal: str, token: str) -> None:
+        data = self.keychain()
+        data[f"dopemux-mcp|{principal}"] = token
+        self.store.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
+
+    def argv_calls(self) -> list[list[str]]:
+        if not self.log.exists():
+            return []
+        out = []
+        for line in self.log.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
+            row = json.loads(line)
+            if "argv" in row:
+                out.append(row["argv"])
+        return out
+
+    def stdin_lens(self) -> list[int]:
+        if not self.log.exists():
+            return []
+        return [
+            json.loads(line)["stdin_len"]
+            for line in self.log.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
+            if "stdin_len" in json.loads(line)
+        ]
+
+    def set_mode(self, mode: str) -> None:
+        self.monkeypatch.setenv("FAKE_SECURITY_MODE", mode)
+
+    def home_files_text(self) -> str:
+        chunks = []
+        for path in self.home.rglob("*"):
+            if path.is_file():
+                chunks.append(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"))
+        return "\n".join(chunks)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def env(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> Env:
+    home = tmp_path / "home"
+    home.mkdir()
+    outside = tmp_path / "outside"
+    outside.mkdir()
+    stub = outside / "security"
+    stub.write_text(STUB_SOURCE.format(python=sys.executable), encoding="utf-8")
+    stub.chmod(stub.stat().st_mode | stat.S_IXUSR)
+    store = outside / "store.json"
+    log = outside / "argv.log"
+
+    monkeypatch.setenv("HOME", str(home))
+    monkeypatch.setenv("FAKE_SECURITY_STORE", str(store))
+    monkeypatch.setenv("FAKE_SECURITY_LOG", str(log))
+    monkeypatch.delenv("FAKE_SECURITY_MODE", raising=False)
+    for name in ("DOPE_MEMORY_AUTH_DIR", "CONPORT_AUTH_DIR"):
+        monkeypatch.delenv(name, raising=False)
+
+    from dopemux.auth import keychain
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(keychain, "SECURITY_BIN", str(stub))
+    monkeypatch.setattr(keychain, "TIMEOUT_SECONDS", 1.0)
+    return Env(home=home, store=store, log=log, monkeypatch=monkeypatch)

+"""CLI tests for ``dopemux auth`` (issue, rotate, revoke, list)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import logging
+import re
+
+from click.testing import CliRunner
+
+from dopemux.auth import hash_token
+from dopemux.commands.auth_commands import auth
+
+HEX64 = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{64}")
+
+
+def run(*args: str):
+    return CliRunner().invoke(auth, list(args))
+
+
+def assert_clean(env, result, *tokens: str) -> None:
+    blob = (result.output or "") + env.home_files_text()
+    for token in tokens:
+        assert token not in blob
+        for argv in env.argv_calls():
+            assert all(token not in part for part in argv)
+
+
+def test_should_register_auth_group_on_main_cli():
+    from dopemux.cli import cli
+
+    assert "auth" in cli.commands
+    assert set(cli.commands["auth"].commands) == {"issue", "rotate", "revoke", "list"}
+
+
+def test_should_print_dry_run_plan_and_change_nothing_by_default(env):
+    result = run("issue", "runner:codex")
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    assert "dry run" in result.output.lower()
+    assert "--apply" in result.output
+    assert not env.auth_dir.exists()
+    assert env.argv_calls() == []
+    assert env.keychain() == {}
+
+
+def test_should_note_unknown_principal_in_dry_run(env):
+    result = run("issue", "runner:grok")
+    assert result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "not in the known 2A principal list" in result.output
+    known = run("issue", "runner:codex")
+    assert "not in the known" not in known.output
+
+
+def test_should_reject_invalid_principal(env):
+    result = run("issue", "codex", "--apply")
+    assert result.exit_code != 0
+    assert env.argv_calls() == []
+
+
+def test_should_issue_token_store_hash_and_hide_token(env):
+    result = run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    token = env.keychain_token("runner:codex")
+    assert json.loads(env.hash_file.read_text()) == {hash_token(token): "runner:codex"}
+    assert_clean(env, result, token)
+    assert hash_token(token) not in result.output
+
+
+def test_should_not_log_token_when_issuing(env, caplog):
+    caplog.set_level(logging.DEBUG)
+    result = run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    token = env.keychain_token("runner:codex")
+    assert token not in caplog.text and token not in result.output
+
+
+def test_should_refuse_second_issue_for_same_principal(env):
+    assert run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply").exit_code == 0
+    first = env.keychain_token("runner:codex")
+    again = run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    assert again.exit_code != 0
+    assert "rotate" in again.output
+    assert env.keychain_token("runner:codex") == first
+
+
+def test_should_keep_other_principals_when_issuing(env):
+    run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    run("issue", "runner:claude", "--apply")
+    hashes = json.loads(env.hash_file.read_text())
+    assert sorted(hashes.values()) == ["runner:claude", "runner:codex"]
+
+
+def test_should_not_clobber_unreadable_hash_file_on_issue(env):
+    env.auth_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
+    env.hash_file.write_text("{oops", encoding="utf-8")
+    result = run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    assert result.exit_code != 0
+    assert env.hash_file.read_text() == "{oops"
+    assert env.keychain() == {}
+
+
+def test_should_leave_no_hash_when_keychain_write_fails(env):
+    env.set_mode("write_fail")
+    result = run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    assert result.exit_code != 0
+    assert not env.hash_file.exists()
+
+
+def test_should_rotate_replacing_old_hash(env):
+    run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    run("issue", "runner:claude", "--apply")
+    old = env.keychain_token("runner:codex")
+    result = run("rotate", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    new = env.keychain_token("runner:codex")
+    assert new != old
+    hashes = json.loads(env.hash_file.read_text())
+    assert hash_token(old) not in hashes
+    assert hashes[hash_token(new)] == "runner:codex"
+    assert sorted(hashes.values()) == ["runner:claude", "runner:codex"]
+    assert_clean(env, result, old, new)
+
+
+def test_should_rotate_dry_run_change_nothing(env):
+    run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    before_token = env.keychain_token("runner:codex")
+    before_file = env.hash_file.read_text()
+    result = run("rotate", "runner:codex")
+    assert result.exit_code == 0 and "dry run" in result.output.lower()
+    assert env.keychain_token("runner:codex") == before_token
+    assert env.hash_file.read_text() == before_file
+
+
+def test_should_refuse_rotate_for_unissued_principal(env):
+    result = run("rotate", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    assert result.exit_code != 0
+    assert "issue" in result.output
+    assert env.keychain() == {}
+

+def test_should_revoke_hash_metadata_and_keychain_item(env):
+    run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    run("issue", "runner:claude", "--apply")
+    result = run("revoke", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    assert list(json.loads(env.hash_file.read_text()).values()) == ["runner:claude"]
+    assert "dopemux-mcp|runner:codex" not in env.keychain()
+    assert "runner:codex" not in run("list").output

+
+def test_should_revoke_dry_run_change_nothing(env):
+    run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    result = run("revoke", "runner:codex")
+    assert result.exit_code == 0 and "dry run" in result.output.lower()

+    assert len(json.loads(env.hash_file.read_text())) == 1
+

+def test_should_revoke_hash_even_when_keychain_item_already_gone(env):

+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    assert json.loads(env.hash_file.read_text()) == {}
+    assert "already" in result.output.lower()
+
+

+    run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")

+    result = run("list")
+    assert result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "runner:codex" in result.output
+    assert re.search(r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}", result.output)
+    assert not HEX64.search(result.output)
+    assert_clean(env, result, token)
+
+
+def test_should_list_nothing_gracefully(env):
+    result = run("list")
+    assert result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "no principals" in result.output.lower()
+
+
+def test_should_keep_creation_date_across_rotation(env):
+    run("issue", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    meta = env.auth_dir / "memory-token-meta.json"
+    data = json.loads(meta.read_text())
+    created = data["runner:codex"]["created"]
+    run("rotate", "runner:codex", "--apply")
+    data = json.loads(meta.read_text())
+    assert data["runner:codex"]["created"] == created
+    assert data["runner:codex"]["rotated"]
+    assert not HEX64.search(meta.read_text()), "metadata sidecar must carry no hashes"

+"""Helper-module tests (keychain + hash file) for ``dopemux.auth``."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import base64
+import hashlib
+import json
+import logging
+import os
+import re

+
+import pytest
+
+from dopemux.auth import (
+    InvalidPrincipalError,
+    KeychainError,
+    TokenNotFoundError,
+    generate_token,
+    get_token,
+    hash_token,
+    is_known_principal,
+    validate_principal,
+)

+def test_should_accept_only_runner_and_internal_principals():
+    for good in ("runner:codex", "internal:bridge-proxy", "runner:a", "internal:x_1.y-2"):
+        assert validate_principal(good) == good
+    for bad in ("", "codex", "Runner:codex", "runner:", "runner:-x", "other:x", "runner:a b", "runner:" + "a" * 65):
+        with pytest.raises(InvalidPrincipalError):
+            validate_principal(bad)
+
+
+def test_should_flag_principals_outside_the_known_2a_list():
+    assert is_known_principal("runner:claude")
+    assert is_known_principal("internal:brain")
+    assert not is_known_principal("runner:grok")
+
+
+def test_should_generate_32_random_bytes_as_base64url():
+    a, b = generate_token(), generate_token()
+    assert a != b
+    assert not any(generate_token().startswith("-") for _ in range(300))
+    assert re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]+", a)
+    assert len(base64.urlsafe_b64decode(a + "=" * (-len(a) % 4))) == 32
+
+
+def test_should_hash_token_as_lowercase_hex_sha256_of_utf8():
+    assert hash_token("abc") == hashlib.sha256(b"abc").hexdigest()
+
+
+def test_should_store_token_without_putting_it_in_argv_and_read_it_back(env):
+    token = generate_token()
+    keychain.store_token("runner:codex", token)
+    assert env.keychain_token("runner:codex") == token
+    for argv in env.argv_calls():
+        assert all(token not in part for part in argv)
+    assert env.argv_calls()[0] == ["-i"]
+    assert get_token("runner:codex") == token
+    # one read-back inside store_token, one explicit get_token: both with stdin closed
+    assert env.stdin_lens() == [0, 0], "reads must run with stdin closed"
+    read_argv = env.argv_calls()[-1]
+    assert read_argv[:1] == ["find-generic-password"]
+    assert "dopemux-mcp" in read_argv and "runner:codex" in read_argv and "-w" in read_argv
+
+
+def test_should_raise_typed_error_when_item_absent(env):
+    with pytest.raises(TokenNotFoundError) as exc:
+        get_token("runner:codex")
+    assert "runner:codex" in str(exc.value)
+    assert "dopemux auth issue" in str(exc.value)
+
+
+def test_should_fail_hard_and_name_cause_when_keychain_locked(env):
+    env.set_mode("locked")
+    with pytest.raises(KeychainError) as exc:
+        get_token("runner:codex")
+    msg = str(exc.value).lower()
+    assert "locked" in msg and "login session" in msg
+    assert len(env.argv_calls()) == 1, "never retry into a prompt"
+
+
+def test_should_fail_hard_on_empty_output(env):
+    env.set_mode("empty")
+    with pytest.raises(KeychainError):
+        get_token("runner:codex")
+
+
+def test_should_fail_hard_on_timeout(env):
+    env.set_mode("hang")
+    env.monkeypatch.setattr(keychain, "TIMEOUT_SECONDS", 0.5)
+    with pytest.raises(KeychainError) as exc:
+        get_token("runner:codex")
+    assert "timed out" in str(exc.value)
+
+
+def test_should_fail_when_security_binary_missing(env):
+    env.monkeypatch.setattr(keychain, "SECURITY_BIN", "/nonexistent/security")
+    with pytest.raises(KeychainError):
+        get_token("runner:codex")
+
+
+def test_should_fail_when_write_fails_and_not_leak_token(env):
+    env.set_mode("write_fail")
+    token = generate_token()
+    with pytest.raises(KeychainError) as exc:
+        keychain.store_token("runner:codex", token)
+    assert token not in str(exc.value)
+
+
+def test_should_fail_when_readback_differs_after_write(env):
+    # security -i may exit 0 even if the add failed; the helper verifies.
+    env.set_mode("empty")
+    with pytest.raises(KeychainError):
+        keychain.store_token("runner:codex", generate_token())
+
+
+def test_should_refuse_invalid_principal_before_calling_security(env):
+    with pytest.raises(InvalidPrincipalError):
+        get_token("nope")
+    assert env.argv_calls() == []
+
+
+def test_should_not_log_token_or_hash(env, caplog):
+    caplog.set_level(logging.DEBUG)
+    token = generate_token()
+    keychain.store_token("runner:codex", token)
+    get_token("runner:codex")
+    assert token not in caplog.text
+    assert hash_token(token) not in caplog.text
+
+
+def test_should_write_hash_file_atomically_with_private_modes(env):
+    h = hash_token("t1")
+    hashfile.write_hashes({h: "runner:codex"})
+    path = hashfile.hash_file_path()
+    assert path == env.hash_file
+    assert stat.S_IMODE(path.stat().st_mode) == 0o600
+    assert stat.S_IMODE(path.parent.stat().st_mode) == 0o700
+    assert json.loads(path.read_text()) == {h: "runner:codex"}
+    assert sorted(p.name for p in path.parent.iterdir()) == ["memory-token-hashes.json"]
+
+
+def test_should_tighten_existing_directory_mode(env):
+    env.auth_dir.mkdir(parents=True, mode=0o755)
+    os.chmod(env.auth_dir, 0o755)
+    hashfile.write_hashes({})
+    assert stat.S_IMODE(env.auth_dir.stat().st_mode) == 0o700
+
+
+def test_should_leave_no_temp_file_when_write_fails(env, monkeypatch):
+    hashfile.write_hashes({hash_token("a"): "runner:codex"})
+    before = env.hash_file.read_text()
+
+    def boom(*_a, **_k):
+        raise OSError("disk full")
+
+    with monkeypatch.context() as scoped:
+        scoped.setattr(os, "replace", boom)
+        with pytest.raises(OSError):
+            hashfile.write_hashes({hash_token("b"): "runner:claude"})
+    assert env.hash_file.read_text() == before
+    assert [p.name for p in env.auth_dir.iterdir()] == ["memory-token-hashes.json"]
+
+
+def test_should_refuse_to_overwrite_unreadable_hash_file(env):
+    env.auth_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
+    env.hash_file.write_text("{not json", encoding="utf-8")
+    with pytest.raises(hashfile.HashFileError):
+        hashfile.load_hashes()
+    assert env.hash_file.read_text() == "{not json"
+
+
+def test_should_load_missing_hash_file_as_empty(env):
+    assert hashfile.load_hashes() == {}

+"""Fixtures for the W04 bridge tests: an in-process fake streamable-HTTP MCP server.
+
+The fake listens on an ephemeral 127.0.0.1 port inside the test process. It is
+not a live service: no container, no keychain, no real token. Every token in
+these tests is a throwaway value generated here.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+import json
+import secrets
+import threading
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
+
+import pytest
+
+from dopemux.mcp import http_stdio_bridge as bridge
+
+SESSION_ID = "fake-session-1"
+
+# What the fake server advertises in tools/list. Schemas decide which ids the
+# bridge may fill; the bridge must never carry its own list of tool names.
+DOPE_MEMORY_TOOLS: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [
+    {
+        "name": "memory_store",
+        "inputSchema": {
+            "type": "object",
+            "properties": {
+                "workspace_id": {"type": "string"},
+                "instance_id": {"type": "string"},
+                "text": {"type": "string"},
+            },
+            "required": ["text"],
+        },
+    },
+    {
+        "name": "memory_search",
+        "inputSchema": {
+            "type": "object",
+            "properties": {"query": {"type": "string"}},
+        },
+    },
+]
+
+CONPORT_TOOLS: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [
+    {
+        "name": "update_context",
+        "inputSchema": {
+            "type": "object",
+            "properties": {
+                "workspace_id": {"type": "string"},
+                "instance_id": {"type": "string"},
+                "patch": {"type": "object"},
+            },
+        },
+    },
+    {
+        "name": "save_custom_data",
+        "inputSchema": {
+            "type": "object",
+            "properties": {
+                "workspace_id": {"type": "string"},
+                "category": {"type": "string"},
+            },
+        },
+    },
+    {
+        "name": "fork_instance",
+        "inputSchema": {
+            "type": "object",
+            "properties": {
+                "workspace_id": {"type": "string"},
+                "target_instance": {"type": "string"},
+            },
+            "required": ["target_instance"],
+        },
+    },
+]
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Recorded:
+    method: str
+    path: str
+    headers: Dict[str, str]
+    body: Any
+
+
+@dataclass
+class FakeServer:
+    """Shared state for the fake; the handler reads and writes it."""
+
+    token: str
+    tools: List[Dict[str, Any]]
+    sse_responses: bool = True
+    auth_status: Optional[int] = None  # force 401/403 on every request
+    page_tools: bool = False
+    sessions: set = field(default_factory=set)
+    requests: List[Recorded] = field(default_factory=list)
+    port: int = 0
+    _httpd: Optional[ThreadingHTTPServer] = None
+    _lock: threading.Lock = field(default_factory=threading.Lock)
+
+    def expire_sessions(self) -> None:
+        with self._lock:
+            self.sessions.clear()
+
+    def bodies(self, method: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+        out = [r.body for r in self.requests if isinstance(r.body, dict)]
+        if method is not None:
+            out = [b for b in out if b.get("method") == method]
+        return out
+
+
+def _make_handler(state: FakeServer):
+    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+        protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
+
+        def log_message(self, *args: Any) -> None:  # silence server logging
+            return
+
+        def _send(self, status: int, body: bytes = b"", headers: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None) -> None:
+            self.send_response(status)
+            for key, value in (headers or {}).items():
+                self.send_header(key, value)
+            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
+            self.end_headers()
+            self.wfile.write(body)
+
+        def _reply(self, message: Dict[str, Any], headers: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None) -> None:
+            payload = json.dumps(message).encode("utf-8")
+            if state.sse_responses:
+                body = b"event: message\ndata: " + payload + b"\n\n"
+                ctype = "text/event-stream"
+            else:
+                body = payload
+                ctype = "application/json"
+            self._send(200, body, {"Content-Type": ctype, **(headers or {})})
+
+        def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
+            length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
+            raw = self.rfile.read(length)
+            try:
+                body = json.loads(raw)
+            except ValueError:
+                body = None
+            hdrs = {k.lower(): v for k, v in self.headers.items()}
+            state.requests.append(Recorded("POST", self.path, hdrs, body))
+
+            if state.auth_status in (401, 403):
+                extra = {"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"} if state.auth_status == 401 else {}
+                self._send(state.auth_status, b'{"error":"denied"}', extra)
+                return
+            if hdrs.get("authorization") != f"Bearer {state.token}":
+                self._send(401, b'{"error":"unauthenticated"}', {"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"})
+                return
+            accept = hdrs.get("accept", "")
+            if "application/json" not in accept or "text/event-stream" not in accept:
+                self._send(406, b"Not Acceptable")
+                return
+            if self.path != "/mcp" or not isinstance(body, dict):
+                self._send(404, b"not found")
+                return
+
+            method = body.get("method")
+            if method == "initialize":
+                state.sessions.add(SESSION_ID)
+                self._reply(
+                    {
+                        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
+                        "id": body.get("id"),
+                        "result": {
+                            "protocolVersion": "2025-03-26",
+                            "capabilities": {"tools": {}},
+                            "serverInfo": {"name": "fake", "version": "0"},
+                        },
+                    },
+                    {"mcp-session-id": SESSION_ID},
+                )
+                return
+            sid = hdrs.get("mcp-session-id")
+            if sid is None:
+                self._send(400, b"Bad Request: Missing session ID")
+                return
+            if sid not in state.sessions:
+                self._send(404, b"Session not found")
+                return
+            if "id" not in body:  # notification
+                self._send(202)
+                return
+            if method == "tools/list":
+                tools = state.tools
+                result: Dict[str, Any] = {"tools": tools}
+                if state.page_tools:
+                    cursor = (body.get("params") or {}).get("cursor")
+                    if cursor is None:
+                        result = {"tools": tools[:1], "nextCursor": "p2"}
+                    else:
+                        result = {"tools": tools[1:]}
+                self._reply({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": body["id"], "result": result})
+                return
+            if method == "tools/call":
+                args = (body.get("params") or {}).get("arguments")
+                self._reply(
+                    {
+                        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
+                        "id": body["id"],
+                        "result": {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": json.dumps(args, sort_keys=True)}]},
+                    }
+                )
+                return
+            self._reply({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": body["id"], "result": {}})
+
+    return Handler
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def fake_token() -> str:
+    return "fake-" + secrets.token_urlsafe(24)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def fake_server(fake_token: str):
+    state = FakeServer(token=fake_token, tools=DOPE_MEMORY_TOOLS)
+    httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), _make_handler(state))
+    httpd.daemon_threads = True
+    state.port = httpd.server_address[1]
+    state._httpd = httpd
+    thread = threading.Thread(target=httpd.serve_forever, kwargs={"poll_interval": 0.01}, daemon=True)
+    thread.start()
+    try:
+        yield state
+    finally:
+        httpd.shutdown()
+        httpd.server_close()
+        thread.join(timeout=5)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def fake_password() -> str:
+    return "pw-" + secrets.token_urlsafe(18)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def repo(tmp_path: Path, fake_server: FakeServer, fake_password: str, monkeypatch) -> Path:
+    """A fake checkout (has .git) whose envrc points at the fake server."""
+    root = tmp_path / "checkout"
+    root.mkdir()
+    (root / ".git").write_text("gitdir: /nonexistent\n")
+    (root / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp").write_text(
+        "\n".join(
+            [
+                "# Generated by dopemux mcp start",
+                f"CONPORT_MCP_PORT={fake_server.port}",
+                f"DOPE_MEMORY_PORT={fake_server.port}",
+                "DOPE_MEMORY_WORKSPACE_ID=wt-memory-ws",
+                "DOPE_MEMORY_INSTANCE_ID=wt-memory-inst",
+                "DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID=/abs/conport/ws",
+                "DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID=wt-conport-inst",
+                f"POSTGRES_PASSWORD={fake_password}",
+                f"export DATABASE_URL=postgresql://u:{fake_password}@localhost/db",
+                "",
+            ]
+        )
+    )
+    monkeypatch.chdir(root)
+    return root
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def tokens(monkeypatch, fake_token: str):
+    """Monkeypatch the W03 helper; records which principals were requested."""
+    asked: List[str] = []
+
+    def _get_token(principal: str) -> str:
+        asked.append(principal)
+        return fake_token
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(bridge, "get_token", _get_token)
+    return asked
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Run:
+    code: int
+    stdout: str
+    stderr: str
+
+    def lines(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+        return [json.loads(line) for line in self.stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()]
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def run_bridge(monkeypatch, capsys):
+    """Run ``bridge.main(argv)`` with the given JSON-RPC messages on stdin."""
+
+    def _run(argv: List[str], messages: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Run:
+        stdin = "".join(json.dumps(m) + "\n" for m in messages)
+        monkeypatch.setattr("sys.stdin", io.StringIO(stdin))
+        capsys.readouterr()  # drop anything from earlier in the test
+        code = bridge.main(argv)
+        out = capsys.readouterr()
+        return Run(code=code, stdout=out.out, stderr=out.err)
+
+    return _run
+
+
+def init_msg(msg_id: int = 1) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return {
+        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
+        "id": msg_id,
+        "method": "initialize",
+        "params": {
+            "protocolVersion": "2025-03-26",
+            "capabilities": {},
+            "clientInfo": {"name": "test", "version": "0"},
+        },
+    }
+
+
+def initialized_msg() -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "method": "notifications/initialized"}
+
+
+def call_msg(msg_id: int, name: str, arguments: Any = None, *, omit_arguments: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    params: Dict[str, Any] = {"name": name}
+    if not omit_arguments:
+        params["arguments"] = arguments
+    return {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": msg_id, "method": "tools/call", "params": params}
+
+
+def list_msg(msg_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": msg_id, "method": "tools/list"}

+"""B01 / B05: envrc discovery reads only what it needs and fails closed."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import logging
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from dopemux.mcp import http_stdio_bridge as bridge
+
+from .conftest import call_msg, init_msg
+
+ARGV_MEMORY = ["--server", "dope-memory", "--principal", "runner:codex"]
+ARGV_CONPORT = ["--server", "conport", "--principal", "runner:codex"]
+
+
+def test_should_find_envrc_in_the_working_directory(repo: Path):
+    found = bridge.discover_profile("dope-memory", repo)
+
+    assert found.envrc_path == repo / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp"
+    assert found.base_url.startswith("http://127.0.0.1:")
+    assert found.base_url.endswith(str(found.port))
+
+
+def test_should_walk_up_from_a_subdirectory_to_the_nearest_envrc(repo: Path):
+    deep = repo / "src" / "pkg"
+    deep.mkdir(parents=True)
+
+    found = bridge.discover_profile("conport", deep)
+
+    assert found.envrc_path == repo / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp"
+
+
+def test_should_use_the_port_key_of_the_selected_server(tmp_path: Path):
+    root = tmp_path / "r"
+    root.mkdir()
+    (root / ".git").mkdir()
+    (root / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp").write_text("DOPE_MEMORY_PORT=3054\nCONPORT_MCP_PORT=3007\n")
+
+    assert bridge.discover_profile("dope-memory", root).port == 3054
+    assert bridge.discover_profile("conport", root).port == 3007
+
+
+def test_should_use_each_servers_own_id_keys(repo: Path):
+    memory = bridge.discover_profile("dope-memory", repo)
+    conport = bridge.discover_profile("conport", repo)
+
+    assert dict(memory.ids) == {"workspace_id": "wt-memory-ws", "instance_id": "wt-memory-inst"}
+    assert dict(conport.ids) == {"workspace_id": "/abs/conport/ws", "instance_id": "wt-conport-inst"}
+
+
+def test_should_fail_closed_when_no_envrc_exists(tmp_path: Path):
+    root = tmp_path / "bare"
+    root.mkdir()
+    (root / ".git").mkdir()
+
+    with pytest.raises(bridge.BridgeStartupError) as err:
+        bridge.discover_profile("dope-memory", root)
+
+    assert "dopemux mcp init" in str(err.value)
+
+
+def test_should_not_cross_into_another_checkouts_envrc(tmp_path: Path):
+    """A worktree under <main>/.worktrees/ has its own .git file and no envrc."""
+    main = tmp_path / "main"
+    main.mkdir()
+    (main / ".git").mkdir()
+    (main / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp").write_text("DOPE_MEMORY_PORT=3054\n")
+    worktree = main / ".worktrees" / "feature"
+    sub = worktree / "src"
+    sub.mkdir(parents=True)
+    (worktree / ".git").write_text("gitdir: /elsewhere\n")
+
+    with pytest.raises(bridge.BridgeStartupError) as err:
+        bridge.discover_profile("dope-memory", sub)
+
+    assert "dopemux mcp init" in str(err.value)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("line", ["", "DOPE_MEMORY_PORT=", "DOPE_MEMORY_PORT=abc", "DOPE_MEMORY_PORT=70000"])
+def test_should_fail_closed_on_a_missing_or_invalid_port(tmp_path: Path, line: str):
+    root = tmp_path / "r"
+    root.mkdir()
+    (root / ".git").mkdir()
+    (root / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp").write_text(f"{line}\nCONPORT_MCP_PORT=3007\n")
+
+    with pytest.raises(bridge.BridgeStartupError) as err:
+        bridge.discover_profile("dope-memory", root)
+
+    assert "DOPE_MEMORY_PORT" in str(err.value)
+
+
+def test_should_not_stop_at_the_first_envrc_that_lacks_the_port(tmp_path: Path):
+    """Nearest file wins; a missing port there never falls through to an outer file."""
+    outer = tmp_path / "outer"
+    inner = outer / "inner"
+    inner.mkdir(parents=True)
+    (outer / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp").write_text("DOPE_MEMORY_PORT=3054\n")
+    (inner / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp").write_text("CONPORT_MCP_PORT=3007\n")
+
+    with pytest.raises(bridge.BridgeStartupError):
+        bridge.discover_profile("dope-memory", inner)
+

+def test_should_never_keep_or_expose_the_database_password(repo: Path, fake_password: str, monkeypatch):
+    before = dict(os.environ)
+
+    found = bridge.discover_profile("dope-memory", repo)
+
+    assert fake_password not in repr(found)
+    assert fake_password not in repr(vars(found))
+    assert dict(os.environ) == before
+    assert not any("PASSWORD" in key or "DATABASE_URL" in key for key in os.environ if key not in before)

+def test_should_fail_closed_when_the_keychain_has_no_token(repo: Path, fake_server, run_bridge, monkeypatch):
+    from dopemux.auth import TokenNotFoundError
+
+    def _missing(principal: str) -> str:
+        raise TokenNotFoundError(f"no keychain item for {principal}")

+    monkeypatch.setattr(bridge, "get_token", _missing)

+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(5)])

+    reply = result.lines()[0]

+    from dopemux.auth import KeychainError
+
+    def _locked(principal: str) -> str:
+        raise KeychainError("keychain locked")
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(bridge, "get_token", _locked)
+
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(5)])
+
+    assert result.code == bridge.EXIT_STARTUP
+    assert "keychain locked" in result.stderr
+    assert fake_server.requests == []

+def test_should_fail_closed_on_an_invalid_principal(repo: Path, fake_server, run_bridge, monkeypatch):
+    from dopemux.auth import keychain
+
+    def _no_keychain(*args, **kwargs):
+        raise AssertionError("the keychain must not be reached for an invalid principal")
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(keychain, "_run", _no_keychain)
+
+    result = run_bridge(["--server", "dope-memory", "--principal", "not-a-principal"], [init_msg(5)])
+
+    assert result.code == bridge.EXIT_STARTUP
+    assert fake_server.requests == []
+    assert result.lines()[0]["error"]["code"] == bridge.SERVER_ERROR_CODE

+"""B03: streamable-HTTP handshake, bearer on every request, 401/403 stop."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import logging
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from dopemux.mcp import http_stdio_bridge as bridge

+
+ARGV_MEMORY = ["--server", "dope-memory", "--principal", "runner:codex"]
+
+
+def test_should_post_to_slash_mcp_with_both_accept_types(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(1)])
+
+    [req] = fake_server.requests
+    assert req.path == "/mcp"
+    assert req.headers["accept"] == "application/json, text/event-stream"
+    assert req.headers["content-type"] == "application/json"
+
+
+def test_should_parse_an_sse_framed_initialize_response(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    fake_server.sse_responses = True
+
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(1)])
+
+    assert result.code == 0
+    [reply] = result.lines()
+    assert reply["id"] == 1
+    assert reply["result"]["serverInfo"]["name"] == "fake"
+
+
+def test_should_parse_a_plain_json_response(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    fake_server.sse_responses = False
+
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(1), list_msg(2)])
+
+    replies = result.lines()
+    assert [r["id"] for r in replies] == [1, 2]
+    assert replies[1]["result"]["tools"]
+
+
+def test_should_echo_the_session_id_on_later_requests(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(1), initialized_msg(), list_msg(2)])
+
+    first, second, third = fake_server.requests
+    assert "mcp-session-id" not in first.headers
+    assert second.headers["mcp-session-id"] == SESSION_ID
+    assert third.headers["mcp-session-id"] == SESSION_ID
+
+
+def test_should_print_nothing_for_notifications_initialized(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(1), initialized_msg()])
+
+    assert result.code == 0
+    assert [r["id"] for r in result.lines()] == [1]  # one line: the initialize reply
+    assert fake_server.bodies("notifications/initialized")
+
+
+def test_should_send_the_bearer_token_on_every_request(repo, fake_server, fake_token, tokens, run_bridge):
+    run_bridge(
+        ARGV_MEMORY,
+        [init_msg(1), initialized_msg(), list_msg(2), call_msg(3, "memory_store", {"text": "x"})],
+    )
+
+    assert len(fake_server.requests) >= 4
+    for req in fake_server.requests:
+        assert req.headers["authorization"] == f"Bearer {fake_token}"
+
+
+def test_should_never_send_a_request_without_authorization(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(1), list_msg(2)])
+
+    assert all("authorization" in r.headers for r in fake_server.requests)
+
+
+def test_should_not_leak_the_token_to_stdout_stderr_or_logs(
+    repo, fake_server, fake_token, tokens, run_bridge, caplog
+):
+    caplog.set_level(logging.DEBUG)
+
+    result = run_bridge(
+        ARGV_MEMORY,
+        [init_msg(1), initialized_msg(), list_msg(2), call_msg(3, "memory_store", {"text": "x"})],
+    )
+
+    assert fake_token not in result.stdout
+    assert fake_token not in result.stderr
+    assert fake_token not in caplog.text
+
+
+def test_should_reinitialize_once_when_the_session_has_expired(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge, monkeypatch):
+    # Expire the server session just before the third message reaches it.
+    original = bridge._StreamableClient.send
+    state = {"count": 0}
+
+    def _send(self, message):
+        state["count"] += 1
+        if state["count"] == 3:
+            fake_server.expire_sessions()
+        return original(self, message)
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(bridge._StreamableClient, "send", _send)
+
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(1), initialized_msg(), list_msg(2)])
+
+    assert result.code == 0
+    replies = result.lines()
+    assert [r["id"] for r in replies] == [1, 2]  # re-initialization never reaches the client
+    assert len(fake_server.bodies("initialize")) == 2
+    assert len(fake_server.bodies("notifications/initialized")) == 2
+    assert len(fake_server.bodies("tools/list")) == 2  # the expired attempt and the retry
+
+
+def test_should_return_a_jsonrpc_error_when_the_session_is_gone_and_no_initialize_was_seen(
+    repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge
+):
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [list_msg(2)])
+
+    [reply] = result.lines()
+    assert reply["id"] == 2
+    assert reply["error"]["code"] == bridge.SERVER_ERROR_CODE
+    assert result.code == 0
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", [401, 403])
+def test_should_stop_on_401_or_403_with_a_clear_stderr_line(
+    repo, fake_server, fake_token, tokens, run_bridge, status
+):
+    fake_server.auth_status = status
+
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(1), list_msg(2)])
+
+    assert result.code == bridge.EXIT_AUTH
+    assert str(status) in result.stderr
+    assert "runner:codex" in result.stderr
+    [reply] = result.lines()  # the failed request gets an error; the second is never sent
+    assert reply["id"] == 1
+    assert reply["error"]["code"] == bridge.SERVER_ERROR_CODE
+    assert len(fake_server.requests) == 1
+    assert fake_token not in result.stdout + result.stderr
+
+
+def test_should_stop_when_the_server_rejects_the_token(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge, monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setattr(bridge, "get_token", lambda principal: "fake-wrong-token-0000000000")
+
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(1), list_msg(2)])
+
+    assert result.code == bridge.EXIT_AUTH
+    assert len(fake_server.requests) == 1
+    assert "wrong-token" not in result.stdout + result.stderr

+def test_should_report_a_connection_error_as_a_jsonrpc_error(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch, tokens, run_bridge):

+    (root / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp").write_text("DOPE_MEMORY_PORT=1\n")  # nothing listens on port 1

+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, [init_msg(4)])

+    assert reply["id"] == 4
+    assert reply["error"]["message"] == "Bridge transport error"
+

+def test_should_report_parse_errors_without_contacting_the_server(repo, fake_server, tokens, monkeypatch, capsys):
+    import io
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr("sys.stdin", io.StringIO("not json\n[1]\n"))
+
+    code = bridge.main(ARGV_MEMORY)
+
+    out = capsys.readouterr().out.splitlines()
+    assert code == 0
+    assert '"code":-32700' in out[0]
+    assert '"code":-32600' in out[1]
+    assert fake_server.requests == []
+

+def test_should_not_follow_redirects_or_use_proxies():
