+"""B04 id filling (schema-driven, never overriding) and the unchanged --base-url mode."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import io
+import json
+import threading
+from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
+from typing import Any, Dict
+
+import pytest
+
+from dopemux.mcp import http_stdio_bridge as bridge
+
+from .conftest import CONPORT_TOOLS, call_msg, init_msg, initialized_msg, list_msg
+
+ARGV_MEMORY = ["--server", "dope-memory", "--principal", "runner:codex"]
+ARGV_CONPORT = ["--server", "conport", "--principal", "runner:codex"]
+
+
+def _echoed_args(result, msg_id: int) -> Any:
+    reply = next(r for r in result.lines() if r.get("id") == msg_id)
+    return json.loads(reply["result"]["content"][0]["text"])
+
+
+def _handshake():
+    return [init_msg(1), initialized_msg(), list_msg(2)]
+
+
+def test_should_fill_dope_memory_ids_from_its_own_keys(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, _handshake() + [call_msg(3, "memory_store", {"text": "hi"})])
+
+    assert _echoed_args(result, 3) == {
+        "text": "hi",
+        "workspace_id": "wt-memory-ws",
+        "instance_id": "wt-memory-inst",
+    }
+
+
+def test_should_fill_conport_ids_from_its_own_keys(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    fake_server.tools = CONPORT_TOOLS
+
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_CONPORT, _handshake() + [call_msg(3, "update_context", {"patch": {"a": 1}})])
+
+    assert _echoed_args(result, 3) == {
+        "patch": {"a": 1},
+        "workspace_id": "/abs/conport/ws",
+        "instance_id": "wt-conport-inst",
+    }
+
+
+def test_should_fill_only_the_ids_the_tool_schema_declares(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    fake_server.tools = CONPORT_TOOLS
+
+    result = run_bridge(
+        ARGV_CONPORT,
+        _handshake()
+        + [
+            call_msg(3, "save_custom_data", {"category": "c"}),  # workspace_id only
+            call_msg(4, "fork_instance", {"target_instance": "t"}),  # workspace_id + required target
+        ],
+    )
+
+    assert _echoed_args(result, 3) == {"category": "c", "workspace_id": "/abs/conport/ws"}
+    assert _echoed_args(result, 4) == {"target_instance": "t", "workspace_id": "/abs/conport/ws"}
+
+
+def test_should_leave_a_tool_without_id_parameters_untouched(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, _handshake() + [call_msg(3, "memory_search", {"query": "q"})])
+
+    assert _echoed_args(result, 3) == {"query": "q"}
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("supplied", ["caller-ws", "", None])
+def test_should_never_override_a_caller_supplied_value(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge, supplied):
+    result = run_bridge(
+        ARGV_MEMORY,
+        _handshake() + [call_msg(3, "memory_store", {"text": "hi", "workspace_id": supplied})],
+    )
+
+    echoed = _echoed_args(result, 3)
+    assert echoed["workspace_id"] == supplied
+    assert echoed["instance_id"] == "wt-memory-inst"  # the omitted one is still filled
+
+
+def test_should_create_the_arguments_object_when_the_caller_omitted_it(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, _handshake() + [call_msg(3, "memory_store", omit_arguments=True)])
+
+    assert _echoed_args(result, 3) == {"workspace_id": "wt-memory-ws", "instance_id": "wt-memory-inst"}
+
+
+def test_should_learn_schemas_from_the_server_not_from_a_built_in_list(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    fake_server.tools = [
+        {
+            "name": "brand_new_tool",
+            "inputSchema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"instance_id": {"type": "string"}}},
+        }
+    ]
+
+    result = run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, _handshake() + [call_msg(3, "brand_new_tool", {})])
+
+    assert _echoed_args(result, 3) == {"instance_id": "wt-memory-inst"}

+# --- tools/list shown to the client: filled ids are no longer required ---
+
+REQUIRED_TOOLS = [
+    {
+        "name": "write_thing",
+        "description": "keeps this exact text",
+        "inputSchema": {
+            "type": "object",
+            "properties": {
+                "workspace_id": {"type": "string"},
+                "instance_id": {"type": "string"},
+                "text": {"type": "string"},
+            },
+            "required": ["workspace_id", "text", "instance_id"],
+        },
+        "annotations": {"readOnlyHint": False},
+    },
+    {
+        "name": "no_ids",
+        "inputSchema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"q": {"type": "string"}}, "required": ["q"]},
+    },
+    {
+        "name": "ids_only_required",
+        "inputSchema": {
+            "type": "object",
+            "properties": {"workspace_id": {"type": "string"}},
+            "required": ["workspace_id"],
+        },
+    },
+]
+
+
+def _forwarded_tools(result, msg_id: int = 2):
+    reply = next(r for r in result.lines() if r.get("id") == msg_id)
+    return {t["name"]: t for t in reply["result"]["tools"]}
+
+
+def test_should_drop_filled_ids_from_required_in_the_forwarded_tool_list(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    fake_server.tools = REQUIRED_TOOLS
+
+    tools = _forwarded_tools(run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, _handshake()))
+
+    assert tools["write_thing"]["inputSchema"]["required"] == ["text"]
+    assert "required" not in tools["ids_only_required"]["inputSchema"]
+
+
+def test_should_keep_properties_and_every_other_field_of_the_forwarded_tool(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    fake_server.tools = REQUIRED_TOOLS
+
+    tools = _forwarded_tools(run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, _handshake()))
+
+    original = REQUIRED_TOOLS[0]
+    sent = tools["write_thing"]
+    assert sent["inputSchema"]["properties"] == original["inputSchema"]["properties"]
+    assert sent["inputSchema"]["type"] == "object"
+    assert {k: v for k, v in sent.items() if k != "inputSchema"} == {
+        k: v for k, v in original.items() if k != "inputSchema"
+    }
+    assert tools["ids_only_required"]["inputSchema"]["properties"] == {"workspace_id": {"type": "string"}}
+
+
+def test_should_leave_a_tool_without_ids_unchanged_in_the_forwarded_list(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    fake_server.tools = REQUIRED_TOOLS
+
+    tools = _forwarded_tools(run_bridge(ARGV_MEMORY, _handshake()))
+
+    assert tools["no_ids"] == REQUIRED_TOOLS[1]
+
+
+def test_should_keep_required_for_an_id_the_envrc_does_not_define(tmp_path, monkeypatch, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):
+    root = tmp_path / "r"

+def test_should_fill_and_be_accepted_when_the_model_omits_a_required_id(repo, fake_server, tokens, run_bridge):

+# --- compatibility: the --base-url mode ---
+
+
+class _PlainJsonRpc(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+    seen: list = []
+
+    def log_message(self, *args: Any) -> None:
+        return
+
+    def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
+        raw = self.rfile.read(int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0))
+        _PlainJsonRpc.seen.append({"headers": dict(self.headers), "path": self.path, "body": json.loads(raw)})
+        reply = json.dumps({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": json.loads(raw).get("id"), "result": {"ok": True}}).encode()
+        self.send_response(200)
+        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
+        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(reply)))
+        self.end_headers()
+        self.wfile.write(reply)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def plain_server():
+    _PlainJsonRpc.seen = []
+    httpd = HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), _PlainJsonRpc)
+    thread = threading.Thread(target=httpd.serve_forever, kwargs={"poll_interval": 0.01}, daemon=True)
+    thread.start()
+    yield f"http://127.0.0.1:{httpd.server_address[1]}"
+    httpd.shutdown()
+    httpd.server_close()
+    thread.join(timeout=5)
+
+
+def test_should_keep_base_url_mode_unchanged(plain_server, monkeypatch, capsys, tokens):
+    monkeypatch.setattr("sys.stdin", io.StringIO(json.dumps(init_msg(1)) + "\n"))
+
+    code = bridge.main(["--base-url", plain_server])
+
+    out = capsys.readouterr()
+    assert code == 0
+    assert json.loads(out.out) == {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "result": {"ok": True}}
+    [seen] = _PlainJsonRpc.seen
+    assert seen["path"] == "/mcp"
+    assert seen["headers"]["Accept"] == "application/json"
+    assert not any(k.lower() in {"authorization", "mcp-session-id"} for k in seen["headers"])
+    assert tokens == []  # the keychain is never consulted in --base-url mode
+
+
+def test_should_keep_base_url_transport_error_shape(monkeypatch, capsys):
+    monkeypatch.setattr("sys.stdin", io.StringIO(json.dumps(init_msg(9)) + "\n"))
+
+    code = bridge.main(["--base-url", "http://127.0.0.1:1"])
+
+    reply = json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)
+    assert code == 0
+    assert reply["id"] == 9
+    assert reply["error"]["code"] == bridge.SERVER_ERROR_CODE
+    assert reply["error"]["message"] == "Bridge transport error"
+    assert "detail" in reply["error"]["data"]
+
+
+def test_should_still_render_the_config_generator_command(tmp_path):
+    """The only in-repo caller renders `--base-url`; its argv must still parse."""
+    parser = bridge.build_parser()

+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "argv",
+    [
+        [],
+        ["--server", "dope-memory"],  # no principal
+        ["--principal", "runner:codex"],  # no server

+def test_should_reject_invalid_argument_combinations(argv, capsys):

+    assert exit_info.value.code == 2

+"""Fixtures for the W05 HTTP-caller tests.
+
+Nothing here is live: the "server" is a throwaway HTTP listener on an
+ephemeral 127.0.0.1 port inside the test process, and every token is a fake
+value generated in this file. ``dopemux.auth.get_token`` is always replaced,
+and the real ``/usr/bin/security`` path is blanked so that no test can reach
+the keychain even when a fixture forgets to patch ``get_token``.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import importlib
+import json
+import secrets
+import sys
+import threading
+from dataclasses import dataclass, field
+from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
+from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
+from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlsplit
+
+import pytest
+
+from dopemux.auth import KeychainError, TokenNotFoundError
+from dopemux.instance_state import InstanceStateManager
+
+# tests/conftest.py replaces save_instance_state with a no-op for every test
+# (it guards against writes to a live ConPort). Capture the real method now,
+# before any fixture runs, so the instance_state tests can aim it at the fake.
+_REAL_SAVE_INSTANCE_STATE = InstanceStateManager.save_instance_state
+
+# Obviously fake; generated per test session, never a real credential.
+FAKE_TOKEN = "fake-w05-" + secrets.token_urlsafe(18)
+
+_ID_ENV = (
+    "DOPEMUX_WORKSPACE_ID",
+    "DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID",
+    "DOPE_MEMORY_WORKSPACE_ID",
+    "DOPE_MEMORY_INSTANCE_ID",
+    "CONPORT_URL",
+    "DOPE_MEMORY_URL",
+)
+
+# One body that satisfies every caller's response parsing.
+_REPLY = {
+    "status": "logged",
+    "success": True,
+    "entry_id": "entry-1",
+    "decision": {"id": 1},
+    "progress": {"id": "p-1"},
+    "decision_id": "dec-1",
+    "items": [],
+    "count": 0,
+}
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Seen:
+    method: str
+    path: str
+    query: Dict[str, List[str]]
+    headers: Dict[str, str]
+    body: Optional[Any]
+
+    @property
+    def authorization(self) -> Optional[str]:
+        return self.headers.get("authorization")
+
+
+@dataclass
+class FakeServer:
+    url: str
+    port: int
+    requests: List[Seen] = field(default_factory=list)
+
+    def writes(self) -> List[Seen]:
+        return [r for r in self.requests if r.method != "GET"]
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def _isolate(monkeypatch):
+    """No ambient ids, and no path to the real keychain."""
+    for name in _ID_ENV:
+        monkeypatch.delenv(name, raising=False)
+    keychain = importlib.import_module("dopemux.auth.keychain")
+    monkeypatch.setattr(keychain, "SECURITY_BIN", "/nonexistent/security")
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def real_instance_state_save(monkeypatch):
+    """Undo the root guard for one test; the target is always the 127.0.0.1 fake."""
+    monkeypatch.setattr(InstanceStateManager, "save_instance_state", _REAL_SAVE_INSTANCE_STATE)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def fake_server():
+    store: List[Seen] = []
+
+    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+        def _handle(self) -> None:
+            length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
+            raw = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""
+            try:
+                body = json.loads(raw) if raw else None
+            except ValueError:
+                body = None
+            parts = urlsplit(self.path)
+            store.append(
+                Seen(
+                    method=self.command,
+                    path=parts.path,
+                    query=parse_qs(parts.query),
+                    headers={k.lower(): v for k, v in self.headers.items()},
+                    body=body,
+                )
+            )
+            payload = json.dumps(_REPLY).encode()
+            self.send_response(200)
+            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
+            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(payload)))
+            self.end_headers()
+            self.wfile.write(payload)
+
+        do_GET = do_POST = do_PUT = do_DELETE = do_PATCH = _handle
+
+        def log_message(self, *args: Any) -> None:  # keep test output quiet
+            pass
+
+    httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
+    port = httpd.server_address[1]
+    thread = threading.Thread(target=httpd.serve_forever, kwargs={"poll_interval": 0.01}, daemon=True)
+    thread.start()
+    try:
+        yield FakeServer(url=f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}", port=port, requests=store)
+    finally:
+        httpd.shutdown()
+        httpd.server_close()
+        thread.join(timeout=5)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def token_ok(monkeypatch):
+    """get_token returns the fake token; the list records which principals asked."""
+    asked: List[str] = []
+
+    def fake_get_token(principal: str) -> str:
+        asked.append(principal)
+        return FAKE_TOKEN
+
+    auth = importlib.import_module("dopemux.auth")
+    monkeypatch.setattr(auth, "get_token", fake_get_token)
+    return asked
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def token_missing(monkeypatch):
+    """get_token fails the way it does with no keychain item."""
+    asked: List[str] = []
+
+    def fake_get_token(principal: str) -> str:
+        asked.append(principal)
+        raise TokenNotFoundError(f"no dopemux-mcp keychain item for {principal}")
+
+    auth = importlib.import_module("dopemux.auth")
+    monkeypatch.setattr(auth, "get_token", fake_get_token)
+    return asked
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def token_keychain_down(monkeypatch):
+    """get_token raises KeychainError, as inside a Linux container."""
+
+    def fake_get_token(principal: str) -> str:
+        raise KeychainError("cannot run /usr/bin/security (FileNotFoundError); needs macOS")
+
+    auth = importlib.import_module("dopemux.auth")
+    monkeypatch.setattr(auth, "get_token", fake_get_token)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def no_auth_package(monkeypatch):
+    """`from dopemux.auth import ...` raises ImportError (a service image without it)."""
+    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "dopemux.auth", None)
+
+
+def assert_no_secret(caplog, capsys) -> None:
+    out = capsys.readouterr()
+    assert FAKE_TOKEN not in caplog.text
+    assert FAKE_TOKEN not in out.out
+    assert FAKE_TOKEN not in out.err
diff --git a/tests/unit/runner_memory_2a_r2_w05/keychain_guard_plugin.py b/tests/unit/runner_memory_2a_r2_w05/keychain_guard_plugin.py

index 000000000..a514408e1

+"""Optional pytest plugin: keep other suites off the real keychain.
+
+Once W05 lands, the existing tests of these writers reach `dopemux.auth.get_token`.
+On a Mac with issued tokens that would read the real keychain. Run those suites with
+
+    PYTHONPATH=tests/unit/runner_memory_2a_r2_w05 pytest -p keychain_guard_plugin ...
+
+and every `get_token` fails with KeychainError, which is the Linux-container case.
+"""
+
+import importlib
+
+import pytest
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def _no_real_keychain(monkeypatch):
+    keychain = importlib.import_module("dopemux.auth.keychain")
+    monkeypatch.setattr(keychain, "SECURITY_BIN", "/nonexistent/security")

+"""Host-side ConPort REST writers: tools/conport_client, instance_state,
+profile_analytics, decisions_commands, adhd/rte_adapter (all internal:cli,
+except rte_adapter which is internal:adhd)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import logging
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+
+import pytest
+from click.testing import CliRunner
+
+from dopemux import profile_analytics
+from dopemux.adhd import rte_adapter
+from dopemux.commands import decisions_commands
+from dopemux.instance_state import InstanceState, InstanceStateManager
+from dopemux.tools.conport_client import ConPortClient
+
+from .conftest import FAKE_TOKEN, assert_no_secret
+
+NO_TOKEN = ["token_missing", "token_keychain_down", "no_auth_package"]
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# src/dopemux/tools/conport_client.py  (internal:cli)
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+async def _tools_client_writes(client: ConPortClient):
+    context = {"focus": "x"}
+    await client.update_active_context("ws-bound", context)
+    await client.log_decision("ws-bound", "sum", "why", ["alt"])
+    await client.log_progress("ws-bound", "IN_PROGRESS", "doing", 10)
+    return context
+
+
+async def test_should_send_cli_token_and_ids_on_every_tools_client_write(
+    fake_server, token_ok, monkeypatch
+):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID", "inst-a")
+    client = ConPortClient(base_url=fake_server.url)
+    try:
+        context = await _tools_client_writes(client)
+    finally:
+        await client.close()
+
+    ctx, dec, prog = fake_server.requests
+    assert [r.path for r in (ctx, dec, prog)] == [
+        "/api/context/ws-bound",
+        "/api/decisions",
+        "/api/progress",
+    ]
+    for seen in (ctx, dec, prog):
+        assert seen.authorization == f"Bearer {FAKE_TOKEN}"
+        assert seen.headers["content-type"] == "application/json"
+        assert seen.body["instance_id"] == "inst-a"
+    assert dec.body["workspace_id"] == prog.body["workspace_id"] == "ws-bound"
+    assert ctx.body["focus"] == "x"
+    assert context == {"focus": "x"}, "the caller's dict must not be mutated"
+    assert set(token_ok) == {"internal:cli"}
+
+
+async def test_should_prefer_explicit_instance_id_on_tools_client(fake_server, token_ok, monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID", "from-env")
+    client = ConPortClient(base_url=fake_server.url, instance_id="from-ctor")
+    try:
+        await client.log_progress("ws-bound", "IN_PROGRESS", "doing", 0)
+    finally:
+        await client.close()
+
+    assert fake_server.requests[0].body["instance_id"] == "from-ctor"
+
+
+async def test_should_not_invent_an_instance_id_on_tools_client(fake_server, token_ok):
+    client = ConPortClient(base_url=fake_server.url)
+    try:
+        await client.log_progress("ws-bound", "IN_PROGRESS", "doing", 0)
+        await client.log_decision("ws-bound", "s", "r")
+    finally:
+        await client.close()

+def test_should_keep_the_original_post_call_shape_when_no_token(decisions_env, token_missing, monkeypatch):
+    """With no token the call is exactly as before: no `headers` argument at all."""
+    recorded = {}
+
+    class _Resp:
+        def raise_for_status(self):
+            return None
+
+        def json(self):
+            return {"status": "logged", "decision": {"id": "d-9"}}
+
+    def strict_post(url, *, json, timeout):  # the pre-W05 call shape
+        recorded["json"] = json
+        return _Resp()
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(decisions_commands.requests, "post", strict_post)
+
+    result = CliRunner().invoke(
+        decisions_commands.decisions, ["review", "decision-1", "--note", "ok"]
+    )
+
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    assert recorded["json"]["workspace_id"] == "ws-bound"
+

+        entry_id="e-1",
+        correction_type="summary",
+        corrected_summary="s2",
+    )

+"""scripts/proof_conport_mcp.py is not an HTTP writer.
+
+It builds an in-process server and calls ``mcp_endpoint`` directly with mock
+requests, so it bypasses the auth middleware and no bearer token can apply. W05
+only makes its tool-call payload carry the ids a ConPort progress write requires.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import ast
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SCRIPT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "scripts" / "proof_conport_mcp.py"
+
+
+def _tool_call_arguments() -> dict:
+    for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(SCRIPT.read_text())):
+        if isinstance(node, ast.Dict):
+            keys = [k.value for k in node.keys if isinstance(k, ast.Constant)]
+            if "name" in keys and "arguments" in keys:
+                arguments = node.values[keys.index("arguments")]
+                return ast.literal_eval(arguments)
+    raise AssertionError("no tools/call params literal found in the proof script")
+
+
+def test_should_carry_both_ids_in_the_progress_tool_call():
+    arguments = _tool_call_arguments()
+
+    assert arguments["workspace_id"]
+    assert arguments["instance_id"]
+
+
+def test_should_stay_an_in_process_script_without_http_calls():
+    source = SCRIPT.read_text()
+
+    assert "aiohttp" not in source and "requests" not in source and "httpx" not in source

+"""Service-side ConPort REST writers: services/adhd_engine/conport_client.py
+(internal:adhd), services/claude_brain/brain_manager.py (internal:brain) and the
+two services/dopecon-bridge files (internal:dopecon).
+
+These run in Linux containers where the macOS keychain does not exist, so each
+class below also proves the container case: `get_token` fails, the write is sent
+without a header, and nothing else changes.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import importlib
+import importlib.util
+import logging
+import sys
+import types
+from pathlib import Path
+from types import SimpleNamespace
+
+import pytest
+
+from .conftest import FAKE_TOKEN, assert_no_secret
+
+REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+SERVICES = REPO / "services"
+NO_TOKEN = ["token_missing", "token_keychain_down", "no_auth_package"]
+
+
+def _load(name: str, path: Path):
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
+    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
+    return module
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# services/adhd_engine/conport_client.py  (internal:adhd, container)
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def adhd_engine_module():
+    return _load("w05_adhd_engine_conport_client", SERVICES / "adhd_engine" / "conport_client.py")
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def adhd_engine_client(fake_server, adhd_engine_module):
+    return adhd_engine_module.ConPortClient(base_url=fake_server.url)
+
+
+async def _adhd_writes(client):
+    await client.update_active_context("ws-x", {"focus": "x"})
+    await client.log_decision("ws-x", "sum", "why")
+    await client.log_progress("ws-x", "IN_PROGRESS", "doing", 5)
+    await client.close()
+
+
+async def test_should_send_adhd_token_and_ids_from_adhd_engine(
+    fake_server, adhd_engine_module, token_ok, monkeypatch
+):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID", "inst-a")
+
+    await _adhd_writes(adhd_engine_module.ConPortClient(base_url=fake_server.url))
+
+    ctx, dec, prog = fake_server.requests
+    assert [r.path for r in (ctx, dec, prog)] == ["/api/context/ws-x", "/api/decisions", "/api/progress"]
+    for seen in (ctx, dec, prog):
+        assert seen.authorization == f"Bearer {FAKE_TOKEN}"
+        assert seen.body["instance_id"] == "inst-a"
+    assert dec.body["workspace_id"] == prog.body["workspace_id"] == "ws-x"
+    assert set(token_ok) == {"internal:adhd"}
+
+
+async def test_should_not_fetch_a_token_for_adhd_engine_reads(fake_server, adhd_engine_client, token_ok):
+    await adhd_engine_client.get_active_context("ws-x")
+    await adhd_engine_client.get_progress()
+    await adhd_engine_client.close()
+
+    assert all(r.authorization is None for r in fake_server.requests)
+    assert token_ok == []
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("fixture", NO_TOKEN)
+async def test_should_write_without_header_when_adhd_engine_has_no_token(
+    fake_server, adhd_engine_client, request, fixture, caplog, capsys
+):
+    request.getfixturevalue(fixture)

+        await _adhd_writes(adhd_engine_client)

+    assert all(r.authorization is None for r in fake_server.requests)
+    assert_no_secret(caplog, capsys)

+
+async def test_should_degrade_in_a_container_without_the_security_binary(
+    fake_server, adhd_engine_client, caplog
+):
+    """No patching of get_token: the real helper fails because /usr/bin/security is absent."""
+    with caplog.at_level(logging.DEBUG):
+        await _adhd_writes(adhd_engine_client)
+
+    assert len(fake_server.requests) == 3
+    assert all(r.authorization is None for r in fake_server.requests)
+    assert "internal:adhd" in caplog.text

+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# services/claude_brain/brain_manager.py  (internal:brain, container)
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+FAKE_API_KEY = "fake-conport-api-key"
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def brain(monkeypatch, fake_server):
+    """A manager built with stubbed siblings: config.py cannot import under pydantic 2."""
+    package = types.ModuleType("claude_brain")
+    package.__path__ = [str(SERVICES / "claude_brain")]
+    config = types.ModuleType("claude_brain.config")
+    config.settings = SimpleNamespace(
+        ADHD_ENGINE_URL="http://adhd.invalid",
+        ADHD_ENGINE_API_KEY="",
+        CONPORT_URL=fake_server.url,
+        CONPORT_API_KEY=FAKE_API_KEY,
+        SERENA_URL="http://serena.invalid",
+        SERENA_API_KEY="",
+    )
+    cache = types.ModuleType("claude_brain.cache_manager")
+    cache.CacheManager = type("CacheManager", (), {})
+    failure = types.ModuleType("claude_brain.failure_handler")
+    failure.FailureHandler = type("FailureHandler", (), {})
+    for name, module in {
+        "claude_brain": package,
+        "claude_brain.config": config,
+        "claude_brain.cache_manager": cache,
+        "claude_brain.failure_handler": failure,
+    }.items():
+        monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, name, module)
+
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
+        "claude_brain.brain_manager", SERVICES / "claude_brain" / "brain_manager.py"
+    )
+    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "claude_brain.brain_manager", module)
+    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
+    return module.ClaudeBrainManager()
+
+
+async def test_should_send_brain_token_and_ids_when_logging_a_decision(

+@pytest.fixture
+def bridge_client(monkeypatch, request, fake_server):
+    """clients.py with `core` stubbed: the real one needs sqlalchemy (a services extra)."""
+    monkeypatch.syspath_prepend(str(SERVICES / "dopecon-bridge"))
+    core = types.ModuleType("dopecon_bridge.core")
+    core.cache_manager = SimpleNamespace()
+    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "dopecon_bridge.core", core)
+    monkeypatch.delitem(sys.modules, "dopecon_bridge.clients", raising=False)
+    request.addfinalizer(lambda: sys.modules.pop("dopecon_bridge.clients", None))
+    clients = importlib.import_module("dopecon_bridge.clients")
+    client = clients.ConPortClient()
+    client.base_url = fake_server.url
+    return client

+async def _bridge_writes(client):
+    await client.save_custom_data({"workspace_id": "ws-x", "category": "c", "key": "k", "value": {}})
+    await client.claim_custom_data({"workspace_id": "ws-x", "category": "c", "key": "k", "value": {}})
+    await client.log_decision({"workspace_id": "ws-x", "summary": "s", "rationale": "r"})
+    await client.log_progress({"workspace_id": "ws-x", "description": "d"})
+    await client.close()
+
+
+async def test_should_send_dopecon_token_and_ids_on_bridge_writes(
+    fake_server, bridge_client, token_ok, monkeypatch
+):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_INSTANCE_ID", "inst-a")
+
+    await _bridge_writes(bridge_client)

+    assert [r.path for r in fake_server.requests] == [
+        "/api/custom_data",
+        "/api/custom_data/claim",
+        "/api/decisions",
+        "/api/progress",
+    ]
+    for seen in fake_server.requests:
+        assert seen.authorization == f"Bearer {FAKE_TOKEN}"
+        assert seen.body["workspace_id"] == "ws-x", "the caller's workspace is never overridden"
+    assert progress.body["instance_id"] == "inst-a"
+    assert decision.body["instance_id"] == "inst-a"
+    assert "instance_id" not in custom.body, "custom_data has no instance column"
+    assert set(token_ok) == {"internal:dopecon"}

+    assert payload == {"workspace_id": "ws-x", "description": "d", "instance_id": "from-caller"}

+"""Wave 2A R2 W06: local writers record self-asserted internal principals.
+
+Covers design 5.3 / operator ruling 3: capture_client.py (SQLite ledger) and
+native_hooks.py (Redis XADD) carry no token, so they record ``internal:cli`` or
+``internal:hooks`` with the self-asserted marker. All state lives in tmp dirs;
+Redis is a fake client.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import re
+import sqlite3
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from dopemux.claude import native_hooks
+from dopemux.memory import capture_client as capture_client_module
+from dopemux.memory.capture_client import (
+    CaptureError,
+    emit_capture_event,
+    emit_promotable_capture_event,
+    try_emit_promotable_capture_event,
+)
+
+REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+PRINCIPAL_RE = re.compile(r"^(runner|internal):[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]{0,63}$")
+
+
+def _row(ledger: Path, event_id: str) -> tuple:
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(ledger))
+    try:
+        return conn.execute(
+            "SELECT write_principal, write_principal_self_asserted "
+            "FROM raw_activity_events WHERE id = ?",
+            (event_id,),
+        ).fetchone()
+    finally:
+        conn.close()
+
+
+def _columns(ledger: Path) -> set[str]:
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(ledger))
+    try:
+        return {r[1] for r in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(raw_activity_events)")}
+    finally:
+        conn.close()
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def ledger(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> Path:
+    path = tmp_path / "chronicle.sqlite"
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DOPEMUX_CAPTURE_LEDGER_PATH", str(path))
+    monkeypatch.delenv("DOPEMUX_CAPTURE_EMIT_EVENTBUS", raising=False)
+    return path
+
+
+def _event(text: str) -> dict:
+    return {"event_type": "manual.note", "payload": {"summary": text}}
+
+
+def test_default_capture_records_internal_cli_self_asserted(ledger):
+    result = emit_capture_event(_event("cli default"), mode="cli", repo_root=REPO_ROOT)
+
+    assert _row(ledger, result.event_id) == ("internal:cli", 1)
+
+
+def test_explicit_hooks_principal_is_recorded_self_asserted(ledger):
+    result = emit_capture_event(
+        _event("from hooks"),
+        mode="plugin",
+        repo_root=REPO_ROOT,
+        principal="internal:hooks",
+    )
+
+    assert _row(ledger, result.event_id) == ("internal:hooks", 1)
+
+
+def test_promotable_wrappers_forward_principal(ledger):
+    direct = emit_promotable_capture_event(
+        "error.encountered",
+        {"message": "direct"},
+        source="dopemux.native_hooks",
+        mode="plugin",
+        repo_root=REPO_ROOT,
+        principal="internal:hooks",
+    )
+    best_effort = try_emit_promotable_capture_event(
+        "error.encountered",
+        {"message": "best effort"},
+        source="dopemux.native_hooks",
+        mode="plugin",
+        repo_root=REPO_ROOT,
+        principal="internal:hooks",
+    )
+    default = emit_promotable_capture_event(
+        "error.encountered",
+        {"message": "default"},
+        source="cli",
+        mode="cli",
+        repo_root=REPO_ROOT,
+    )
+
+    assert best_effort is not None
+    assert _row(ledger, direct.event_id) == ("internal:hooks", 1)
+    assert _row(ledger, best_effort.event_id) == ("internal:hooks", 1)
+    assert _row(ledger, default.event_id) == ("internal:cli", 1)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "bad",
+    [
+        "",
+        "unverified",
+        "hooks",
+        "internal:",
+        "internal:Hooks",
+        "internal:" + "a" * 65,
+        "runner:claude",  # local writers may only self-assert an internal principal
+        "internal:hooks; DROP TABLE raw_activity_events",
+    ],
+)
+def test_invalid_principal_is_rejected_and_writes_nothing(ledger, bad):
+    with pytest.raises(CaptureError):
+        emit_capture_event(
+            _event("bad principal"), mode="cli", repo_root=REPO_ROOT, principal=bad
+        )
+
+    assert not ledger.exists()
+
+
+def test_invalid_principal_makes_best_effort_wrapper_return_none(ledger):
+    result = try_emit_promotable_capture_event(
+        "error.encountered",
+        {"message": "x"},
+        source="dopemux.native_hooks",
+        mode="plugin",
+        repo_root=REPO_ROOT,
+        principal="not a principal",
+    )
+
+    assert result is None
+
+
+def test_event_dict_cannot_smuggle_a_principal(ledger):
+    event = _event("smuggle")
+    event["write_principal"] = "runner:claude"
+    event["write_principal_self_asserted"] = 0
+
+    result = emit_capture_event(event, mode="cli", repo_root=REPO_ROOT)
+
+    assert _row(ledger, result.event_id) == ("internal:cli", 1)
+
+
+def test_principal_stored_is_valid_per_ledger_contract(ledger):
+    result = emit_capture_event(_event("shape"), mode="cli", repo_root=REPO_ROOT)
+
+    principal, _ = _row(ledger, result.event_id)
+    assert PRINCIPAL_RE.match(principal)
+
+
+def test_event_stream_envelope_carries_principal(ledger, monkeypatch):
+    emitted: list[dict] = []
+    monkeypatch.setattr(capture_client_module, "_emit_to_event_stream", emitted.append)
+
+    emit_capture_event(
+        _event("bus"),
+        mode="plugin",
+        repo_root=REPO_ROOT,
+        emit_event_bus=True,
+        principal="internal:hooks",
+    )
+
+    assert len(emitted) == 1
+    assert emitted[0]["write_principal"] == "internal:hooks"
+
+
+def test_event_stream_xadd_has_principal_fields(ledger, monkeypatch):
+    class _Redis:
+        calls: list[tuple] = []
+
+        @classmethod
+        def from_url(cls, *_a, **_k):
+            return cls()
+
+        def xadd(self, stream, fields):
+            self.calls.append((stream, fields))
+
+    class _Module:
+        Redis = _Redis
+
+    monkeypatch.setitem(__import__("sys").modules, "redis", _Module)
+
+    emit_capture_event(
+        _event("xadd"), mode="cli", repo_root=REPO_ROOT, emit_event_bus=True
+    )
+
+    assert len(_Redis.calls) == 1
+    fields = _Redis.calls[0][1]
+    assert fields["write_principal"] == "internal:cli"
+    assert fields["write_principal_self_asserted"] == "1"
+

+# --- B03: ledger written before and after W01's migration -------------------
+
+
+def _make_pre_migration_ledger(path: Path) -> None:
+    """A ledger as written before W01: no principal columns, v1.2.1 recorded."""
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path))
+    try:
+        conn.executescript(
+            """
+            CREATE TABLE raw_activity_events (
+              id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL,
+              instance_id TEXT NOT NULL, session_id TEXT,
+              ts_utc TEXT NOT NULL, event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
+              source TEXT NOT NULL, payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
+              redaction_level TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'strict',
+              ttl_days INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 7,
+              created_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
+            );
+            CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (
+              version TEXT PRIMARY KEY, applied_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL
+            );
+            INSERT INTO schema_migrations VALUES ('v1.2.1', datetime('now'));
+            INSERT INTO raw_activity_events VALUES
+              ('legacy-1', 'ws', 'A', NULL, '2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00',
+               'manual.note', 'cli', '{}', 'strict', 7, '2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00');
+            """
+        )
+        conn.commit()
+    finally:
+        conn.close()
+
+
+def test_pre_migration_ledger_is_migrated_and_new_row_carries_principal(ledger):
+    _make_pre_migration_ledger(ledger)
+    assert "write_principal" not in _columns(ledger)
+
+    result = emit_capture_event(
+        _event("after migration"),
+        mode="plugin",
+        repo_root=REPO_ROOT,
+        principal="internal:hooks",
+    )
+
+    assert {"write_principal", "write_principal_self_asserted"} <= _columns(ledger)
+    assert _row(ledger, result.event_id) == ("internal:hooks", 1)
+    # Existing rows are not rewritten: they read as unverified and not self-asserted.
+    assert _row(ledger, "legacy-1") == ("unverified", 0)
+
+
+def test_ledger_written_after_migration_stays_writable_by_default_principal(ledger):
+    first = emit_capture_event(_event("one"), mode="cli", repo_root=REPO_ROOT)
+    capture_client_module._SCHEMA_READY_LEDGER_PATHS.discard(str(ledger.resolve()))
+    second = emit_capture_event(_event("two"), mode="cli", repo_root=REPO_ROOT)
+
+    assert _row(ledger, first.event_id) == ("internal:cli", 1)
+    assert _row(ledger, second.event_id) == ("internal:cli", 1)
+
+
+# --- native_hooks: Redis event + capture call -------------------------------
+
+
+class _FakeRedis:
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.xadd_calls = []
+
+    def xadd(self, stream, fields, maxlen=None, approximate=True):
+        self.xadd_calls.append({"stream": stream, "fields": fields})
+        return "1-0"
+
+    def close(self):
+        return None
+
+
+def test_native_hook_redis_event_carries_internal_hooks_principal(monkeypatch):
+    fake = _FakeRedis()
+    monkeypatch.setattr(native_hooks, "_open_activity_redis_client", lambda: fake)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(native_hooks, "should_emit_heartbeat", lambda *a, **k: True)
+
+    emitted = native_hooks._emit_activity_event(
+        hook_event_name="PreToolUse", status="attempt", tool_name="Read"
+    )
+
+    assert emitted is True
+    fields = fake.xadd_calls[0]["fields"]
+    assert fields["write_principal"] == "internal:hooks"
+    assert fields["write_principal_self_asserted"] == "1"
+    # Content-free payload is unchanged: the principal is an envelope field.
+    assert set(json.loads(fields["data"])) == {"hook_event_name", "status", "tool_name"}
+    assert "token" not in json.dumps(fields).lower()
+
+
+def test_native_hook_error_capture_passes_internal_hooks_principal(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    captured = []
+    monkeypatch.setattr(
+        native_hooks,
+        "try_emit_promotable_capture_event",
+        lambda event_type, payload, **kwargs: captured.append(kwargs),
+    )
+
+    native_hooks._emit_bounded_hook_error_capture(
+        project_root=tmp_path,
+        session_id=None,
+        hook_event_name="PostToolUseFailure",
+        tool_name="Bash",
+    )
+
+    assert captured and captured[0]["principal"] == "internal:hooks"
+
+
+def test_native_hook_error_capture_writes_hooks_principal_to_ledger(ledger, monkeypatch):
+    """End to end through the real capture_client (tmp ledger, no Redis)."""
+    monkeypatch.setattr(capture_client_module, "_emit_to_event_stream", lambda e: None)
+
+    native_hooks._emit_bounded_hook_error_capture(
+        project_root=REPO_ROOT,
+        session_id=None,
+        hook_event_name="PostToolUseFailure",
+        tool_name="Bash",
+    )
+
+    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(ledger))
+    try:
+        rows = conn.execute(
+            "SELECT write_principal, write_principal_self_asserted "
+            "FROM raw_activity_events WHERE event_type = 'error.encountered'"
+        ).fetchall()
+    finally:
+        conn.close()
+    assert rows == [("internal:hooks", 1)]
+

+# --- B02: fail-open is unchanged --------------------------------------------

+def test_native_hook_redis_failure_still_fails_open(monkeypatch):

+    )

+"""W07 B01/B02/B05: the real Codex gate, released servers and the stdio-route entries.
+
+No test here reads the keychain, starts a server or opens a socket: the gate's
+two seams (`fleet_catalog._fetch_health`, `fleet_catalog.get_token`) are
+monkeypatched, and every file lives under pytest's tmp_path.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import tomllib
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any, Dict
+
+import pytest
+from click.testing import CliRunner
+
+from dopemux.auth import KeychainError, TokenNotFoundError
+from dopemux.commands import mcp_commands
+from dopemux.mcp import fleet_catalog, http_stdio_bridge
+
+REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+FAKE_TOKEN = "fake-token-for-w07-tests-only-0123456789"  # generated for tests; not a real secret
+
+GOOD_MEMORY: Dict[str, Any] = {"auth_mode": "enforce", "identity_required_writes": True}
+GOOD_CONPORT: Dict[str, Any] = {
+    "auth_mode": "enforce",
+    "identity_required_writes": True,
+    "auth_principal_column": "ok",
+}
+MEMORY_PORT = 43120
+CONPORT_PORT = 43104
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# fixtures
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def checkout(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
+    root = tmp_path / "checkout"
+    root.mkdir()
+    (root / ".git").mkdir()
+    (root / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp").write_text(
+        f"export DOPE_MEMORY_PORT={MEMORY_PORT}\n"
+        f"export CONPORT_HTTP_PORT={CONPORT_PORT}\n"
+        "export POSTGRES_PASSWORD=fake-password-must-never-be-read\n"
+    )
+    return root
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def seams(monkeypatch):
+    """Healthy servers and a present token; tests overwrite pieces of `state`."""
+    state: Dict[str, Any] = {
+        "health": {MEMORY_PORT: dict(GOOD_MEMORY), CONPORT_PORT: dict(GOOD_CONPORT)},
+        "token": FAKE_TOKEN,
+        "probed_ports": [],
+        "token_principals": [],
+    }
+
+    def fake_fetch(port: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        state["probed_ports"].append(port)
+        value = state["health"][port]
+        if isinstance(value, Exception):
+            raise value
+        return value
+
+    def fake_get_token(principal: str) -> str:
+        state["token_principals"].append(principal)
+        if isinstance(state["token"], Exception):
+            raise state["token"]
+        return state["token"]
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(mcp_commands, "_fetch_health", fake_fetch)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(mcp_commands, "get_token", fake_get_token)
+    return state
+
+
+def _real_catalog() -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    return fleet_catalog.load_root_catalog(REPO_ROOT)
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# gate: each check failing alone, all passing
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def test_should_hold_when_both_servers_enforce_and_the_token_exists(checkout, seams):
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is True
+    assert gate.failures == ()
+    assert sorted(seams["probed_ports"]) == sorted([MEMORY_PORT, CONPORT_PORT])
+    assert seams["token_principals"] == ["runner:codex"]
+
+
+def test_should_never_carry_the_token_in_the_result(checkout, seams):
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert FAKE_TOKEN not in repr(gate)
+    assert "fake-password-must-never-be-read" not in repr(gate)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "server_port, key, value, needle",
+    [
+        (MEMORY_PORT, "auth_mode", "off", "dope-memory"),
+        (MEMORY_PORT, "auth_mode", "enforced", "dope-memory"),
+        (MEMORY_PORT, "auth_mode", None, "dope-memory"),
+        (MEMORY_PORT, "identity_required_writes", False, "dope-memory"),
+        (MEMORY_PORT, "identity_required_writes", "true", "dope-memory"),
+        (CONPORT_PORT, "auth_mode", "off", "conport"),
+        (CONPORT_PORT, "identity_required_writes", False, "conport"),
+        (CONPORT_PORT, "auth_principal_column", "missing", "conport"),
+        (CONPORT_PORT, "auth_principal_column", "partial", "conport"),
+    ],
+)
+def test_should_fail_when_one_health_field_is_wrong(checkout, seams, server_port, key, value, needle):
+    seams["health"][server_port][key] = value
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is False
+    assert len(gate.failures) == 1
+    assert needle in gate.failures[0]
+    assert key in gate.failures[0]
+
+
+def test_should_name_the_new_accepted_modes_when_auth_mode_is_off(checkout, seams):
+    seams["health"][MEMORY_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "off"
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is False
+    assert "auth_mode is 'off', expected 'log' or 'enforce'" in gate.failures[0]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["log", "enforce"])
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("port", [MEMORY_PORT, CONPORT_PORT])
+def test_should_hold_for_either_accepted_mode_on_either_server(checkout, seams, port, mode):
+    seams["health"][port]["auth_mode"] = mode
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is True
+    assert gate.failures == ()
+
+
+def test_should_still_require_identity_required_writes_in_log_mode(checkout, seams):
+    seams["health"][MEMORY_PORT].update(auth_mode="log", identity_required_writes=False)
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is False
+    assert "identity_required_writes" in gate.failures[0]
+
+
+def test_should_still_require_the_conport_principal_column_in_log_mode(checkout, seams):
+    seams["health"][CONPORT_PORT].update(auth_mode="log", auth_principal_column="missing")
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is False
+    assert "auth_principal_column" in gate.failures[0]
+
+
+def test_should_still_require_the_runner_token_in_log_mode(checkout, seams):
+    seams["health"][MEMORY_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "log"
+    seams["health"][CONPORT_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "log"
+    seams["token"] = TokenNotFoundError("no item")
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is False
+    assert len(gate.failures) == 1
+    assert "gate check 2" in gate.failures[0]
+
+
+def test_should_report_which_servers_are_in_log_mode(checkout, seams):
+    seams["health"][CONPORT_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "log"
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.log_mode_servers == ("conport",)
+    seams["health"][CONPORT_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "enforce"
+    assert mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout).log_mode_servers == ()
+
+
+def test_should_fail_when_a_required_health_field_is_absent(checkout, seams):
+    del seams["health"][MEMORY_PORT]["identity_required_writes"]
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is False
+    assert "identity_required_writes" in gate.failures[0]
+
+
+def test_should_not_require_a_principal_column_field_from_dope_memory(checkout, seams):
+    assert "auth_principal_column" not in seams["health"][MEMORY_PORT]
+
+    assert mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout).ok is True
+
+
+def test_should_fail_when_a_health_endpoint_is_unreachable(checkout, seams):
+    seams["health"][CONPORT_PORT] = mcp_commands._HealthProbeError("connection refused")
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is False
+    assert "conport" in gate.failures[0]
+    assert "connection refused" in gate.failures[0]
+
+
+def test_should_fail_closed_without_an_envrc(tmp_path, seams):
+    bare = tmp_path / "bare"
+    bare.mkdir()
+    (bare / ".git").mkdir()
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(bare)
+
+    assert gate.ok is False
+    assert any("dope-memory" in f for f in gate.failures)
+    assert any("conport" in f for f in gate.failures)
+    assert any("dopemux mcp init" in f for f in gate.failures)
+    assert seams["probed_ports"] == []
+
+
+def test_should_fail_when_the_envrc_lacks_one_port(checkout, seams):
+    (checkout / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp").write_text(f"export DOPE_MEMORY_PORT={MEMORY_PORT}\n")
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is False
+    assert len(gate.failures) == 1
+    assert "CONPORT_HTTP_PORT" in gate.failures[0]
+
+
+def test_should_fail_when_no_token_exists_for_the_runner(checkout, seams):
+    seams["token"] = TokenNotFoundError("no item")
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is False
+    assert len(gate.failures) == 1
+    assert "runner:codex" in gate.failures[0]
+    assert "TokenNotFoundError" in gate.failures[0]
+    assert "dopemux auth issue runner:codex" in gate.failures[0]
+
+
+def test_should_fail_and_name_any_other_auth_error(checkout, seams):
+    seams["token"] = KeychainError("keychain locked")
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert gate.ok is False
+    assert "KeychainError" in gate.failures[0]
+
+
+def test_should_report_every_failed_check_together(checkout, seams):
+    seams["health"][MEMORY_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "off"
+    seams["health"][CONPORT_PORT]["auth_principal_column"] = "missing"
+    seams["token"] = TokenNotFoundError("no item")
+
+    gate = mcp_commands.evaluate_codex_memory_gate(checkout)
+
+    assert len(gate.failures) == 3
+

+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# generate: write vs preview, --allow-sequenced
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+@pytest.fixture()
+def generate_env(tmp_path, monkeypatch, seams):
+    repo = tmp_path / "repo"
+    repo.mkdir()
+    (repo / ".git").mkdir()
+    (repo / ".envrc.dopemux-mcp").write_text(
+        f"export DOPE_MEMORY_PORT={MEMORY_PORT}\nexport CONPORT_HTTP_PORT={CONPORT_PORT}\n"
+    )
+    monkeypatch.setattr(mcp_commands, "get_repo_root", lambda fallback_cwd=False: str(repo))
+    out = tmp_path / "out"
+    return repo, out
+
+
+def _invoke(out: Path, *extra: str):
+    # The real catalog declares profiles, so the in-repo agent targets (and with
+    # them .codex/config.toml) are only produced in agent-matrix mode.
+    return CliRunner().invoke(
+        mcp_commands.mcp_generate_cmd,
+        ["--agent-matrix", "--apply", "--output-dir", str(out), *extra],
+    )
+
+
+def test_should_write_the_codex_config_when_the_gate_holds(generate_env):
+    repo, out = generate_env
+
+    result = _invoke(out)
+
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    text = (repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").read_text()
+    servers = tomllib.loads(text)["mcp_servers"]
+    assert set(servers) == {"conport", "dope-memory"}
+
+
+LOG_NOTICE_NEEDLES = (
+    "log mode",
+    "runner:codex",
+    "without a token",
+    "unverified",
+    "until enforce",
+)
+
+
+def test_should_print_the_log_mode_notice_once_when_a_server_is_in_log_mode(generate_env, seams):
+    repo, out = generate_env
+    seams["health"][MEMORY_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "log"
+
+    result = _invoke(out)
+
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    assert (repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").is_file()
+    notice_lines = [line for line in result.output.splitlines() if "log mode" in line]
+    assert len(notice_lines) == 1
+    for needle in LOG_NOTICE_NEEDLES:
+        assert needle in " ".join(result.output.split())
+    assert "dope-memory" in notice_lines[0]
+
+
+def test_should_print_one_notice_naming_both_servers_when_both_are_in_log_mode(generate_env, seams):
+    _repo, out = generate_env
+    seams["health"][MEMORY_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "log"
+    seams["health"][CONPORT_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "log"
+
+    result = _invoke(out)
+
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    assert sum("log mode" in line for line in result.output.splitlines()) == 1
+    assert "conport" in result.output and "dope-memory" in result.output
+
+
+def test_should_print_the_notice_for_a_mixed_log_and_enforce_pair(generate_env, seams):
+    repo, out = generate_env
+    seams["health"][CONPORT_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "log"
+
+    result = _invoke(out)
+
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    assert (repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").is_file()
+    assert sum("log mode" in line for line in result.output.splitlines()) == 1
+
+
+def test_should_print_no_log_notice_when_both_servers_enforce(generate_env):
+    repo, out = generate_env
+
+    result = _invoke(out)
+
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    assert (repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").is_file()
+    assert "log mode" not in result.output
+    assert "unverified" not in result.output
+
+
+def test_should_print_no_log_notice_when_the_gate_fails(generate_env, seams):
+    _repo, out = generate_env
+    seams["health"][MEMORY_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "log"
+    seams["token"] = TokenNotFoundError("none")
+
+    result = _invoke(out)
+
+    assert "Refused to write .codex/config.toml" in result.output
+    assert "Memory servers in log mode" not in result.output
+
+
+def test_should_keep_unreleased_servers_out_of_the_written_file(generate_env):
+    repo, out = generate_env
+
+    _invoke(out)
+
+    written = (repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").read_text()
+    for name in ("task-orchestrator", "serena", "dope-context", "gpt-researcher", "pal-stdio"):
+        assert f'"{name}"' not in written
+
+
+def test_should_still_preview_unreleased_servers_without_writing_them(generate_env):
+    repo, out = generate_env
+
+    _invoke(out)
+
+    preview = tomllib.loads((out / "codex" / "config.toml").read_text())["mcp_servers"]
+    assert {"conport", "dope-memory", "pal-stdio"} <= set(preview)
+    assert "task-orchestrator" not in preview
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "break_it, needle",
+    [
+        (lambda s: s["health"][MEMORY_PORT].update(auth_mode="off"), "dope-memory"),
+        (lambda s: s["health"][CONPORT_PORT].update(identity_required_writes=False), "conport"),
+        (lambda s: s["health"][CONPORT_PORT].update(auth_principal_column="missing"), "auth_principal_column"),
+        (lambda s: s.update(token=TokenNotFoundError("none")), "runner:codex"),
+    ],
+)
+def test_should_write_only_the_preview_and_name_the_failed_check(generate_env, seams, break_it, needle):
+    repo, out = generate_env
+    break_it(seams)
+
+    result = _invoke(out)
+
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    assert not (repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").exists()
+    assert (out / "codex" / "config.toml").is_file()
+    assert needle in result.output
+    assert "Refused to write .codex/config.toml" in result.output
+
+
+def test_should_not_touch_an_existing_codex_config_when_the_gate_fails(generate_env, seams):
+    repo, out = generate_env
+    (repo / ".codex").mkdir()
+    (repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").write_text('model = "x"\n')
+    seams["health"][MEMORY_PORT]["auth_mode"] = "off"
+
+    _invoke(out)
+
+    assert (repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").read_text() == 'model = "x"\n'
+
+
+def test_should_preserve_user_keys_when_the_gate_holds(generate_env):
+    repo, out = generate_env
+    (repo / ".codex").mkdir()
+    (repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").write_text('model = "x"\n\n[agents]\nmax_threads = 4\n')
+
+    result = _invoke(out)
+
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    parsed = tomllib.loads((repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").read_text())
+    assert parsed["model"] == "x"
+    assert parsed["agents"]["max_threads"] == 4
+    assert set(parsed["mcp_servers"]) == {"conport", "dope-memory"}
+
+
+def test_should_reject_allow_sequenced_and_name_the_new_gate(generate_env, seams):
+    repo, out = generate_env
+
+    result = _invoke(out, "--allow-sequenced")
+
+    assert result.exit_code != 0
+    assert "--allow-sequenced" in result.output
+    assert "auth_mode" in result.output
+    assert "'log' or 'enforce'" in " ".join(result.output.split())
+    assert "runner:codex" in result.output
+    assert not (repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").exists()
+    assert not out.exists()
+    assert seams["probed_ports"] == []
+    assert seams["token_principals"] == []
+
+
+def test_should_reject_allow_sequenced_even_when_the_gate_would_hold(generate_env):
+    repo, out = generate_env
+
+    result = _invoke(out, "--allow-sequenced")
+
+    assert result.exit_code != 0
+    assert not (repo / ".codex" / "config.toml").exists()
+
+
+def test_should_not_probe_or_read_the_keychain_on_a_dry_run(generate_env, seams):
+    _repo, out = generate_env
+
+    result = CliRunner().invoke(
+        mcp_commands.mcp_generate_cmd, ["--agent-matrix", "--output-dir", str(out)]
+    )
+
+    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
+    assert "Dry-run only" in result.output
+    assert seams["probed_ports"] == []
+    assert seams["token_principals"] == []
+
+
+def test_should_list_the_memory_gate_in_generate_help():
+    result = CliRunner().invoke(mcp_commands.mcp_generate_cmd, ["--help"])
+
+    assert result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "--allow-sequenced" in result.output
+    assert "removed" in result.output.lower()
+

+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# rendered entries
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+BRIDGE_ARGS = ["-m", "dopemux.mcp.http_stdio_bridge"]
+
+
+def test_should_render_the_codex_entries_through_the_stdio_route():
+    region = fleet_catalog.render_codex_managed_region(_real_catalog(), released_only=True)
+    body = "\n".join(
+        line for line in region.splitlines() if not line.startswith("#")
+    )
+    servers = tomllib.loads(body)["mcp_servers"]
+
+    assert servers == {
+        "conport": {
+            "command": "python",
+            "args": [*BRIDGE_ARGS, "--server", "conport", "--principal", "runner:codex"],
+        },
+        "dope-memory": {
+            "command": "python",
+            "args": [*BRIDGE_ARGS, "--server", "dope-memory", "--principal", "runner:codex"],
+        },
+    }
+
+
+def test_should_render_no_cwd_env_or_token_in_the_codex_entries():
+    region = fleet_catalog.render_codex_managed_region(_real_catalog(), released_only=True)
+
+    assert "cwd" not in region
+    assert "env" not in region
+    assert "token" not in region.lower().replace("--principal", "")
+
+
+def test_should_render_the_codex_bridge_entries_despite_the_per_worktree_scope():
+    catalog = _real_catalog()
+    assert catalog["servers"]["conport"]["scope"] == "per-worktree"
+    assert catalog["servers"]["dope-memory"]["scope"] == "per-worktree"
+
+    lines = fleet_catalog._codex_server_lines(catalog)
+
+    assert '[mcp_servers."conport"]' in lines
+    assert '[mcp_servers."dope-memory"]' in lines
+
+
+def test_should_keep_the_per_worktree_skip_for_other_servers():
+    catalog = _real_catalog()
+    assert catalog["servers"]["task-orchestrator"]["scope"] == "per-worktree"
+
+    for released_only in (False, True):
+        lines = fleet_catalog._codex_server_lines(catalog, released_only=released_only)
+        assert '[mcp_servers."task-orchestrator"]' not in lines
+
+
+def test_should_skip_a_per_worktree_server_that_is_not_a_memory_server():
+    catalog = {
+        "version": 1,
+        "defaults": {"per_worktree": []},
+        "servers": {
+            "other-local": {
+                "scope": "per-worktree",
+                "transport": "http",
+                "url_template": "http://localhost:1/mcp",
+                "agents": {"codex": "full"},
+            }
+        },
+    }
+
+    assert fleet_catalog._codex_server_lines(catalog) == []
+
+
+def test_should_not_render_a_bridge_entry_for_a_server_closed_to_codex():
+    catalog = _real_catalog()
+    catalog["servers"]["dope-memory"]["agents"]["codex"] = "none"
+
+    lines = fleet_catalog._codex_server_lines(catalog)
+
+    assert '[mcp_servers."dope-memory"]' not in lines
+    assert '[mcp_servers."conport"]' in lines
+
+
+def test_should_keep_the_default_claude_code_rendering_on_direct_http():
+    """AMENDMENT_02: Claude Code stays on direct HTTP/SSE in 2A; the tracked .mcp.json still matches."""
+    catalog = _real_catalog()
+    local = mcp_commands._build_local_mcp_json(["conport", "dope-memory"], catalog)["mcpServers"]
+
+    assert local["conport"]["type"] == "sse"
+    assert local["conport"]["url"] == "http://localhost:${CONPORT_MCP_PORT:-3005}/sse"
+    assert local["dope-memory"]["type"] == "http"
+    assert local["dope-memory"]["url"] == "http://localhost:${DOPE_MEMORY_PORT:-3020}/mcp"
+    for entry in local.values():
+        assert "command" not in entry
+        assert "runner:claude" not in json.dumps(entry)
+
+    committed = json.loads((REPO_ROOT / ".mcp.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

+"""W09 (Runner Memory Wave 2A, R2): ADR-227 narrow DCP-RED-MERGE-SEAM-0001 carve-out.
+
+Exactly 22 files named by W01, W02, W05 and (Amendment 1, W10) the two memory-image
+Dockerfiles are exempt from the path-level block, each as a file-exact negative
+lookahead anchored with ``\\Z``. Everything else in the three trees, and
+``services/task-orchestrator/``, stays blocked.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+_HOOKS_DIR = _ROOT / ".claude" / "hooks"
+if str(_HOOKS_DIR) not in sys.path:
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(_HOOKS_DIR))
+_SRC_DIR = _ROOT / "src"
+if str(_SRC_DIR) not in sys.path:
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(_SRC_DIR))
+
+from dcp_surface_guard import (  # noqa: E402
+    RED_LANE_ID,
+    _FALLBACK_COMPILED,
+    surface_guard_block,
+)
+from dopemux.dcp import red_lane_rules  # noqa: E402
+from dopemux.dcp.red_lane_rules import FORBIDDEN_PATHS  # noqa: E402
+
+WMA = "services/working-memory-assistant/"
+CONPORT = "docker/mcp-servers-source/conport/"
+BRIDGE = "services/dopecon-bridge/"
+
+EXEMPT_BY_TREE = {
+    WMA: (
+        "dope_memory_main.py",
+        "mcp_http.py",
+        "auth_identity.py",
+        "eventbus_consumer.py",
+        "chronicle/store.py",
+        "chronicle/schema.sql",
+        "chronicle/sqlite_migrations.py",
+        "chronicle/migrations/v1_3_0_add_write_principal.sql",
+        "tests/test_mcp_http_endpoint.py",
+        "Dockerfile.dope-memory",  # ADR-227 Amendment 1 (W10)
+    ),
+    CONPORT: (
+        "server.py",
+        "enhanced_server.py",
+        "instance_detector.py",
+        "conport_mcp_stdio.py",
+        "auth_identity.py",
+        "schema.sql",
+        "migrations/008_write_principal.sql",
+        "migrations/008_rollback.sql",
+        "migrations/conport_migration_gate.py",
+        "Dockerfile",  # ADR-227 Amendment 1 (W10)
+    ),
+    BRIDGE: (
+        "dopecon_bridge/clients.py",
+        "ddg_mcp_stdio.py",
+    ),
+}
+EXEMPT = tuple(tree + name for tree, names in EXEMPT_BY_TREE.items() for name in names)
+
+# Sampled files that exist (or plausibly exist) in each tree and are NOT exempt.
+STILL_BLOCKED = (
+    WMA + "conport_client.py",
+    WMA + "chronicle/capture_client.py",
+    WMA + "chronicle/migrations/v1_2_0_something.sql",
+    WMA + "tests/test_other.py",
+    WMA + "Dockerfile",  # the suffix-less name is NOT the exempt Dockerfile.dope-memory
+    WMA + "requirements.txt",
+    WMA + "src/mcp/server.py",
+    CONPORT + "direct_server.py",
+    CONPORT + "info_server.py",
+    CONPORT + "unified_queries.py",
+    CONPORT + "migrations/007_worktree_support_simple.sql",
+    CONPORT + "migrations/008_other.sql",
+    CONPORT + "tests/test_anything.py",
+    BRIDGE + "main.py",
+    BRIDGE + "dopecon_bridge/app.py",
+    BRIDGE + "dopecon_bridge/auth.py",
+    BRIDGE + "kg_endpoints.py",
+    BRIDGE + "Dockerfile",
+    BRIDGE + "tests/test_clients.py",
+    "services/task-orchestrator/app/main.py",
+    "services/task-orchestrator/dopemux_orchestrator/auth.py",
+    "src/conport/anything.py",  # ^src/conport/ is not part of this carve-out
+)
+
+
+def _edit(rel: str, tool: str = "Edit", root: Path = _ROOT):
+    return surface_guard_block(tool, {"file_path": str(root / rel)}, root)
+
+
+def _regex_blocked(rel: str) -> bool:
+    return any(p.search(rel) for p in FORBIDDEN_PATHS)
+
+
+def _near_misses(rel: str) -> list[str]:
+    """Variants of an exempt path that must NOT inherit its exemption."""
+    d, _, base = rel.rpartition("/")
+    stem, dot, ext = base.rpartition(".")
+    out = [
+        rel + ".bak",  # suffix
+        rel + "~",
+        f"{d}/x{base}",  # prefix
+        f"{d}/{base}.orig",
+        f"{d}/sub/{base}",  # nested same-named file
+        f"{d}/{stem}2.{ext}",
+        f"{d}/{base.upper()}",  # case variant of the file name
+        f"{d}/{stem.title()}{dot}{ext}",
+        f"{d}/../{rel.rsplit('/', 1)[0].rsplit('/', 1)[-1]}/{base}",  # spelled via ..
+        f"{d}/./sub/../{base}",  # ..-segment inside the subtree
+    ]
+    # `.title()` leaves names such as `Dockerfile` unchanged; a "variant" equal to the
+    # exempt path itself is not a near-miss.
+    return [v for v in out if v != rel]
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# The exempt set is exactly the 22 files (20 from W09 plus the two Amendment 1
+# Dockerfiles), as the rules file spells it
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_exempt_list_is_exactly_twenty_two_paths():
+    assert len(EXEMPT) == 22
+    assert len(set(EXEMPT)) == 22
+
+
+AMENDMENT_1_DOCKERFILES = (WMA + "Dockerfile.dope-memory", CONPORT + "Dockerfile")
+
+# Other Dockerfile-shaped names in the two trees: none of them inherits the exemption.
+DOCKERFILE_NEAR_MISSES = (
+    WMA + "Dockerfile",  # the suffix-less name
+    WMA + "Dockerfile.dope-memory.bak",  # suffix
+    WMA + "Dockerfile.dope-memory~",
+    WMA + "Dockerfile.dope-memory2",
+    WMA + "xDockerfile.dope-memory",  # prefix
+    WMA + "Dockerfile.dope",  # truncated
+    WMA + "Dockerfile.other",  # another Dockerfile name in the same tree
+    WMA + "Dockerfile.dev",
+    WMA + "dockerfile.dope-memory",  # case variants, including the all-lowercase spelling
+    WMA + "DOCKERFILE.DOPE-MEMORY",
+    WMA + "Dockerfile.Dope-Memory",
+    WMA + "dockerFile.dope-memory",
+    WMA + "sub/Dockerfile.dope-memory",  # nested same-named file
+    WMA + "tests/Dockerfile.dope-memory",
+    WMA + "chronicle/../Dockerfile.dope-memory",  # spelled via ..
+    WMA + "Dockerfile.dope-memory/x",  # a path below the "file"
+    CONPORT + "Dockerfile.bak",
+    CONPORT + "Dockerfile~",
+    CONPORT + "Dockerfile.dev",
+    CONPORT + "Dockerfile.dope-memory",  # the other tree's exempt name
+    CONPORT + "Dockerfile2",
+    CONPORT + "xDockerfile",
+    CONPORT + "dockerfile",  # the all-lowercase spelling is not exempt either
+    CONPORT + "xdockerfile",
+    CONPORT + "dockerfile.bak",
+    CONPORT + "DOCKERFILE",
+    CONPORT + "DockerFile",
+    CONPORT + "sub/Dockerfile",
+    CONPORT + "migrations/Dockerfile",
+    CONPORT + "migrations/../direct_server.py",
+    BRIDGE + "Dockerfile",  # a third tree is untouched by Amendment 1
+    BRIDGE + "Dockerfile.dope-memory",
+    "services/task-orchestrator/Dockerfile",
+)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("rel", AMENDMENT_1_DOCKERFILES)
+def test_amendment_1_dockerfiles_are_exempt_at_the_regex_layer(rel):
+    """FORBIDDEN_PATHS (read by PR Steward's gate and red_lane_scanner) exempts exactly these."""
+    assert not _regex_blocked(rel), rel
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("rel", AMENDMENT_1_DOCKERFILES)
+def test_amendment_1_dockerfiles_still_denied_by_the_claude_code_guard(rel):
+    """Known, accepted, fail-closed: the PreToolUse guard also tests the case-folded
+    (lower-case) reading of every path (ADR-226 residual F-001-A), and ``dockerfile`` /
+    ``dockerfile.dope-memory`` match the deny pattern, so Edit-tool edits to these two
+    capitalized names stay blocked. A later guard change, not this carve-out, resolves it."""
+    for tool in ("Edit", "Write", "NotebookEdit"):
+        result = _edit(rel, tool)
+        assert result is not None, (tool, rel)
+        assert RED_LANE_ID in result, (tool, rel)
+        assert rel.lower() in result, (tool, rel)  # the denial names the case-folded reading
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("rel", DOCKERFILE_NEAR_MISSES)
+def test_other_dockerfile_names_stay_blocked(rel):
+    for tool in ("Edit", "Write", "NotebookEdit"):
+        result = _edit(rel, tool)
+        assert result is not None, (tool, rel)
+        assert RED_LANE_ID in result, (tool, rel)
+    assert _regex_blocked(rel), rel
+
+
+def _lookahead_names(tree: str) -> set[str]:
+    """Parse the file-exact negative lookaheads out of the live pattern for ``tree``."""
+    matches = [p for p in FORBIDDEN_PATHS if p.pattern.startswith("^" + tree)]
+    assert len(matches) == 1, (tree, [m.pattern for m in matches])
+    pat = matches[0]
+    assert pat.flags & re.DOTALL, "`.*` patterns must compile with re.DOTALL"
+    assert pat.pattern.endswith(r".*\Z")
+    names = re.findall(r"\(\?!(.*?)\\Z\)", pat.pattern)
+    return {re.sub(r"\\(.)", r"\1", n) for n in names}
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("tree", list(EXEMPT_BY_TREE))
+def test_rules_file_lookaheads_match_the_pinned_list(tree):
+    assert _lookahead_names(tree) == set(EXEMPT_BY_TREE[tree])
+
+
+def test_rules_file_has_exactly_22_lookaheads():
+    total = sum(len(_lookahead_names(tree)) for tree in EXEMPT_BY_TREE)
+    assert total == 22
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("tree", list(EXEMPT_BY_TREE))
+def test_no_directory_exemption_in_lookaheads(tree):
+    """Every lookahead ends in ``\\Z``; none is a bare directory prefix like ``(?!tests/)``."""
+    pat = next(p for p in FORBIDDEN_PATHS if p.pattern.startswith("^" + tree))
+    for chunk in re.findall(r"\(\?!.*?\)", pat.pattern):
+        assert chunk.endswith(r"\Z)"), chunk
+
+
+def test_no_traversal_companion_pattern_was_added():
+    """File-exact exemptions need no `..` companion (unlike the ADR-226 eval/ directory)."""
+    companions = [p.pattern for p in FORBIDDEN_PATHS if r"\.\." in p.pattern]
+    assert all(c.startswith("^services/dope-context/") for c in companions), companions
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Every exempted path is allowed (all three edit tools)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("rel", [p for p in EXEMPT if p not in AMENDMENT_1_DOCKERFILES])
+def test_exempt_path_is_editable(rel):
+    # The two Amendment 1 Dockerfiles are exempt at the regex layer only; the guard layer is
+    # asserted separately (test_amendment_1_dockerfiles_still_denied_by_the_claude_code_guard).
+    for tool in ("Edit", "Write", "NotebookEdit"):
+        assert _edit(rel, tool) is None, (tool, rel)
+    assert not _regex_blocked(rel), rel
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("rel", EXEMPT)
+def test_every_exempt_path_is_exempt_at_the_regex_layer(rel):
+    assert not _regex_blocked(rel), rel
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("rel", EXEMPT)
+def test_exempt_path_is_not_a_read_block_either(rel):
+    assert surface_guard_block("Read", {"file_path": str(_ROOT / rel)}, _ROOT) is None
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Near-misses stay blocked
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("rel", EXEMPT)
+def test_near_misses_of_exempt_paths_stay_blocked(rel):
+    for variant in _near_misses(rel):
+        result = _edit(variant)
+        assert result is not None, variant
+        assert RED_LANE_ID in result, variant
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("rel", EXEMPT)
+def test_embedded_and_trailing_newline_variants_stay_blocked_at_regex_level(rel):
+    # Regex layer on its own (the guard's control-char short-circuit is a second layer).
+    for variant in (rel + "\n", rel + "\nextra.py"):
+        assert _regex_blocked(variant), repr(variant)
+    for variant in (rel + "\n", rel + "\nextra.py"):
+        result = surface_guard_block("Edit", {"file_path": str(_ROOT / rel) + variant[len(rel):]}, _ROOT)
+        assert result is not None, repr(variant)
+        assert RED_LANE_ID in result
+
+
+def test_embedded_newline_does_not_leak_into_a_blocked_sibling():
+    """`mcp_http.py\\nDockerfile` must not borrow mcp_http.py's exemption."""
+    rel = WMA + "mcp_http.py\nDockerfile"
+    assert _regex_blocked(rel)
+    assert surface_guard_block("Edit", {"file_path": str(_ROOT / WMA) + "mcp_http.py\nDockerfile"}, _ROOT)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("rel", EXEMPT)
+def test_trailing_slash_variant_stays_blocked_at_regex_level(rel):
+    """`mcp_http.py/` names a directory, not the exempt file: the rule must not match it.
+
+    Regex layer only. ``surface_guard_block`` builds a ``pathlib.Path``, which drops a
+    trailing slash, so at the guard layer the string collapses to the exempt file itself
+    (a write to ``file.py/`` fails with ENOTDIR, so this opens nothing).
+    """
+    assert _regex_blocked(rel + "/"), rel
+    assert _regex_blocked(rel + "//"), rel
+
+
+def test_dotdot_segments_stay_blocked():
+    for rel in (
+        WMA + "chronicle/../mcp_http.py",  # resolves to an exempt file but is spelled with ..
+        WMA + "chronicle/../tests/test_other.py",  # resolves to a blocked file
+        WMA + "../task-orchestrator/app/main.py",  # escapes into a sibling tree
+        WMA + "../working-memory-assistant/conport_client.py",
+        CONPORT + "migrations/../direct_server.py",
+        CONPORT + "migrations/../../conport/info_server.py",
+        BRIDGE + "dopecon_bridge/../main.py",

diff --git a/tests/unit/runner_memory_2a_r2_w10/test_image_copies_local_modules.py b/tests/unit/runner_memory_2a_r2_w10/test_image_copies_local_modules.py

+"""W10 (Runner Memory Wave 2A, R2): each memory image must COPY the local modules it imports.
+
+Both the dope-memory and the ConPort Dockerfiles copy application modules one file at a
+time. A module the entry points import but the Dockerfile does not copy fails at import
+time inside a rebuilt image (``ModuleNotFoundError``), which no source-tree test sees.
+This static test parses each Dockerfile's ``COPY`` lines and the entry modules' import
+statements; it builds, pulls and runs nothing.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import ast
+import shlex
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
+
+# image -> (Dockerfile, service source directory, entry modules), all repo-relative.
+IMAGES = {
+    "dope-memory": (
+        "services/working-memory-assistant/Dockerfile.dope-memory",
+        "services/working-memory-assistant",
+        ("dope_memory_main.py", "mcp_http.py", "eventbus_consumer.py"),
+    ),
+    "conport": (
+        "docker/mcp-servers-source/conport/Dockerfile",
+        "docker/mcp-servers-source/conport",
+        ("server.py", "enhanced_server.py", "conport_mcp_stdio.py"),
+    ),
+}
+
+_WORKDIR = "/app"
+
+
+def _copied_files(dockerfile: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
+    """Map repo-relative COPY source -> destination for single-file ``COPY`` lines."""
+    out: dict[str, str] = {}
+    for raw in dockerfile.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
+        line = raw.strip()
+        if not line.upper().startswith("COPY "):
+            continue
+        args = shlex.split(line)[1:]
+        if any(a.startswith("--from") for a in args):
+            continue  # copies from another stage or image, not from the build context
+        parts = [a for a in args if not a.startswith("--")]
+        if len(parts) < 2:
+            continue
+        *sources, dest = parts
+        for src in sources:
+            out[src] = dest
+    return out
+
+
+def _lands_importable(src: str, dest: str) -> bool:
+    """True when ``src`` ends up directly in the working directory under its own name."""
+    name = src.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
+    return dest.rstrip("/") in (".", _WORKDIR) or dest in (name, f"{_WORKDIR}/{name}", f"./{name}")
+
+
+def _imported_top_level_modules(path: Path) -> set[str]:
+    """Top-level module names named by every absolute import statement in ``path``."""
+    mods: set[str] = set()
+    for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))):
+        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
+            mods.update(alias.name.split(".")[0] for alias in node.names)
+        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.level == 0 and node.module:
+            mods.add(node.module.split(".")[0])
+    return mods
+
+
+def _local_modules_needed(service_dir: str, entries: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
+    """Local ``<module>.py`` files (directly in the service dir) -> entry modules importing them."""
+    needed: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
+    for entry in entries:
+        for mod in _imported_top_level_modules(_ROOT / service_dir / entry):
+            if (_ROOT / service_dir / f"{mod}.py").is_file():
+                needed.setdefault(f"{mod}.py", set()).add(entry)
+    return needed
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("image", IMAGES)
+def test_entry_modules_and_dockerfile_exist(image):
+    dockerfile, service_dir, entries = IMAGES[image]
+    assert (_ROOT / dockerfile).is_file(), dockerfile
+    for entry in entries:
+        assert (_ROOT / service_dir / entry).is_file(), entry
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("image", IMAGES)
+def test_entry_modules_import_at_least_one_local_module(image):
+    """Guard against a vacuous pass: the import scan must actually find local modules."""
+    _, service_dir, entries = IMAGES[image]
+    needed = _local_modules_needed(service_dir, entries)
+    assert "auth_identity.py" in needed, needed
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("image", IMAGES)
+def test_image_copies_every_local_module_its_entry_points_import(image):
+    dockerfile, service_dir, entries = IMAGES[image]
+    copied = {
+        src.removeprefix(service_dir + "/")
+        for src, dest in _copied_files(_ROOT / dockerfile).items()
+        if src.startswith(service_dir + "/") and _lands_importable(src, dest)
+    }
+    needed = _local_modules_needed(service_dir, entries)
+    missing = {mod: sorted(who) for mod, who in sorted(needed.items()) if mod not in copied}
+    assert not missing, f"{dockerfile} does not COPY: {missing}"
+
+
+def test_copy_parser_reads_a_plain_copy_line(tmp_path):
+    df = tmp_path / "Dockerfile"
+    df.write_text(
+        "FROM x\n"
+        "COPY svc/a.py .\n"
+        "COPY svc/b.py /app/\n"
+        "COPY svc/c.py /elsewhere/c.py\n"
+        "COPY --from=other /uv /bin/\n"
+    )
+    copied = _copied_files(df)
+    assert _lands_importable("svc/a.py", copied["svc/a.py"])
+    assert _lands_importable("svc/b.py", copied["svc/b.py"])
+    assert not _lands_importable("svc/c.py", copied["svc/c.py"])
+    assert "/uv" not in copied
diff --git a/tests/unit/runner_memory_2a_r2_w12/__init__.py b/tests/unit/runner_memory_2a_r2_w12/__init__.py

diff --git a/tests/unit/runner_memory_2a_r2_w12/test_conport_defaults.py b/tests/unit/runner_memory_2a_r2_w12/test_conport_defaults.py
